## Verbatim original designated-owner request (unchanged source follows)

# Required designated-owner contracts, not ordinary-author rules

This proposal defines the ordinary successful dataflow, whole-frame observation and arithmetic. It does not decide the following source/admission/identity/error/security/resource rules. The designated Opus or explicitly authorized Gemini/Muse owner must supply the applicable contracts and judgments. No hostile tests, security conclusions, error codes, commitment scheme, admission waivers or resource approval are authored here. Existing clauses remain verbatim in the pinned basis, including their original scenarios.

| Dependency | Concrete interface requiring owner input | Undecided question and required handoff |
| --- | --- | --- |
| OD1 — Source/domain admission | `Sequence::plan`, `FrameBindings`, projection before `stage::start` | What exact valid binding/domain/alias/schema judgments apply? Which local duty registry identities may be projected, including discharge-only incoming duties and await/R7 boundaries? Current predicates are not silently weakened. Supply exact admission ordering and supported domain; ordinary SC2 projection is not an admission proof. |
| OD2 — Identity and predecessor carrier | Catalog's existing Program/Plan hashes, invocation ordinals, any future persisted Sequence | Existing stage identities are preserved verbatim. Specify any new source/plan/manifest/whole-prefix identity, canonical preimage, verification, origin, reopen and persistent parent rules. Local labels/array indices are only observations. No new sequence hash, proof lineage or authenticity follows from this design. |
| OD3 — Errors and publication | `SequenceError`, failures during planning/stage calls/projection/lift/checked totals/export | Specify variants, exact public wire, diagnostic precedence, mutation/publication points and retained earlier observations/work. Existing original StageRejection envelopes/counters must remain available under their current contract; how a new envelope carries them is not defined by this ordinary author. No fabricated zero-attempt result or silent truncation is proposed. |
| OD4 — Global resource envelope | `LogicalAllocation`, cumulative attempted/committed/remaining observations, output tables | Decide global preflight/enforcement, false/rejected/late-call charges, overflow, invocation/plan/output limits, adapter overhead and closure-reserve use. Existing per-action caps are not raised or borrowed. The proposed positive-trace equations are descriptive arithmetic, not adequacy or lifecycle enforcement evidence. Native DUST/service/proof/wall-time/resource controls retain their owners and historical counters. |
| OD5 — Participant authority, consent and effects | Whole head/duty creation, source inputs, events, any authenticated successor | Supply required participant/source/intent authority and consent semantics. A shared key, transfer, ordinary local outcome or reviewer decision supplies no such authority. The product remains permissionless; project membership is not a source-language validity argument. |
| OD6 — Head/ledger/phase semantics | Future native `Step`, failed Q after completed P, reserved/recovery paths | Specify actual phase-dependent effects, consumption, rejection/recovery and native/ledger correspondence. Ordinary local prefix retention does not decide transaction phase atomicity, rollback, ledger uniqueness or proof induction. |
| OD7 — Private/persistent handoff and asynchronous evidence | Any reopened continuation, external callback or another process/party | Specify permitted witness/artifact ownership, disclosure, availability, origin/correlation/finality/consumption, late results and recovery. The proposed one-process raw StageEvent flow retains current local labels and is not an authenticated asynchronous outcome or isolated private handoff. |
| OD8 — Other operators | Shared-state schedule, split/join, atomic domain and external asynchronous branches | Supply each operator's actual rule and designated-owner judgments. This sequence has one active action and one local predecessor; it cannot accept a many-parent Join or concurrent head by merely listing it as the next invocation. Existing D1–D5/RV1–RV3 and unadopted SI1–SI4 are not modified. |

The normal-path records include the complete data these future rules must bind; they do not replace those rules with optional report fields. Production integration depends on applicable owner contracts and ordinary consequential-design votes. This is a concrete dependency handoff, not a new maintainer permission requirement on public developers. No provider dispatch or owner review occurred in this task.

# Completed predecessor → successor: ordinary production contract proposal

Status: UNADOPTED design, source-only. Basis is main `79de664a3d16b5e0456043a530c8f33d723561a2`, never ambient HEAD. All seventy-one complete basis files and original35 MPLR notes are pinned in `SOURCE-PINS.json`. `ORIGINAL-MPLR-001-035.json` contains every original required-behavior paragraph verbatim; the complete notes and canonical EARS remain under `basis/`. No old blocker, oracle, proposal or result is replaced.

**Recommendation:** implement a production-owned `stage::sequence::Sequence` that owns the complete current financial head and every original invocation observation. It starts a successor from its actual completed predecessor through an explicit, typed frame projection; it then lifts the real stage result into the preserved whole frame. Add a public `mori compose` consumer and an adapter in the existing `mori test`; both use this same object. Arbitrary new finite chains of currently supported Core/2 programs use the same implementation. No example registry, test-owned evaluator or application-specific lowering is involved.

This proposes the ordinary local sequential operator. It does not claim full native `Step`, shared-state interleaving, atomic `Join`, asynchronous settlement, persistent authenticated handoff, or full MPLR completion. `OWNER-DEPENDENCIES.md` names the source/admission/identity/error/resource questions reserved to the designated owner. The complete normal-path contract is specified here; those dependent rules must be supplied before integration. Ordinary design adoption requires the existing substantive votes; none has been obtained for this proposal.

## Existing boundary and two concrete approaches

Current `stage::start` accepts a freshly supplied `Scenario`; `StageOutcome::Complete` supplies one segment and work/residual values. No completed-program successor operation exists. `Pair::start` starts both independent members before publication and requires disjoint whole declared domains. `Pair::resume` resumes its original member from that member's continuation. These APIs remain unchanged.

| Approach | Concrete interface connected to existing code | Advantages | Costs and limitation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A — Stateless completed-prefix transition | Add `start_successor(prefix: CompletePrefix, next: StagedProgram, action, inputs, bindings)` beside `stage::start`. `CompletePrefix` must contain every earlier outcome, whole head, effects and accounting; a single existing `Complete` is insufficient. The new public CLI must carry that full prefix between calls. | A small direct transition interface; convenient when an independently specified persistent predecessor already exists. | Introduces a new externally supplied full-prefix carrier and its admission/reopen/identity contract immediately. Every caller must retain the right predecessor rather than merely a final Snapshot. It still needs the same projection, lifting, accounting and reporting implementation. Reusing a bare `StageOutcome::Complete` silently loses earlier await segments. |
| B — Owned sequence over existing real stage calls, RECOMMENDED | Add `Sequence::plan`, `begin`, `resume`, `next`, `report` in `mori-core::stage::sequence`. The object retains the actual outputs from every call. `next` takes no Snapshot or predecessor supplied by the caller; it starts the next planned program from the owned completed head. | Directly fixes the observed boundary, retains original reports automatically, supports awaiting programs and different program schemas, and gives both public consumers one production path. Existing stage evaluation and local continuations remain authoritative for their own invocations. | Adds an explicit frame adapter and an owned prefix report. Persistent cross-process reopening is a separate owner-defined contract. Output/memory bounds and failure publication need the existing owner decisions; local ownership is not authenticated lineage or ledger uniqueness. |

Choose B. Do not implement A as a second path in this slice. A later persistent handoff may reopen B's full prefix only under its separately specified owner contract; an arbitrary JSON final head is not treated as that operation.

## Generality and execution domain

The input is a finite ordered list of invocation specifications, each selecting one action in any actually loaded Core/2 program, with explicit fresh action inputs, state/duty bindings and the existing optional per-action logical cap. A program may occur more than once. No equality of complete source schemas, distinct exact assets, hard-coded two-program limit or application whitelist is required by this ordinary sequential contract. Real stage admission still applies to each projected view. A local program unsupported by current stage admission does not become supported by this proposal.

All programs may share exact assets, accounts, state keys and duty keys along this serial order. There is at most one active pending action. Another invocation begins only after the previous one completes. There is no interleaving, simultaneous execution or rebasing of a pending continuation. Sequential source order does not create atomic multi-program settlement: the completed P prefix remains a completed prefix when Q later runs. Completion means the selected local program list completed, even when the final residual duty set is positive.

The sequence owns a full `Snapshot` as its global head. Its global state and liability names are explicit keys; every selected program has a local-to-global binding map. The global head may contain unrelated state, other exact assets, unrelated accounts and unrelated liabilities. They stay in the complete report and survive every projection/lift. A name map is not an authorization, commitment, authenticated completeness domain or namespace certificate; those judgments belong to the designated owner.

## Frame projection and lifting

For invocation i let G be the exact current whole head and B_i its declared bindings.

1. The local state view contains each declared program state under its original source name, with its value read from the mapped global key in G. The explicit opening supplies every required value once. A successor never reuses its source initializer to reset a mapped state. Separate local names may refer to one shared global state in successive programs; within one admitted view the mapping shape and type conditions must be satisfied under the eventual owner admission contract.
2. The local balance view contains **all** G balance rows whose exact AssetId is declared by this program, retaining every account, including observers. Asset identity and account strings are not renamed. Other exact-asset rows stay outside. Equal ticker/reference/scale is insufficient to merge assets.
3. The local duty view contains the existing global duty rows selected by the explicit duty binding map, under their original local names. The map also names source duties that are currently absent, so an absent row stays absent until an actual source statement creates it. Other duties stay in the global outside frame, even if they use a selected asset. No present-zero row is changed to absent. Current `stage::decode::canonical_boundary` and source-registry predicates remain in force; this proposal does not create a bypass for incoming duties that the actual stage API would not admit.
4. Inputs come only from the invocation specification. Inputs/lets/events/captures are not inserted into global persistent state. The local `Scenario` passed to the actual `stage::start` is constructed **inside this production adapter** from this projection and the specified inputs, not by the test or CLI caller from a supplied successor scenario.
5. Let F be the complete outside Snapshot omitted by the projection. Lift every original local Step.snapshot and the final boundary by applying the inverse state/duty name map and joining it to F. Balances retain their exact identities. No Step, effect, field, zero row, unselected duty or outside row is sampled or truncated. The current stage rules define the original local effect; this adapter records its global name image separately.
6. When Waiting, retain the exact original continuation. No other program executes before it completes, so the same frame stays outside that continuation on resume. `resume` calls the existing real stage API with that continuation and event. It does not synthesize a continuation from the whole head.
7. `next` starts Q only from the head retained after P's actual complete result. The caller cannot provide Q's balances, liabilities, persistent state, prior effects or prior work. All those components are owned by Sequence. Q receives only its declared fresh inputs and existing selected cap in addition to the internal projection.

Each call records its exact projection, complete outside frame, full original `stage::outcome_report` object, full lifted Step snapshots and actual whole boundary. This makes both the local and global frames observable. A projection is never advertised as a complete global view or evidence of global absence. The nested original outcome and its labels are not rewritten.

## Original history, effects and accounting

The sequence stores every original real invocation outcome once in call order. Each outcome retains its complete segment/Execution/Step/statement/value/snapshot/effect/frame/capture/continuation/residual/work fields. Latest outcomes are references into this table; history is the entire table prefix. Completing an action does not replace its earlier await segments with its final segment.

The global effect table is an ordered list of references to original call/Step/effect positions, each with the exact global name image. Effects follow real invocation order, then original source order. Repeated, zero and self effects stay present. There is no optimization, cancellation, netting, fabricated inverse or removal of previously emitted events. `WIRE-CONTRACT.md` defines exact prefix attachments and table ordering.

For each exact `(asset, account)` at every prefix, report opening/closing balance, transfer debits/credits, fees paid/received, gross debits/credits and signed-direction net change. Gross debit is the sum of Transfer amounts and Fee amounts from that account in the original observed prefix. A later credit or refund does not subtract an earlier debit. A fee received is distinct from a transfer received, and fees paid count in gross debits. Sum across accounts only in the same exact asset; never add identically named denominations from different issuers. Existing zero/self effects remain source effects even when closing minus opening is zero.

For every global liability key/identity, report opening and closing **presence** and amount, all source additions, all explicit discharges, and the original event references. Creation/increase is distinguished from discharge by the attached original Statement, not merely the sign of an Effect::Liability delta. The arithmetic is opening amount + additions − discharges = closing amount, with presence tracked separately. Explicit discharge to zero preserves the original present-zero row. Positive residuals are the complete set of positive global closing liabilities, including outside duties. No external debt, custody or native fee is inferred from these local scalar operations; current Core/2 has no mint/burn primitive, and this design does not silently add one.

## Work and reserve observations

The original local `/2` schedule remains unchanged: statement dispatch/node visits, await entry and guard work, per-action committed prefixes, cap values and every original attempted counter. A successor's local committed counter legitimately begins at zero; its sequence cumulative counters do not reset. For each call record its actual attempted amount and the latest committed amount of the selected action instance. Sum **attempted amounts per call**; sum **latest committed values per action instance**, not every repeated cumulative field. A successful false guard adds its actual attempted work and leaves successful committed work/head/frame/effects unchanged while retaining that original observation.

The proposed allocation fields make an ordinary envelope and a separate closure-reserve amount visible. On a successful ordinary trace with sufficient envelope, available ordinary work = declared ordinary allocation − cumulative attempted work, and the closure-reserve amount remains unchanged. No ordinary transition silently spends that reserve. These are accounting equations for the proposed success trace, not a new cap admission/refusal, funding, metering of adapter overhead, native fee or closure-use policy. The exact global preflight, rejected-call charges, overflow/error handling, and reserve authority are explicitly owner-dependent in `OWNER-DEPENDENCIES.md`. No claim of finite resource adequacy or native lifecycle conservation is made by these observations alone.

## Public path and implementation boundary

Add `crates/mori-core/src/stage/sequence.rs` as the production operation. Reuse actual `stage::load`, `start`, `resume`, `outcome_report` and existing typed IR. Use a small typed frame adapter and prefix-observation builder in that module; do not modify or copy the evaluator. No application-specific branch, new scheduler, external service or test-only success path is introduced.

Add `crates/mori/src/commands/compose.rs`, a `Compose` CLI variant and its ordinary dispatcher arm. `mori compose plan.json --json` loads source/opening/input/event files through existing bounded public readers, constructs the production sequence, follows its explicit start/resume/next operations, and prints the exact run report. It accepts no expected-output files. Keep native/proof/target commands out of this path. The new local feature/profile exposure and resource/error details remain proposed decisions, not current availability.

Add `commands/test/sequence_test.rs` and a distinct `moriarty-tests/sequential-proposed/1` profile. It preloads independent complete expectations, loads the **same** composition plan, and calls the same production operation driver. Expectations cannot supply successor state or history. The adapter compares every complete observation and the full public report/bytes; it never performs projection, financial accumulation or execution itself. Existing `/1`, `/2`, disjoint formats and Pair semantics remain unchanged.

`API-AND-CALLER-TRACE.md` supplies exact normal-path signatures and manifest fields; `WIRE-CONTRACT.md` specifies every new success-report field. `OPENSPEC-DELTA.md` supplies proposed EARS requirements. `EXAMPLES-AND-TESTS.md` supplies full author-worked financial prefixes and the independent-oracle requirement, not fabricated independent goldens.

## Decisions recommended for ordinary votes

SC1: choose owned Sequence over a stateless externally supplied CompletePrefix. SC2: use an explicitly bound whole global Snapshot with lossless local projection/lift, supporting differing schemas and outside frames. SC3: retain original outcome tables and complete global Step/effect prefixes with explicit source attachments. SC4: expose one production compose path reused by the existing test consumer; no expected inputs in production. SC5: preserve full per-asset/per-duty accounting and original local/cumulative-work distinction, with owner-dependent global enforcement/reserve/failure details unresolved. SC6: retain all other operators and native/private/persistent/target/identity contracts as separate requirements; no sequential result substitutes for them.

These recommendations supply one concrete design for adoption. They are not adopted semantics, implementation, execution evidence, independent expected values, security judgments or parent acceptance.

# Proposed production API and public caller trace

UNADOPTED interface specification, not compiled Rust or existing callable API. All types below are normal-path contracts. `SequenceError` and all new admission/refusal/reopen rules are reserved to the designated owner; no substitute variants or error bytes are authored here.

## Library

Recommended location: `mori_core::stage::sequence`, exposed through a proposed non-default `core2-sequential-proposed` feature and forwarded by `mori`. Existing feature names and defaults are untouched. Public developer use has no project-registration or reviewer-record argument.

```rust
pub struct ProgramEntry {
    pub key: String,                 // manifest-local routing label, not identity evidence
    pub program: stage::StagedProgram,
}
pub struct FrameBindings {
    pub states: BTreeMap<String, String>, // local source state -> global Snapshot.state key
    pub duties: BTreeMap<String, String>, // local source duty name -> global liability name
}
pub struct Invocation {
    pub program: String,            // ProgramEntry.key
    pub action: String,
    pub inputs: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    pub bindings: FrameBindings,
    pub logical_cap: Option<UInt>,  // mandatory JSON key; null retains existing no-local-cap meaning
}
pub struct LogicalAllocation {
    pub ordinary: UInt,
    pub closure_reserve: UInt,
}
pub struct Sequence { /* owns catalog, plan, complete head, outside frame,
                        original pending continuation and every observed prefix */ }

impl Sequence {
    pub fn plan(catalog: Vec<ProgramEntry>, plan: Vec<Invocation>,
                opening: Snapshot, allocation: LogicalAllocation)
        -> Result<Self, SequenceError>;
    pub fn begin(&mut self) -> Result<ObservationIndex, SequenceError>;
    pub fn resume(&mut self, event: stage::StageEvent)
        -> Result<ObservationIndex, SequenceError>;
    pub fn next(&mut self) -> Result<ObservationIndex, SequenceError>;
    pub fn report(&self) -> SequenceRunReport;
}
```

`ObservationIndex` is a local unsigned array index, not a parent/head identity or replay token. `Sequence` has no public constructor from an arbitrary Complete/Execution/Snapshot after planning, and no proposed Deserialize in this slice. This is an in-process ordinary API choice, not a claim of uniqueness, authenticated origin or secret isolation. A separately specified persistent API must preserve the same data and supply its owner-defined validation.

`plan` stores an explicitly provided complete opening head and yields observation0 with no executed call, no manufactured frames, empty effect/call prefixes and zero local consumed work. The sequence plan is a nonempty finite list; numerical invocation/plan/output bounds and their errors must be fixed through the owner-dependent resource contract before implementation. This proposal neither raises existing reader/export bounds nor invents a new supported maximum.

`begin` executes invocation0 through actual `stage::start`. `resume` executes only the active action through actual `stage::resume` and its original continuation. `next` selects the next planned invocation after the current one is actually complete, projecting the owned whole head and calling real `stage::start`. There is no `scenario` or `predecessor` parameter on `next`, no caller-supplied prior accounting, and no auto-start of later programs. The ordinary successful transition ordering and state shape are SC1–SC3 recommendations; concrete misuse/admission/refusal handling belongs to the designated owner.

After a waiting call, progress is `waiting`; after completion with planned successors, it is `between-programs`; after final local completion it is `complete`. A positive residual set does not change this local control status into an incomplete scalar action or into settlement. The `programs_completed` count is separate from paid/delivered/settled external obligations, for which this profile supplies no evidence.

## Production manifest and command

Proposed command: `mori compose PLAN.json --json`. It is additive to the existing CLI and deliberately names the language operation. `--json` prints one full normal-path run report with one final LF. A successfully executed finite list of requested operations returns exit0 whether the resulting local progress is waiting, between programs or complete; actual report status exposes that distinction. New command errors and their publication/exit contract require the designated owner. No compiler/native/prover operation is selected.

The exact root input fields are `profile`, `programs`, `opening`, `invocations`, `allocation`, `operations`; all are mandatory. There are no expected-output fields. Existing bounded public file/strict JSON readers are reused without enlarged caps. All paths are relative to the manifest directory. Input paths are read/frozen before driving the requested calls; expected files can never affect production state.

| Object | Exact fields |
| --- | --- |
| Root | `profile:"moriarty-composition/sequential-proposed/1"`, `programs:ProgramPath[]`, `opening:path`, `invocations:InvocationPath[]`, `allocation:LogicalAllocation`, `operations:Operation[]` |
| ProgramPath | `key:string`, `source:path` |
| InvocationPath | `program:string`, `action:string`, `inputs:path`, `state_bindings:{local:global}`, `duty_bindings:{local:global}`, `logical_cap:UInt|null` |
| LogicalAllocation | `ordinary:UInt`, `closure_reserve:UInt` |
| Operation begin | `kind:"begin"` |
| Operation next | `kind:"next"` |
| Operation resume | `kind:"resume"`, `event:path` |

Opening is the complete existing `Snapshot` DTO, with global state/duty keys. Each inputs file is exactly a map of existing typed Values for that invocation's fresh action parameters. Each event file is the current complete `StageEvent` DTO. Programs load through the actual `stage::load`, yielding the original Program/filled StagePlan and existing owner-defined program/plan digests. No new sequence digest, authenticated head ID or source-admission identity rule is specified here.

The bindings name every declared local state and every source duty name relevant to its declared local view; they do not map assets/accounts, inject values, supply missing history or rename the original nested reports. Ordinary projection equations are in DESIGN.md. Exact admissibility of aliases, registry identities and input/state schemas, including the existing R7 distinctions, is explicitly an owner handoff. The positive examples use straightforward one-to-one maps and currently supported scalar statements.

## One real call trace

1. CLI parser selects the new `Compose` arm in `commands::run`. `compose::load_plan` reads the public manifest/opening/inputs/events using `core_io`, then calls actual `stage::load` for each source. It constructs `ProgramEntry`, `Invocation` and the complete opening once. A test adapter may call this same loader; neither adapter performs financial execution.
2. Production `Sequence::plan` builds the owned session and opening observation. It stores real admitted Programs/Plans and the declared bindings. Design/admission preparation does not pretend any program ran.
3. The driver calls `begin`. Sequence projects G0 into P's local Scenario, retaining F0, and invokes existing `stage::start(P, actionP, actual_projection, capP)`. The existing runtime supplies original Steps/effects/frames/work. Sequence stores that full outcome and the lifted whole snapshots. If P waits, the driver must supply declared resume operations; Q does not start.
4. After P completes, Sequence retains its actual whole head GP and every earlier P segment. A report exposes this boundary before Q starts. The driver calls `next` with **no Q state parameter**. Sequence projects GP under Q's own bindings and calls existing `stage::start(Q, actionQ, inputsQ + actual_projection, capQ)`. A source initializer in Q cannot overwrite GP.
5. Q's success updates the selected projection, preserves every outside row, appends actual original outcomes/effects, and carries gross debit/fees/residual/work prefixes forward. Any Q awaits use Q's exact returned continuation. The production report retains both original programs' records.
6. `compose::run` renders the typed full `SequenceRunReport`. No expected output exists on this path. The whole semantic JSON and exact public bytes require independently frozen expectations before a test.

No host or test creates a Q Snapshot, concatenates two arbitrary executions, synthesizes a continuation, resets global counters or substitutes a final-state summary. Projection/accumulation are actual production behavior under review.

## Existing test consumer integration

Proposed `moriarty-tests/sequential-proposed/1` root: exactly `{profile,cases}`. Each case is exactly `{name,plan,expected_observations,expected_report}`. `plan` is a path to the same production manifest. `expected_observations` is an ordered list of paths, one complete expanded PrefixObservation for opening and each requested operation. `expected_report` names the whole independently expected run report. The suite preloads all expected values before production calls. It executes the same plan driver and compares complete values; it does not derive runtime state from expectations or perform its own projection/accounting.

The adapter uses the existing aggregate reporting behavior: every actual observation and each comparison flag remains visible; an ordinary expectation mismatch yields overall failed/exit1 while later otherwise valid requested operations and cases are still observed. It keeps the production run tables intact and names complete prefixes, never truncations. The separate public binary regression runs actual `mori compose`, compares full report values/bytes, and separately exercises `mori test` for complete comparison/aggregate behavior. Proposed names and required goldens are in EXAMPLES-AND-TESTS.md; no command/test is callable at the pinned source yet.

## Exact production file boundary for later integration

New: `crates/mori-core/src/stage/sequence.rs`, `crates/mori/src/commands/compose.rs`, `crates/mori/src/commands/test/sequence_test.rs`. Shared edits: `stage/mod.rs`, `crates/mori-core/Cargo.toml`, `crates/mori/Cargo.toml`, `crates/mori/src/cli.rs`, `commands/mod.rs`, `commands/test.rs`. Public tests/fixtures are additive. Existing parser, scalar evaluator, stage DTOs/digests/admission, Pair source and old goldens stay intact. If owner decisions require changes to those existing rules, their complete changed source and affected correspondence become a separately reviewed extension, not an invisible prerequisite repair.

# Proposed complete ordinary success-report DTO

UNADOPTED authored codec/observation contract. It is derived from inspected current stage/IR DTOs plus the proposed ordinary sequence design; it is not an independent wire oracle. All original embedded objects retain their complete existing fields. Owner-dependent error/identity/admission/resource semantics are outside this success DTO and remain explicit dependencies. These exact field sets are sufficient for a separate custodian to derive normal-path expectations after adoption; no implementation-generated expected values are permitted.

Notation: Index and Count are JSON unsigned integer array positions/counts, not hashes. UInt is the current canonical unsigned decimal-string/u128 type. Snapshot, Effect, PendingFrame, Value, Program, StagePlan and StageEvent are the existing complete DTOs at the pinned main. OutcomeReport is the entire JSON value produced by current `stage::outcome_report(Ok(outcome))`, without field projection or label changes. Optional fields specified below are mandatory keys with null when absent.

## Root and catalog

`SequenceRunReport` has exactly:

| Key | Type and meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `format` | `"moriarty-local-sequential-proposed/1"` |
| `qualification` | Existing `"local-reference-stage"` qualification retained for this reference observation |
| `composition_qualification` | `"proposed-local-sequential-composition"`; no native/operator acceptance claimed |
| `authentication`, `consumption` | Existing local labels `"none"`, `"untracked"`; no new assurance level |
| `status` | Final observation.progress.status: `ready`, `waiting`, `between-programs` or `complete` |
| `catalog` | CatalogEntry[] in program manifest order |
| `plan` | InvocationSpec[] in planned execution order |
| `opening` | The complete initial global Snapshot |
| `calls` | CallObservation[] in actual invocation order; each original real call occurs once |
| `effects` | EffectLink[] in actual call, Step and original effect order |
| `observations` | PrefixObservation[]: opening followed by one per successful requested operation |
| `final_observation` | Index of the final element in observations; never a separate summary replacing it |

CatalogEntry has exactly `{key,program,stage_plan,program_hash,plan_hash}`. `program` and `stage_plan` are complete original objects from actual `stage::load`, including unused actions/expressions and filled costs; hashes are the original existing fields copied as data, with no new commitment/identity assertion. There is no sequence hash in this proposal.

InvocationSpec has exactly `{program,action,inputs,state_bindings,duty_bindings,logical_cap}`. It contains actual typed input values rather than input file paths. program is the catalog routing key. Bindings are the declared local-to-global maps. logical_cap is the original Option<UInt>, including mandatory null when absent. No successor scenario or caller financial prefix is present.

## Each real call

CallObservation has exactly:

| Key | Type |
| --- | --- |
| `index` | Its position in root.calls |
| `invocation` | Index of the action instance in root.plan |
| `kind` | `start` or `resume`; both P start and Q start use start |
| `event` | Complete StageEvent for resume; null for start |
| `whole_pre`, `whole_post` | Complete global Snapshots immediately before/after this call |
| `projected_pre`, `projected_post` | Exact local Snapshots passed/returned by the real stage operation |
| `outside` | Complete unselected global Snapshot retained for this call; the same frame is used for lifting each original Step and post |
| `outcome` | Entire original OutcomeReport, with all original fields/frames/captures/Steps/effects/work/continuation/residuals and labels |
| `framed_steps` | FramedStep[] in the original segment.execution.steps order; [] for a successful nonadvancing Waiting observation |

FramedStep has exactly `{step_offset,source_index,whole_snapshot,effect_prefix_len}`. step_offset is the position in the original Execution.steps array; source_index is the untouched original Step.index. whole_snapshot is the complete lifted global image of that exact Step.snapshot. effect_prefix_len is the length of root.effects after that Step, including all prior calls. It is a length, not a last index or limit. Empty Step deltas leave the length unchanged. The original full Step is still in outcome; this record does not synthesize its statement, ExprId, value or local snapshot.

For original Waiting(advanced:false), projected_pre and projected_post equal the retained continuation.boundary; whole_pre equals whole_post, outside is unchanged, framed_steps is empty and no effect is appended. The original outcome has segment:null and its original attempted/committed continuation data. This is an ordinary successful observation, not a new error branch.

EffectLink has exactly `{index,call_index,step_offset,step_effect_offset,execution_effect_offset,whole_effect}`. Each index names a real original record. The original per-Step effect at step_effect_offset must equal the original Execution.effects element at execution_effect_offset; whole_effect is its explicit global name image. State.name and Liability.name map through the invocation bindings. AssetId/from/to/amount and Emit name/value retain their original values. The complete original local effects stay embedded in outcome. Effects are never sorted, deduplicated, netted or canceled.

## Every published prefix

PrefixObservation has exactly `{index,cause,call_prefix_len,effect_prefix_len,head,progress,frames,latest,financial,logical,residual}`.

| Member | Exact meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `cause` | Opening is `{kind:"opening"}`; later observations are `{kind:"call",call_index:Index}` |
| `call_prefix_len` | Complete prefix of root.calls covered by this observation; opening is0 |
| `effect_prefix_len` | Complete prefix of root.effects covered by this observation; opening is0 |
| `head` | Entire global Snapshot at this observation |
| `progress` | Exactly `{status,completed_invocations,active_invocation,next_invocation}`; optional indices are null |
| `frames` | Exactly `{invocation,frame}` rows for every started invocation, plan order; complete actions retain original idle frame, pending action its actual frame; no invented frame for an unstarted action |
| `latest` | Exactly `{invocation,call_index}` rows for every started invocation in plan order; the index names its last original call, not a cumulative synthesized segment |
| `financial` | Complete FinancialSummary below, derived from opening and this exact effect prefix with attached original statements |
| `logical` | Complete LogicalSummary below |
| `residual` | Every positive global head liability in complete existing Liability representation, global name order; positive outside duties included |

Tables are run-local. Expanding a prefix means taking all calls/effects at positions below the corresponding length. No suffix is needed to invent fields in a call; all call bodies are already complete. Original member/local work, latest envelopes, original continuations, global state and complete history are distinct observations. Prefix equality/extension must preserve every earlier complete object. No table is shared between test cases. A public regression compares the entire root, complete expanded prefixes and bytes, not merely these lengths.

Progress rules for a successful trace: ready has completed0, active null, next0. Waiting has exactly its actual pending invocation active and only completed earlier invocations. Between-programs has active null and next equal to the next not-yet-started invocation. Complete has all invocations completed and active/next null. These are local program-control states, not external settlement/finality statuses.

## Complete financial fields

FinancialSummary has exactly `{balances,liabilities,asset_totals}`.

BalanceAccounting rows, sorted by exact `(AssetId,account)`, have exactly `{asset,account,opening,closing,transfer_debits,transfer_credits,fees_paid,fees_received,gross_debits,gross_credits,net_change}`. The set includes every opening/closing global balance row, including untouched rows with zero volumes. All amount fields are UInt. net_change is exactly `{direction,amount}`, where direction is `increase`, `decrease` or `unchanged`, and amount is the nonnegative magnitude of closing−opening; unchanged uses0. Gross debits = transfer_debits + fees_paid; gross credits = transfer_credits + fees_received. Each original Transfer/Fee contributes its full amount even for a zero or same-account effect. A later refund contributes a credit, never a negative earlier debit. These descriptive counters do not grant authority or certify an optimization.

LiabilityAccounting rows, sorted by global name, have exactly `{name,asset,debtor,creditor,opening,additions,discharges,closing,effect_indices}`. opening/closing are each exactly `{present,amount}`, with amount:null for absence and amount:UInt for presence, including present zero. additions sums the actual source Liability amounts; discharges sums actual source Discharge amounts. effect_indices names every original liability effect for this global duty in root.effects prefix order. The EffectLink's attached original Statement distinguishes addition from discharge; before/after amounts alone are insufficient. Rows include untouched outside duties and newly created or zero-valued duties; absent in both opening and closing with no effect yields no invented duty row. This adds no interest/day-count convention, mint/burn, refund right or duty consent rule.

AssetTotals rows, sorted by complete AssetId, have exactly `{asset,transfer_volume,fee_volume,gross_debit_volume,gross_credit_volume}`. They sum only the corresponding observed amounts in that exact asset; whole asset identities remain present even where an untouched opening asset has zero volumes. There is no cross-asset grand money total, implicit conversion, custody claim or native-fee field.

## Complete logical fields

LogicalSummary has exactly `{profile,resource_basis,allocation,per_invocation,attempted_total,committed_total,available_ordinary,remaining_closure_reserve}`.

profile is the existing `/2` logical-cost profile string; resource_basis is `"reference-logical-accounting-only"`. allocation is exactly `{ordinary,closure_reserve}` with the declared UInt values unchanged. per_invocation contains exactly `{invocation,cap,attempted,committed}` for every started action instance, in plan order. cap is its unchanged Option<UInt>. attempted sums actual attempted fields for that instance's calls; committed is its latest original committed prefix (from its continuation while pending, or original Complete.committed). attempted_total sums all original call attempted values; committed_total sums these latest per-instance committed values once each. No unstarted instance is assigned fictitious work.

On the sufficiently funded ordinary successful trace, available_ordinary = allocation.ordinary − attempted_total; remaining_closure_reserve equals allocation.closure_reserve. These success equations preserve a visible reserve instead of treating it as ordinary spendable work. They do not define owner-dependent cap preflight, rejected-call accounting, reserve consumption/authority, adapter cost, numeric overflow, native fees or target adequacy. Those rules must be fixed by the owner before production. Existing local caps/committed/attempted fields never change to make a sequence fit.

## Rendering and independent expected bytes

Arrays above retain their specified order. Object key order does not determine execution order. The proposed public rendering uses the existing pretty JSON string/escaping convention and one final LF. The production serializer must retain the existing document/individual-export limits; the new report's finite admissible size and diagnostic path require the owner contract. This document provides no size waiver, streaming-protocol invention, truncation, partial success output or output-memory adequacy claim.

Complete semantics and byte compatibility require separate independent expectations after adoption. The original disjoint goldens are not sequential expectations and cannot be relabeled. Embedded OutcomeReport/Program/Plan bytes inherit their exact existing contracts, with their original independent-wire qualifications retained. New outer-field ordering/prefix representation is an authored proposed codec and cannot certify itself by producer round-trip.

===== END COMPLETE BODY =====

# Original MPLR, composition and acceptance scope

All35 original MPLR notes are complete under basis/wiki/research/mplr; their required-behavior sections are verbatim in ORIGINAL-MPLR-001-035.json. Canonical partial/conditional, kernel-security, consolidated, MC06 and PCD-composition EARS are complete and hash-pinned. Existing hostile clauses are retained original source, not new tests or judgments authored here. MPLR-035 is retained through its complete original note and UNI-004/traceability link; it is not missing merely because the earlier kernel package enumerates019–034.

| Original MPLR | This proposal's ordinary relation; requirement retained beyond it |
| --- | --- |
| 001 staged/partial | Every original local call/prefix retained; accepted external phases remain separate. |
| 002 typed persistent continuations | Original local continuations retained; persistent authenticated reopening/handoff remains OD2/OD7. |
| 003 conditional evidence | Existing local guard/event observations only; full authenticated condition policy remains required. |
| 004 partial fills/duties | Complete explicit addition/discharge/presence/residual observation; signed fill/cumulative authority conditions remain owner requirements. |
| 005 refund/compensation | A later credit cannot erase gross debit or history; actual refund/recovery authority/phase rules remain required. |
| 006 asynchronous outcomes | Raw local StageEvents remain qualified; authenticated origin/correlation/finality/consumption is not supplied. |
| 007 joins/late results | Full join availability/success and losing/late-branch duties remain open, separately from serial next. |
| 008 stage authority | No new authority rule; complete signed lifecycle/consent/revocation/expiry carrier remains OD5. |
| 009 work/fees/reserves | Original local/cumulative observations and explicit reserve data; global enforcement/native/resource/closure rules remain OD4/OD6. |
| 010 time/finality | No timeout or finality rule added; unresolved external duties remain required. |
| 011 interference | One active serial action; shared-state interference/stale-state revalidation remains separate. |
| 012 assurance status | Exact local progress/report qualifications; no inferred proof/ledger/settlement result. |
| 013 privacy | No privacy/witness delivery contract supplied; private isolation/availability/disclosure remains required. |
| 014 lowering | Actual Core source calls do not supply source-to-ZKIRv3 correspondence or arbitrary-witness soundness. |
| 015 open workflows | General finite supported programs through one public production path; no example whitelist. |
| 016 application/project scope | Existing permissionless product boundary retained; participant authorization remains separate. |
| 017 full accounting | All current local scalar balance/effect/duty fields retained; complete authenticated custody/supply/authority frame remains required. |
| 018 inspectable intent | Composition inputs/reports are inspectable; canonical signed condition/amendment/recovery displays remain required. |
| 019 new obligations | Example creation is local arithmetic, not consent; designated-owner obligation-formation rules remain required. |
| 020 certified substitution | Existing real evaluator reused; no jet or certification introduced. |
| 021 resource certificates | Descriptive logical arithmetic is not an artifact/late-bound resource certificate. |
| 022 identity roles | Existing Program/Plan fields retained; no new sequence commitment or substitution rule authored. |
| 023 mandatory conditions | Actual source Requires executes; general signed-intent control/effect correspondence remains required. |
| 024 atomic domains | Local serial completion is not global atomic settlement; domain/relocation rules remain required. |
| 025 gross-to-net | Full gross effects/fees retained; no netting optimizer or certified equivalence is introduced. |
| 026 settlement implementations | Matching new API does not establish financial/authority postconditions or external delivery. |
| 027 recursive origin/history | Full local history data is not authenticated genesis/recursive compliance/ledger uniqueness. |
| 028 semantic evolution | No migration rule; changes to code/policy/continuation semantics require the original preserved-consent contract. |
| 029 private completeness | Complete supplied global Snapshot is a local observation, not authenticated global absence/completeness evidence. |
| 030 common evidence/federation | No federation/threshold/hardware/signing evidence contract added. |
| 031 solver delegation | No delegation/authority rule added; no project solver whitelist. |
| 032 concurrent budgets | No concurrent task reservation protocol; sequential arithmetic does not discharge this case. |
| 033 payment/service | Local list completion does not imply payment finality, result availability or recipient delivery. |
| 034 logical paid request | No transport/retrieval/billing/retry identity rule added. |
| 035 solver completion | No signed solution-set refinement/optimality/liveness claim; all introduced effects and residual-duty requirements remain. |

## Five different operators

| Operator | Actual proposed contribution | Full retained boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Sequential | General finite currently supported Core/2 P-complete→Q-start in production, full local/global prefixes. | Genuine native Step/accepted predecessor/history/signed lifecycle and target correspondence remain required. |
| Disjoint parallel | Existing Pair remains unchanged, with its stronger exact-asset/state/source-duty subset, fixed starts, caps and diagnostics. | All original independence/complete joining/native Split, same-asset disjoint-account and broader schedule requirements remain. |
| Shared-state interleaving | The serial frame projection makes shared state explicit but admits no concurrent pending successor. | SI1–SI4 remain unadopted; actual reviewed scheduling/interference/read-write/history rules remain required. |
| Atomic synchronization | No many-parent source or new atomic call is implemented or advertised by Sequence.next. | Original same-contract Join/claimed cross-contract atomicity/domain/fan-in/lifetime/phase rules remain, with owner interpretation. |
| Asynchronous messaging | Existing local awaits retain original input/output data only. | Authenticated progress, pending/partial/unknown/settled/recovered outcomes, late duties, availability versus success and actual external effects remain required. |

The original PCD/MC06 source clauses are retained literally. Their older recipient-opening/ledger-induction scope and later full recursive target must remain distinguished under the current product contract; this ordinary author does not settle identity/security or proof-lineage contradictions by choosing a narrower source. Full native recursion/private multi-parent scope remains required.

## Meaning of complete frame here

Current Core/2 Snapshot contains state, balances and liabilities, and its scalar effects contain State/Transfer/Fee/Liability/Emit. The proposal preserves **every field of that current ordinary profile**. Snapshot does not encode signed intention, affine authority, consumed receipts, authenticated domains, external custody/mint/burn, pending settlement, expiry or proof artifacts. No opaque/untyped placeholder field is presented as implementing them. OD2/OD4–OD8 must specify those complete carriers/bindings and their integration before any corresponding full MPLR/native/accepted-history claim. A future profile adding fields must explicitly extend projection/lift/accounting/independent expectations; silently projecting them away is not this contract.

All original148requirements/292scenarios/20families and U0–U7/M1–M17/MC/SP/G exits remain retained as assigned, alongside the canonical source's detailed clauses, ACTUS/DeFi/library/target membership, original failures/caps/contradictions and existing child qualifications. This packet recalculates no whole-language denominator or completion percentage. Design votes, owner contracts, independent oracles, implementation, actual checks and parent acceptance remain separate records.

===== END COMPLETE BODY =====


===== COMPLETE BODY original-proposal/openspec/changes/completed-successor-reference-sequence/proposal.md SHA256 de3b255e98a4e0c21e922c89cf23b177ed8404e207d717f6f82e56e20fff6339 =====
# Complete ordinary predecessor-to-successor composition

UNADOPTED source-only proposal against main79de664a. The current ordinary public Core/2 composition consumer starts two independent disjoint members and has no completed-program successor operation. A caller copying a post Snapshot into another standalone Scenario does not supply the missing production history, cumulative accounting or public consumer.

Add a production-owned finite Sequence over actual Core/2 stage calls. Each successor receives a projection of the real complete predecessor head; the production object retains the full outside frame and every original outcome/effect/work prefix. Expose it through one public compose command reused by the existing test consumer. The complete proposed fields/signatures and comparison of two approaches are in the packet's DESIGN.md, API-AND-CALLER-TRACE.md and WIRE-CONTRACT.md.

This is general finite composition of currently supported actions, not an application whitelist or new source compiler. It keeps arbitrary third-frame state, balances and duties, supports shared exact assets along serial order and does not require identical program state schemas. All source/admission/identity/error/security/resource dependencies are enumerated for the designated owner; no ordinary author replaces those rules. No implementation or runtime/native/target result is supplied.

The CSQ clauses are local proposed refinements under UNI-002/004/005/008/020/021/023/024, MPLR-001–035 as applicable, MC06/MC07 and SP10. They do not replace, renumber or close any original requirement. Sequential, disjoint parallel, shared-state interleaving, atomic synchronization and asynchronous messaging retain separate full completion obligations. Independent expectations, source review, owner decisions, actual public observations and native/private/ledger correspondence remain separate.

===== END COMPLETE BODY =====


===== COMPLETE BODY original-proposal/openspec/changes/completed-successor-reference-sequence/design.md SHA256 172461df62d713a37dd31f2d533ef276de87ef92ca953b538925efe701cd4364 =====
# Design reference

This unadopted change selects the production-owned Sequence approach B in the packet's DESIGN.md. API-AND-CALLER-TRACE.md specifies the actual public command/library call chain and normal-path signatures. WIRE-CONTRACT.md specifies the complete report. EXAMPLES-AND-TESTS.md and the examples directory supply author-worked source inputs/prefix arithmetic and the required independent oracle contract. OWNER-DEPENDENCIES.md separates all designated-owner rules. RETAINED-LINEAGE.md preserves the full original MPLR/composition scope.

SC1–SC6 require substantive ordinary design votes before adoption. OD1–OD8 require the applicable designated-owner contract; no new source/refusal/security judgment is provided here. The success-contract choice does not grant public program approval power to the project. Existing native/resource caps/counters and failed review history remain intact.

===== END COMPLETE BODY =====


===== COMPLETE BODY original-proposal/openspec/changes/completed-successor-reference-sequence/specs/completed-successor-reference-sequence/spec.md SHA256 8ae654ee8242fa7db19a05cc08536001ccd9060e9db23f9e4cb0a6f7bb3d8c13 =====
## ADDED Requirements — proposed, not adopted

These ordinary success-contract clauses apply only after adoption and the applicable designated-owner contracts. They do not define new source/admission/identity/refusal/error/security rules. Existing original clauses and owner obligations remain verbatim in the pinned basis. All scenarios below are ordinary positive observations, not hostile/security tests or native acceptance evidence.

### Requirement: CSQ-001 Owned completed predecessor
WHEN a planned action completes and its successor is requested, the production Sequence SHALL start the successor from its actual retained complete predecessor head, preserving all earlier original outcomes and without accepting caller-supplied successor financial state or prior accounting.

#### Scenario: Two distinct successful programs
- **WHEN** FirstPayment completes with phase1, invoice6 and its original transfer/fee/discharge history, and SecondPayment is requested
- **THEN** SecondPayment's projected opening SHALL use those actual values, its source initializer SHALL NOT reset the mapped state, and the report SHALL retain the complete FirstPayment prefix.

#### Scenario: More than two invocations
- **WHEN** an additional supported program is requested after the second completes
- **THEN** the same production operation SHALL use the current retained head and prefix without an application-specific branch or a new initial budget.

### Requirement: CSQ-002 Complete framed state
WHEN a real stage invocation observes a selected local view, the production adapter SHALL preserve its complete global outside frame and retain both the original local snapshots and every lifted global Step and boundary snapshot.

#### Scenario: Different state schemas and unrelated rows
- **WHEN** P.phase and Q.progress map to one global payment.phase, Q has another declared state, and the opening also contains unrelated state, another exact asset, observer balances and an unselected duty
- **THEN** the projected values SHALL follow their explicit maps and every untouched global row SHALL remain present and equal at each whole Step/head observation.

### Requirement: CSQ-003 Sequential shared financial domain
WHEN sequential supported actions use the same exact asset/account/duty domain, the ordinary sequence SHALL carry that domain along its declared serial order through actual stage calls rather than applying the existing Pair's disjoint operator rule or treating two independent starts as succession.

#### Scenario: One asset, two source programs
- **WHEN** two different programs pay the same recipient from the same payer and explicitly discharge the same mapped invoice
- **THEN** the second program SHALL consume the first program's retained balance/duty head and the complete report SHALL name both original source programs and original call histories.

### Requirement: CSQ-004 Typed duty evolution and residuals
WHEN an observed source action creates, increases or explicitly discharges a liability, the sequence observation SHALL retain the full identity, opening/closing presence and amount, original source/effect references and complete residual set, including outside duties.

#### Scenario: Successful partial payments leave debt
- **WHEN** P creates12 and discharges6, then Q discharges3, while another duty5 is untouched
- **THEN** the complete final head SHALL retain invoice3 and outside duty5, the evolution SHALL be 0+12−6−3=3 with opening absence separately recorded, and local list completion SHALL NOT mean settlement.

#### Scenario: Present zero is observed
- **WHEN** an admitted source action explicitly discharges a present liability to zero
- **THEN** the original zero-valued row and present:true observation SHALL remain distinct from an absent opening/closing row; no effect or row SHALL be deleted by the composition reporter.

### Requirement: CSQ-005 Original complete history
WHEN the sequence reports a prefix, it SHALL retain every original invocation envelope and every segment/Execution/Step/statement/ExprId/value/snapshot/frame/capture/continuation/effect/residual/work field without replacing earlier segments by the final segment.

#### Scenario: An awaiting predecessor completes
- **WHEN** a supported predecessor has start and resume observations before completion
- **THEN** Q's later report SHALL retain all those original observations, their original local labels and work, and the exact whole-head prefix after each call.

### Requirement: CSQ-006 Ordered complete effects
WHEN a call appends source effects, the sequence SHALL preserve invocation-then-source order, original Step/Execution attachment, repetitions, zero/self effects and the explicit global name image.

#### Scenario: Repeated financial effects
- **WHEN** two programs emit similar Transfer/Fee/Emit effects under their own original statements
- **THEN** the global table SHALL retain every separate effect in order and link it to the complete original call/Step/effect rather than deduplicating or netting it.

### Requirement: CSQ-007 Gross debit and fee observations
WHEN an ordinary prefix is reported, its complete financial summary SHALL separately account for each exact asset/account's transfers, fees, gross debit/credit, opening/closing balance and net-change direction, derived from the complete observed effect prefix.

#### Scenario: Later credit follows spending
- **WHEN** two programs incur alice gross debit12 including fee3 and a later supported program transfers1 back to alice
- **THEN** alice gross debit SHALL remain12 and fees paid3 while gross credits become1 and net decrease becomes11; the other payer's debit and all original effects SHALL remain separately visible.

### Requirement: CSQ-008 Original local and cumulative work
WHEN the sequence appends a normal stage observation, it SHALL retain its original attempted/local committed/cap fields and separately report sums of attempted work per call and latest committed work per action instance without resetting earlier consumption on successor start.

#### Scenario: A successor begins at local zero
- **WHEN** P commits12 and Q's actual successful action commits14
- **THEN** original local work SHALL remain12 and14 and sequence committed/attempted work SHALL be26 for this all-advancing example, rather than14 or38 from summing repeated cumulative fields.

#### Scenario: Nonadvancing observation
- **WHEN** the current API returns successful Waiting(advanced:false) for a supported false guard
- **THEN** the sequence SHALL retain that original segment:null outcome, add its actual attempted observation, and preserve the exact continuation/global head/frame/effect prefix and successful committed prefix.

### Requirement: CSQ-009 Explicit ordinary/reserve observation
WHERE a successful ordinary trace fits its declared logical envelope, the sequence SHALL retain its declared ordinary allocation and separate closure-reserve amount, show consumed/remaining ordinary work, and leave the reserve unchanged by ordinary calls.

#### Scenario: A visible unspent reserve survives succession
- **WHEN** an ordinary allocation40 and closure reserve7 accompany the two successful actions totaling26 logical attempted work
- **THEN** the ordinary remaining observation SHALL be14, the reserve observation7, and no successor SHALL reset the cumulative consumed observation to its own local work.

This clause specifies success-trace arithmetic only. Global enforcement, failure charges, reserve authority, numeric limits and target resource adequacy remain designated-owner contracts and cannot be inferred from this scenario.

### Requirement: CSQ-010 Actual pending continuation
WHEN a sequence action waits and later resumes, its production path SHALL use the exact original local continuation with the real existing stage API while preserving the complete outside frame; it SHALL begin no successor until that action actually completes.

#### Scenario: Waiting is visible before succession
- **WHEN** a supported P waits for an event, then advances under the declared normal event input
- **THEN** its original waiting/resume frames SHALL remain observable, the outside frame SHALL be preserved throughout, and Q SHALL first start from the subsequent actual completed P head.

### Requirement: CSQ-011 One real public production path
WHEN an external developer invokes the proposed ordinary compose command or its test-manifest adapter, both SHALL use the same production Sequence/loader/driver over actual loaded programs and stage operations; expected observations SHALL never supply runtime state or accounting.

#### Scenario: Public compose without expected files
- **WHEN** a new finite plan of supported sources, complete opening, inputs, bindings and operations is supplied without any expected-output path
- **THEN** the actual public compose command SHALL execute its local source sequence and print the complete run report through the production path.

### Requirement: CSQ-012 Complete observations and public bytes
WHEN a normal sequence report is serialized or compared, it SHALL preserve every complete root/catalog/plan/call/effect/prefix/head/frame/financial/logical/residual field defined by WIRE-CONTRACT.md, with exact run-local prefix attachments and a final LF in public output.

#### Scenario: Full independent comparison
- **WHEN** complete expectations and consumer-specific byte goldens were independently frozen before the real public run
- **THEN** the binary regression SHALL compare the full report/bytes and every complete expanded prefix, including all preserved rows and original nested reports; matching only final balances SHALL be insufficient evidence for this predicate.

### Requirement: CSQ-013 Local status and evidence boundary
WHEN local planned programs finish, wait or remain between invocations, the report SHALL expose that exact local progress and keep it separate from external payment/delivery/finality, native proof, private handoff and ledger acceptance claims.

#### Scenario: Positive residual after local list completion
- **WHEN** both selected source programs complete while invoice3 and outside duty5 remain
- **THEN** local progress SHALL be complete with the full positive residuals still present and no whole-obligation settlement claim.

### Requirement: CSQ-014 Other operators remain separate
WHEN this ordinary sequential capability is reported, it SHALL retain separate dispositions for sequential, disjoint parallel, shared-state interleaving, atomic synchronization and asynchronous messaging, including full native/history/global-resource/private/target requirements.

#### Scenario: Sequential local observation
- **WHEN** the proposed public two-program sequence later has its named positive predicates observed
- **THEN** only those named ordinary child predicates SHALL receive evidence; Pair requirements, SI1–SI4, atomic Join/domain semantics, asynchronous outcomes/late duties and parent MC06/SP10/U4 exits SHALL remain independently required.

### Requirement: CSQ-015 Independent expectation provenance
WHEN a source or result review uses financial/work or public-wire expectations, it SHALL retain their independent pre-run derivation, exact source/semantic/codec premises and prior source exposure; authored arithmetic or codec agreement SHALL NOT be relabeled an independent compiler-wire oracle.

#### Scenario: This proposal's worked example
- **WHEN** a custodian receives the adopted plan/source inputs and this authored contract
- **THEN** it SHALL derive the complete values/Program/Plan/prefixes/bytes independently of production evaluation, declare exposure and authored-codec conditions, and freeze the exact expected artifacts before any consumer comparison.

### Requirement: CSQ-016 Additive compatibility
WHEN the new ordinary sequence path is integrated, existing Core/1/Core/2 stage APIs, original stage DTOs/digests/admission/costs, disjoint Pair and all original fixture/oracle/result records SHALL retain their exact selected behavior and qualifications unless a separately scoped reviewed extension explicitly changes them.

#### Scenario: New public profile beside existing consumers
- **WHEN** a sequential-proposed manifest is selected
- **THEN** it SHALL use the new production operation without relabeling old disjoint schedules as sequential, changing old profiles or weakening whole-program disjoint requirements.

===== END COMPLETE BODY =====


===== COMPLETE BODY original-proposal/openspec/changes/completed-successor-reference-sequence/tasks.md SHA256 04000233c2318c3f6a8528e99f15bafc7df5b42bed5487c5e71aa62660bc565a =====
# Implementation tasks after adoption

- [ ] Obtain and retain substantive ordinary SC1–SC6 decisions and applicable OD1–OD8 owner contracts; do not silently fill owner rules during coding.
- [ ] Implement production `stage::sequence` over existing real stage APIs, with complete global projection/lift, original call/Step/effect attachment and financial/work observations.
- [ ] Add public `mori compose`, its feature exposure and shared loader/driver; add the existing `mori test` adapter without a test-owned evaluator/accounting path.
- [ ] Supply complete new/changed source and exact pins for the actual integrated tree. Preserve existing stage and Pair semantics/fixtures/goldens.
- [ ] Obtain independent input-only custody after adopted semantics/codec and before execution. Freeze full Program/Plan, every complete projected/global Step/head/financial/work/history observation and consumer-specific bytes. Preserve source-exposure qualifications.
- [ ] Implement meaningful public-binary and library tests for two distinct same-asset programs, third-frame preservation and a contrasting later-credit chain; add staged normal-path observations against independent expectations.
- [ ] Run only root's inspected authorized predicates after source/owner/resource review; preserve actual commands, source pins, terminals, cleanup and mismatches.
- [ ] Obtain fresh non-author full-candidate/result review. Record only named ordinary predicates; retain all parent/native/proof/ledger/target/private/operator obligations.

No task is executed by this proposal. This checklist is implementation scope in the existing workflow, not a new runner or campaign.

# MPLR-001 — Staged and partial transaction semantics

## Required behavior

When a supported transaction spans several settlement steps, Moriarty shall represent each committed step and the outstanding transaction state explicitly.

## Motivation and evidence status

A local transaction succeeds while a later receipt or withdrawal fails. This requirement is a user-directed design draft informed by the NEAR teardown, not a claim of implemented Moriarty behavior. [NEAR source](https://github.com/near/nearcore/tree/a47cf412bf55b020421e99e5fc5b15ed970a7a61), [Intents source](https://github.com/near/intents/tree/32a7836f825e8c984c26149f4456793ec7e3d49a), and the [research log](index.md) anchor the investigation. Exact per-requirement source ranges and expert dispositions are consolidated in the teardown evidence; repository-wide links alone do not discharge a claim.

## Theory questions for later deep research

Investigate Trace semantics, small-step operational semantics, refinement of distributed histories. Which judgment represents this behavior? What assumptions does its soundness theorem need? What counterexample separates candidate approaches? What is preserved under composition and compilation to Midnight ZKIRv3?

Candidate language studies: Daml, Scilla, Motoko. These are research leads, not assertions that those languages already solve the requirement or are directly compatible with Midnight.

## Acceptance examples

- Required: Prove each accepted prefix preserves authority, accounting and outstanding duties; do not label a successful prefix complete.
- Reject or report unresolved: An accepted debit followed by an unresolved withdrawal is reported as fully settled.

## Open design choices

Concrete syntax, static versus dynamic obligations, proof representation, completeness limits and target cost remain open. The behavioral requirement must survive a change of implementation technique. Preserve declared external assumptions and distinguish recorded request/funding, condition fulfillment and final delivery.

## Status history

2026-09-19: drafted during NEAR teardown. Await source-level review and comparative PL research; no implementation or theorem accepted.

## NEAR teardown evidence pointers

- [AR26: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/nearcore/blob/a47cf412bf55b020421e99e5fc5b15ed970a7a61/runtime/runtime/src/lib.rs#L529-L540).
- [AR26: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/nearcore/blob/a47cf412bf55b020421e99e5fc5b15ed970a7a61/runtime/runtime/src/lib.rs#L860-L904).
- [IK12: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/intents/blob/32a7836f825e8c984c26149f4456793ec7e3d49a/contracts/defuse/src/contract/intents/state.rs#L20-L336).
- [IK12: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/intents/blob/32a7836f825e8c984c26149f4456793ec7e3d49a/contracts/defuse/src/contract/tokens/nep141/withdraw.rs#L54-L226).

These source facts motivate the research problem; they do not prove the proposed Moriarty semantics. See the [NEAR study](../near/index.md).

[OpenSpec/EARS traceability](../../../openspec/changes/partial-and-conditional-transactions/requirements.md) records the planned behavior. Theory selection remains open.

# MPLR-002 — Typed persistent continuations

## Required behavior

When execution suspends for a later event, Moriarty shall persist a typed continuation bound to the program, predecessor, awaited events, authority and outstanding duties.

## Motivation and evidence status

NEAR promises and callbacks resume across transactions without a fresh original-user signature. This requirement is a user-directed design draft informed by the NEAR teardown, not a claim of implemented Moriarty behavior. [NEAR source](https://github.com/near/nearcore/tree/a47cf412bf55b020421e99e5fc5b15ed970a7a61), [Intents source](https://github.com/near/intents/tree/32a7836f825e8c984c26149f4456793ec7e3d49a), and the [research log](index.md) anchor the investigation. Exact per-requirement source ranges and expert dispositions are consolidated in the teardown evidence; repository-wide links alone do not discharge a claim.

## Theory questions for later deep research

Investigate Session types, typestate, affine capabilities, defunctionalization, continuation typing. Which judgment represents this behavior? What assumptions does its soundness theorem need? What counterexample separates candidate approaches? What is preserved under composition and compilation to Midnight ZKIRv3?

Candidate language studies: E, Koka, Rust session-type libraries. These are research leads, not assertions that those languages already solve the requirement or are directly compatible with Midnight.

## Acceptance examples

- Required: Resume a valid continuation once with correctly typed evidence and the signed stage policy.
- Reject or report unresolved: Replay or attach an unrelated callback to consume reserved authority twice.

## Open design choices

Concrete syntax, static versus dynamic obligations, proof representation, completeness limits and target cost remain open. The behavioral requirement must survive a change of implementation technique. Preserve declared external assumptions and distinguish recorded request/funding, condition fulfillment and final delivery.

## Status history

2026-09-19: drafted during NEAR teardown. Await source-level review and comparative PL research; no implementation or theorem accepted.

## NEAR teardown evidence pointers

- [AR30: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/nearcore/blob/a47cf412bf55b020421e99e5fc5b15ed970a7a61/runtime/runtime/src/lib.rs#L1460-L1515).
- [AR30: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/nearcore/blob/a47cf412bf55b020421e99e5fc5b15ed970a7a61/runtime/runtime/src/lib.rs#L1625-L1675).
- [AA-fanout: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/mpc/blob/3499a2a44207df89699c9791e21623613a365a16/crates/contract/src/api/sign.rs#L165-L223).

These source facts motivate the research problem; they do not prove the proposed Moriarty semantics. See the [NEAR study](../near/index.md).

# MPLR-003 — Conditional settlement with composable evidence

## Required behavior

When a submitted transaction specifies settlement conditions, Moriarty shall withhold the specified delivery until the required combination of authenticated signatures, documentary evidence, proofs, recipient actions and other supported predicates is satisfied.

## Motivation and evidence status

User definition: submit to an address; settlement waits for the configured combination of conditions. Funding or recording may precede final delivery. This requirement is a user-directed design draft informed by the NEAR teardown, not a claim of implemented Moriarty behavior. [NEAR source](https://github.com/near/nearcore/tree/a47cf412bf55b020421e99e5fc5b15ed970a7a61), [Intents source](https://github.com/near/intents/tree/32a7836f825e8c984c26149f4456793ec7e3d49a), and the [research log](index.md) anchor the investigation. Exact per-requirement source ranges and expert dispositions are consolidated in the teardown evidence; repository-wide links alone do not discharge a claim.

## Theory questions for later deep research

Investigate Refinement/dependent types, proof-relevant predicates, evidence algebra, authorization logic. Which judgment represents this behavior? What assumptions does its soundness theorem need? What counterexample separates candidate approaches? What is preserved under composition and compilation to Midnight ZKIRv3?

Candidate language studies: Daml, Marlowe, Scilla, Simplicity. These are research leads, not assertions that those languages already solve the requirement or are directly compatible with Midnight.

## Acceptance examples

- Required: A funded request remains pending until its bound recipient signature, document predicate and proof all validate at the policy-defined settlement point.
- Reject or report unresolved: A document hash alone, an unrelated signature, or an expired proof releases the funds.

## Open design choices

Concrete syntax, static versus dynamic obligations, proof representation, completeness limits and target cost remain open. The behavioral requirement must survive a change of implementation technique. Preserve declared external assumptions and distinguish recorded request/funding, condition fulfillment and final delivery.

## Status history

2026-09-19: drafted during NEAR teardown. Await source-level review and comparative PL research; no implementation or theorem accepted.

Terminology: **conditional settlement with composable evidence requirements** is the proposed main name; **contingent settlement** remains the user’s alias. **Programmable escrow** identifies the funded/locked variant. Evidence may combine conjunction, alternatives or thresholds only as explicitly specified. A document hash establishes identity, not truth; document acceptance needs a bound predicate, trusted attestation or proof. See [XRPL escrow](https://xrpl.org/docs/concepts/payment-types/escrow) and [Daml propose/accept](https://docs.digitalasset.com/build/3.4/sdlc-howtos/smart-contracts/develop/patterns/propose-accept.html) as different, narrower precedents.

## NEAR teardown evidence pointers

- [AI13: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/intents/blob/32a7836f825e8c984c26149f4456793ec7e3d49a/contracts/escrow-swap/src/state.rs#L43-L89).
- [AI13: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/intents/blob/32a7836f825e8c984c26149f4456793ec7e3d49a/contracts/escrow-swap/src/contract/fund.rs#L11-L33).
- [AI15: inspected source range](https://github.com/near/intents/blob/32a7836f825e8c984c26149f4456793ec7e3d49a/contracts/escrow-swap/src/contract/auth_call.rs#L20-L44).

These source facts motivate the research problem; they do not prove the proposed Moriarty semantics. See the [NEAR study](../near/index.md).

[OpenSpec/EARS traceability](../../../openspec/changes/partial-and-conditional-transactions/requirements.md) records the planned behavior. Theory selection remains open.
