//! Source-derived segment layouts and the only emitter of pending-frame roles.
//! Every pending role is an ordinary public binding created by
//! `Builder::public`; this module edits no Builder field except appending the
//! advance guard's `Instruction::Assert`. The relation
//! binds the executing action's own frame only. Linking a stage-in frame to the
//! latest accepted stage-out frame, and isolation of other actions' frames, is
//! the unperformed `pending-frame-predecessor-binding` gate.
use super::PendingPositions;
use crate::{Builder, Error, Wire, error, immediate};
use midnight_zkir_v3::Instruction;
use midnight_zkir_v3::ir::Operand;
use mori_core::stage::{
    CaptureSlot, MAX_AWAITS_PER_ACTION, MAX_CAPTURE_SLOTS, MAX_EVENT_BINDERS, StageBinder,
    StagedProgram,
};
use mori_core::{ExprId, Statement, Type};
use std::ops::Range;

pub(crate) const PRE_SITE_ROLE: &str = "pre_pending_site";
pub(crate) const PRE_CAPTURE_ROLE: &str = "pre_pending_capture";
pub(crate) const EVENT_ROLE: &str = "event";
pub(crate) const POST_SITE_ROLE: &str = "post_pending_site";
pub(crate) const POST_CAPTURE_ROLE: &str = "post_pending_capture";
pub(crate) const PENDING_ROLES: [&str; 5] = [
    PRE_SITE_ROLE,
    PRE_CAPTURE_ROLE,
    EVENT_ROLE,
    POST_SITE_ROLE,
    POST_CAPTURE_ROLE,
];

/// One circuit: segment j of an action with N awaits, in global index order.
/// `slots` is the width-L layout `captures(N)`; slot i >= len is zero padding.
pub(crate) struct SegmentLayout {
    pub action: usize,
    /// Declared action name; the pending site roles are named by it.
    pub action_name: String,
    pub segment: u16,
    pub entry_site: u16,
    pub exit_site: u16,
    /// Original statement ordinals lowered by this circuit.
    pub statements: Range<usize>,
    pub guard: Option<ExprId>,
    pub slots: Vec<CaptureSlot>,
    pub len_in: u16,
    pub len_out: u16,
    pub event: Vec<StageBinder>,
    /// Emitted local steps: the true entry Await counts, the exit Await does not.
    pub replay_steps: u32,
}

/// Pre-pending wires bound into the advance environment. The recorded
/// positions stay private so only `emit_post` can turn them into the ABI.
pub(crate) struct PreFrame {
    /// Live entry captures `slots[0..len_in]`, named by slot.
    pub captures: Vec<(String, Wire)>,
    /// Event binders of the entry site, in declaration order.
    pub events: Vec<(String, Wire)>,
    pre_site_pos: u32,
    pre_capture_pos: Vec<u32>,
    event_pos: Vec<u32>,
}

fn refuse(message: impl Into<String>) -> Error {
    error("artifact", message)
}

/// N + 1 layouts per action, actions in declaration order, segments ascending.
/// The sealed plan is re-checked against the program it was derived from.
pub(crate) fn layouts(sp: &StagedProgram) -> Result<Vec<SegmentLayout>, Error> {
    let program = sp.program();
    let plan = sp.plan();
    if plan.actions.len() != program.actions.len() {
        return Err(refuse("Stage plan does not cover every program action."));
    }
    let mut layouts = Vec::new();
    for (index, (action, ap)) in program.actions.iter().zip(&plan.actions).enumerate() {
        let count = action.statements.len();
        let n = ap.sites.len();
        if ap.action != action.name || usize::from(ap.statement_count) != count {
            return Err(refuse(format!(
                "Stage plan differs from action {:?}.",
                action.name
            )));
        }
        if n > MAX_AWAITS_PER_ACTION
            || ap.segment_cost.len() != n + 1
            || ap.committed_before.len() != n + 1
        {
            return Err(refuse(format!(
                "Stage plan for {:?} lacks exactly N + 1 segments.",
                action.name
            )));
        }
        // Every source Await is a plan site, in order, and nothing else is.
        let awaits: Vec<_> = action
            .statements
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .filter_map(|(ordinal, statement)| match statement {
                Statement::Await {
                    label,
                    binders,
                    guard,
                } => Some((ordinal, label, binders, *guard)),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect();
        if awaits.len() != n {
            return Err(refuse(format!(
                "Stage plan sites differ from the awaits of {:?}.",
                action.name
            )));
        }
        for (k, (site, (ordinal, label, binders, _))) in ap.sites.iter().zip(&awaits).enumerate() {
            if usize::from(site.site) != k + 1
                || usize::from(site.ordinal) != *ordinal
                || site.label != **label
                || site.binders.len() != binders.len()
                || site.binders.len() > MAX_EVENT_BINDERS
                || site
                    .binders
                    .iter()
                    .zip(binders.iter())
                    .any(|(s, b)| s.name != b.name || s.ty != b.ty)
            {
                return Err(refuse(format!(
                    "Stage site {} of {:?} differs from its await.",
                    k + 1,
                    action.name
                )));
            }
        }
        let slots = ap
            .sites
            .last()
            .map_or_else(Vec::new, |site| site.captures.clone());
        if slots.len() > MAX_CAPTURE_SLOTS {
            return Err(refuse("Capture layout exceeds the slot bound."));
        }
        // Prefix monotonicity fixes each slot's name and type across sites.
        let mut previous = 0;
        for site in &ap.sites {
            let len = site.captures.len();
            if len < previous || site.captures[..] != slots[..len] {
                return Err(refuse(format!(
                    "Capture layouts of {:?} are not prefix-monotone.",
                    action.name
                )));
            }
            previous = len;
        }
        let ordinal = |k: usize| usize::from(ap.sites[k - 1].ordinal);
        let width = |k: usize| -> Result<u16, Error> {
            u16::try_from(ap.sites[k - 1].captures.len())
                .map_err(|_| refuse("Capture layout exceeds the slot bound."))
        };
        for j in 0..=n {
            let entry = j;
            let exit = if j == n { 0 } else { j + 1 };
            let start = if entry == 0 { 0 } else { ordinal(entry) + 1 };
            let end = if exit == 0 { count } else { ordinal(exit) };
            let replay = end
                .checked_sub(if entry == 0 { 0 } else { ordinal(entry) })
                .filter(|_| start <= end)
                .ok_or_else(|| refuse("Stage ordinals are not increasing."))?;
            layouts.push(SegmentLayout {
                action: index,
                action_name: action.name.clone(),
                segment: j as u16,
                entry_site: entry as u16,
                exit_site: exit as u16,
                statements: start..end,
                guard: (entry != 0).then(|| awaits[entry - 1].3),
                slots: slots.clone(),
                len_in: if entry == 0 { 0 } else { width(entry)? },
                len_out: if exit == 0 { 0 } else { width(exit)? },
                event: if entry == 0 {
                    Vec::new()
                } else {
                    ap.sites[entry - 1].binders.clone()
                },
                replay_steps: u32::try_from(replay)
                    .map_err(|_| refuse("Replay step count overflow."))?,
            });
        }
    }
    Ok(layouts)
}

/// Zero-await test: holds iff N = 0, since advance segments have entry >= 1.
fn zero(l: &SegmentLayout) -> bool {
    l.entry_site == 0 && l.exit_site == 0
}

fn check_layout(l: &SegmentLayout) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let width = l.slots.len();
    if usize::from(l.len_in) > width
        || usize::from(l.len_out) > width
        || (l.entry_site == 0 && (l.len_in != 0 || !l.event.is_empty() || l.guard.is_some()))
        || (l.entry_site != 0 && (l.entry_site != l.segment || l.guard.is_none()))
        || (l.exit_site == 0 && l.len_out != 0)
        || (l.exit_site != 0 && l.exit_site != l.segment + 1)
    {
        return Err(refuse("Segment layout is inconsistent with its sites."));
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// The position `Builder::public` assigns is the binding count before it.
fn public_at(
    b: &mut Builder,
    role: &str,
    name: &str,
    ty: Type,
    expected: Option<Operand>,
    constant: Option<String>,
) -> Result<(u32, Wire), Error> {
    let before = b.bindings.len();
    let position =
        u32::try_from(before).map_err(|_| error("limit", "Public ABI size overflow."))?;
    let wire = b.public(role, name, ty, expected, constant, None);
    if b.bindings.len() != before + 1 {
        return Err(refuse("Pending role was not appended at its position."));
    }
    Ok((position, wire))
}

/// Pre-pending block, inserted after the pre-liability positions. The site is
/// constrained to the entry constant and every pre slot >= len_in to 0.
pub(crate) fn emit_pre(b: &mut Builder, l: &SegmentLayout) -> Result<Option<PreFrame>, Error> {
    if zero(l) {
        if !l.slots.is_empty() || l.len_in != 0 || l.len_out != 0 || !l.event.is_empty() {
            return Err(refuse("A zero-await circuit has no pending frame."));
        }
        return Ok(None);
    }
    check_layout(l)?;
    let entry = u128::from(l.entry_site);
    let (pre_site_pos, _) = public_at(
        b,
        PRE_SITE_ROLE,
        &l.action_name,
        Type::UInt,
        Some(immediate(entry)),
        Some(entry.to_string()),
    )?;
    let len_in = usize::from(l.len_in);
    let mut captures = Vec::with_capacity(len_in);
    let mut pre_capture_pos = Vec::with_capacity(l.slots.len());
    for (i, slot) in l.slots.iter().enumerate() {
        let live = i < len_in;
        let (position, wire) = public_at(
            b,
            PRE_CAPTURE_ROLE,
            &slot.name,
            slot.ty,
            (!live).then(|| immediate(0)),
            (!live).then(|| "0".to_string()),
        )?;
        pre_capture_pos.push(position);
        if live {
            captures.push((slot.name.clone(), wire));
        }
    }
    let mut events = Vec::with_capacity(l.event.len());
    let mut event_pos = Vec::with_capacity(l.event.len());
    for binder in &l.event {
        let (position, wire) = public_at(b, EVENT_ROLE, &binder.name, binder.ty, None, None)?;
        event_pos.push(position);
        events.push((binder.name.clone(), wire));
    }
    Ok(Some(PreFrame {
        captures,
        events,
        pre_site_pos,
        pre_capture_pos,
        event_pos,
    }))
}

/// An advance segment exists only for a true guard; a false guard has no circuit.
pub(crate) fn assert_guard(b: &mut Builder, guard: Wire) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if guard.ty != Type::Bool {
        return Err(refuse("An await guard must be Bool."));
    }
    b.instructions.push(Instruction::Assert {
        cond: guard.operand,
    });
    Ok(())
}

/// Post-pending block, appended after the last post-liability position. The
/// site is the exit constant; live slots equal the wires the segment actually
/// computed and slots >= len_out equal 0.
pub(crate) fn emit_post(
    b: &mut Builder,
    l: &SegmentLayout,
    pre: Option<&PreFrame>,
    live: &[(&str, Wire)],
) -> Result<Option<PendingPositions>, Error> {
    let pre = match (zero(l), pre) {
        (true, None) => {
            if !live.is_empty() {
                return Err(refuse("A zero-await circuit has no pending captures."));
            }
            return Ok(None);
        }
        (false, Some(pre)) => pre,
        _ => return Err(refuse("Pending pre and post frames disagree.")),
    };
    check_layout(l)?;
    let len_out = usize::from(l.len_out);
    if pre.pre_capture_pos.len() != l.slots.len()
        || pre.event_pos.len() != l.event.len()
        || pre.captures.len() != usize::from(l.len_in)
    {
        return Err(refuse("Pending pre frame differs from the segment layout."));
    }
    if live.len() != len_out
        || live
            .iter()
            .zip(&l.slots)
            .any(|((name, wire), slot)| *name != slot.name || wire.ty != slot.ty)
    {
        return Err(refuse("Live captures differ from the exit layout."));
    }
    let exit = u128::from(l.exit_site);
    let (post_site, _) = public_at(
        b,
        POST_SITE_ROLE,
        &l.action_name,
        Type::UInt,
        Some(immediate(exit)),
        Some(exit.to_string()),
    )?;
    let mut post_captures = Vec::with_capacity(l.slots.len());
    for (i, slot) in l.slots.iter().enumerate() {
        let (expected, constant) = match live.get(i) {
            Some((_, wire)) => (wire.operand.clone(), None),
            None => (immediate(0), Some("0".to_string())),
        };
        let (position, _) = public_at(
            b,
            POST_CAPTURE_ROLE,
            &slot.name,
            slot.ty,
            Some(expected),
            constant,
        )?;
        post_captures.push(position);
    }
    Ok(Some(PendingPositions {
        pre_site: pre.pre_site_pos,
        pre_captures: pre.pre_capture_pos.clone(),
        events: pre.event_pos.clone(),
        post_site,
        post_captures,
    }))
}

// Test-only Builder introspection (H21/H27 structure). Not executed until the
// exact closure is classified; no SDK check, model, VM or proving is used.
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use midnight_transient_crypto::curve::Fr;

    const ESCROW: &str = r#"language "moriarty/2";
agreement ContinuationEscrowOracle {
  asset PAY = { network: "local", issuer: "continuation-escrow-oracle", reference: "PAY", scale: 0 };
  state marker: UInt = 77;
  action deliver(amount: UInt) {
    transfer(PAY, "alice", "escrow", amount);
    liability("release", PAY, "escrow", "bob", amount);
    await delivery(ready: Bool) when ready;
    transfer(PAY, "escrow", "bob", amount);
    discharge("release", amount);
  }
}
"#;
    const RESERVE: &str = r#"language "moriarty/2";
agreement ReserveContinuationOracle {
  asset CREDIT = { network: "local", issuer: "continuation-reserve-oracle", reference: "UNIT", scale: 0 };
  state basis: UInt = 40;
  state allocated: UInt = 0;
  state marker: UInt = 77;
  action allocate(extra: UInt) {
    let held = basis + extra;
    next basis = basis - 5;
    liability("allocation", CREDIT, "reserve", "target", held);
    await approval(quote: UInt) when quote >= held;
    next allocated = held;
    transfer(CREDIT, "reserve", "target", held);
    discharge("allocation", held);
    next basis = basis + quote;
    await settlement(ready: Bool) when ready;
    requires quote <= 50;
    fee(CREDIT, "reserve", "treasury", extra);
    emit "settled_quote"(quote);
  }
}
"#;
    const MIXED: &str = r#"language "moriarty/2";
agreement Mixed {
  state n: UInt = 0;
  action a(x: UInt) { await go(ready: Bool) when ready; next n = x; }
  action b() { next n = n + 1; }
}
"#;

    fn load(src: &str) -> StagedProgram {
        mori_core::stage::load(src).expect("frozen oracle loads")
    }
    fn zero_imm() -> Operand {
        Operand::Immediate(Fr::from(0u128))
    }
    fn constrained_to(b: &Builder, wire: &Operand, value: &Operand) -> bool {
        b.instructions
            .iter()
            .any(|i| matches!(i, Instruction::ConstrainEq { a, b } if a == wire && b == value))
    }
    fn wire_of(b: &Builder, position: u32) -> Operand {
        b.wires[position as usize].clone()
    }
    fn site(l: &[SegmentLayout], action: usize, segment: u16) -> &SegmentLayout {
        l.iter()
            .find(|s| s.action == action && s.segment == segment)
            .unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn escrow_layouts_are_source_derived_with_original_ordinals() {
        let l = layouts(&load(ESCROW)).unwrap();
        let shape: Vec<_> = l
            .iter()
            .map(|s| {
                (
                    s.segment,
                    s.entry_site,
                    s.exit_site,
                    s.statements.clone(),
                    s.len_in,
                    s.len_out,
                    s.replay_steps,
                    s.guard.is_some(),
                )
            })
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(
            shape,
            vec![
                (0, 0, 1, 0..2, 0, 1, 2, false),
                (1, 1, 0, 3..5, 1, 0, 3, true)
            ]
        );
        for s in &l {
            assert_eq!(s.action_name, "deliver");
            assert_eq!(
                s.slots.iter().map(|c| c.name.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
                vec!["amount"]
            );
        }
        assert!(l[0].event.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(
            l[1].event,
            vec![StageBinder {
                name: "ready".into(),
                ty: Type::Bool
            }]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn reserve_layouts_cover_three_segments() {
        let l = layouts(&load(RESERVE)).unwrap();
        let shape: Vec<_> = l
            .iter()
            .map(|s| (s.statements.clone(), s.len_in, s.len_out, s.replay_steps))
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(
            shape,
            vec![(0..3, 0, 2, 3), (4..8, 2, 3, 5), (9..12, 3, 0, 4)]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            l[0].slots
                .iter()
                .map(|c| c.name.as_str())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec!["extra", "held", "quote"]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn zero_await_action_has_one_segment_and_emits_no_pending_roles() {
        let sp = load(MIXED);
        let l = layouts(&sp).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            l.iter()
                .map(|s| (s.action, s.segment, s.entry_site, s.exit_site))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec![(0, 0, 0, 1), (0, 1, 1, 0), (1, 0, 0, 0)]
        );
        let zero_layout = site(&l, 1, 0);
        assert_eq!(zero_layout.statements, 0..1);
        assert_eq!(zero_layout.replay_steps, 1);
        let mut b = Builder::default();
        let pre = emit_pre(&mut b, zero_layout).unwrap();
        assert!(pre.is_none());
        assert!(emit_post(&mut b, zero_layout, None, &[]).unwrap().is_none());
        assert!(b.bindings.is_empty() && b.instructions.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn start_segment_emits_zero_constrained_pre_frame_and_live_post_frame() {
        let sp = load(ESCROW);
        let l = layouts(&sp).unwrap();
        let mut b = Builder::default();
        let amount = b.public("input", "amount", Type::UInt, None, None, None);
        let pre = emit_pre(&mut b, &l[0]).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(pre.captures.is_empty() && pre.events.is_empty());
        let live = [("amount", amount.clone())];
        let p = emit_post(&mut b, &l[0], Some(&pre), &live)
            .unwrap()
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            (
                p.pre_site,
                p.pre_captures.clone(),
                p.events.clone(),
                p.post_site,
                p.post_captures.clone()
            ),
            (1, vec![2], vec![], 3, vec![4])
        );
        let roles: Vec<_> = b
            .bindings
            .iter()
            .map(|x| {
                (
                    x.role.as_str(),
                    x.name.as_str(),
                    x.ty,
                    x.constant.as_deref(),
                )
            })
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(
            roles,
            vec![
                ("input", "amount", Type::UInt, None),
                (PRE_SITE_ROLE, "deliver", Type::UInt, Some("0")),
                (PRE_CAPTURE_ROLE, "amount", Type::UInt, Some("0")),
                (POST_SITE_ROLE, "deliver", Type::UInt, Some("1")),
                (POST_CAPTURE_ROLE, "amount", Type::UInt, None),
            ]
        );
        assert!(constrained_to(&b, &wire_of(&b, p.pre_site), &zero_imm()));
        assert!(constrained_to(
            &b,
            &wire_of(&b, p.pre_captures[0]),
            &zero_imm()
        ));
        assert!(constrained_to(
            &b,
            &wire_of(&b, p.post_site),
            &Operand::Immediate(Fr::from(1u128))
        ));
        // The post capture equals the actual input wire, not a host value.
        assert!(constrained_to(
            &b,
            &wire_of(&b, p.post_captures[0]),
            &amount.operand
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn final_segment_binds_entry_captures_and_events_and_zeroes_post_frame() {
        let sp = load(ESCROW);
        let l = layouts(&sp).unwrap();
        let mut b = Builder::default();
        let pre = emit_pre(&mut b, &l[1]).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            pre.captures
                .iter()
                .map(|(n, _)| n.as_str())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec!["amount"]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            pre.events
                .iter()
                .map(|(n, w)| (n.as_str(), w.ty))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec![("ready", Type::Bool)]
        );
        let guard = pre.events[0].1.clone();
        let before = b.instructions.len();
        assert_guard(&mut b, guard.clone()).unwrap();
        // The guard is one direct Assert on the event wire, not a ConstrainEq.
        assert_eq!(b.instructions.len(), before + 1);
        assert!(matches!(
            &b.instructions[before],
            Instruction::Assert { cond } if *cond == guard.operand
        ));
        assert!(!constrained_to(
            &b,
            &guard.operand,
            &Operand::Immediate(Fr::from(1u128))
        ));
        let p = emit_post(&mut b, &l[1], Some(&pre), &[]).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(b.bindings[p.events[0] as usize].role, EVENT_ROLE);
        assert!(b.instructions.iter().any(|i| matches!(
            i,
            Instruction::ConstrainToBoolean { val } if *val == wire_of(&b, p.events[0])
        )));
        assert_eq!(
            b.bindings[p.post_site as usize].constant.as_deref(),
            Some("0")
        );
        assert!(constrained_to(&b, &wire_of(&b, p.post_site), &zero_imm()));
        assert_eq!(
            b.bindings[p.post_captures[0] as usize].constant.as_deref(),
            Some("0")
        );
        assert!(constrained_to(
            &b,
            &wire_of(&b, p.post_captures[0]),
            &zero_imm()
        ));
        // A live pre capture is range-checked, never zero-constrained.
        assert_eq!(b.bindings[p.pre_captures[0] as usize].constant, None);
        assert!(!constrained_to(
            &b,
            &wire_of(&b, p.pre_captures[0]),
            &zero_imm()
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn mismatched_frames_and_live_lists_refuse() {
        let sp = load(ESCROW);
        let l = layouts(&sp).unwrap();
        let mut b = Builder::default();
        // A pending layout without its pre frame refuses.
        assert_eq!(
            emit_post(&mut b, &l[0], None, &[]).unwrap_err().code,
            "mori::backend::artifact"
        );
        let pre = emit_pre(&mut b, &l[0]).unwrap().unwrap();
        let wrong = b.public("input", "other", Type::UInt, None, None, None);
        for live in [
            vec![],
            vec![("other", wrong.clone())],
            vec![(
                "amount",
                Wire {
                    operand: wrong.operand.clone(),
                    ty: Type::Bool,
                },
            )],
            vec![("amount", wrong.clone()), ("amount", wrong.clone())],
        ] {
            assert_eq!(
                emit_post(&mut b, &l[0], Some(&pre), &live)
                    .unwrap_err()
                    .code,
                "mori::backend::artifact"
            );
        }
        let not_bool = Wire {
            operand: zero_imm(),
            ty: Type::UInt,
        };
        let before = b.instructions.len();
        assert_eq!(
            assert_guard(&mut b, not_bool).unwrap_err().code,
            "mori::backend::artifact"
        );
        assert_eq!(b.instructions.len(), before);
        // A zero-await layout refuses a pre frame from another layout.
        let mixed = load(MIXED);
        let ml = layouts(&mixed).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            emit_post(&mut b, site(&ml, 1, 0), Some(&pre), &[])
                .unwrap_err()
                .code,
            "mori::backend::artifact"
        );
    }
}

//! Continuation compilation through the ordinary scalar/financial lowerer.
use super::artifact::{
    ActionLogical, LOGICAL_ENFORCEMENT, MAX_STAGED_CIRCUITS, STAGE_GATES, STAGED_ABI_VERSION,
    STAGED_ARTIFACT_FORMAT, STAGED_RELATION_VERSION, SegmentLogical, StagedArtifact, StagedCircuit,
    StagedResources,
};
use super::frame;
use crate::{
    Error, Gate, MAX_LIABILITY_REGISTRY_ENTRIES, Target, error, liabilities, lower_action_segment,
    proving,
};
use mori_core::{
    LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE_2,
    stage::{STAGE_LABELS, StagedProgram},
};
use std::path::Path;

/// Compile every action into its successful continuation segments.
///
/// `out` is the caller's publication destination. Compilation remains pure and
/// the backend performs no filesystem effect; the CLI reserves that directory
/// and publishes the manifest last through its existing publication helpers
/// after this function returns successfully. Fresh output must pass the full
/// `/4` decoder predicate before it is returned. That predicate establishes
/// shape validity only: it does not recompute the program, plan or context
/// digests, so neither a decoded nor a fresh artifact is provenance-bound.
pub fn compile_staged(src: &str, target: &Target, _out: &Path) -> Result<StagedArtifact, Error> {
    if target.signed_policy.is_some() {
        return Err(error(
            "unsupported",
            "Staged moriarty/2 relations are unsigned; a signed Target is refused.",
        ));
    }
    let sp = mori_core::stage::load(src).map_err(|e| error("program", e.to_string()))?;
    target.validate()?;
    let program = sp.program();
    if program
        .assets
        .iter()
        .any(|asset| asset.identity.network != target.network)
    {
        return Err(error(
            "target",
            "Asset network differs from the target domain; cross-domain assets are unsupported.",
        ));
    }
    let registry = liabilities::registry(program)?;
    let layouts = frame::layouts(&sp)?;
    if layouts.len() > MAX_STAGED_CIRCUITS {
        return Err(error("limit", "Program exceeds staged circuit limit."));
    }
    let mut resources = StagedResources {
        instructions: 0,
        zkir_bytes: 0,
        artifact_bytes: 0,
        public_values: 0,
        max_replay_steps: 0,
        liability_registry_entries: registry.len() as u32,
        max_liability_registry_entries: MAX_LIABILITY_REGISTRY_ENTRIES as u32,
        logical_profile: LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE_2.into(),
        logical_enforcement: LOGICAL_ENFORCEMENT.into(),
        logical: logical_resources(&sp),
    };
    let mut circuits = Vec::with_capacity(layouts.len());
    for layout in &layouts {
        let action = &program.actions[layout.action];
        let (circuit, pending_positions) = lower_action_segment(
            program,
            action,
            target,
            &registry,
            None,
            Some((&sp, layout)),
        )?;
        resources.instructions = resources
            .instructions
            .checked_add(circuit.instruction_count)
            .ok_or_else(|| error("limit", "Instruction count overflow."))?;
        resources.zkir_bytes = resources
            .zkir_bytes
            .checked_add(
                u32::try_from(circuit.zkir.len())
                    .map_err(|_| error("limit", "Artifact too large."))?,
            )
            .ok_or_else(|| error("limit", "Artifact size overflow."))?;
        resources.public_values = resources
            .public_values
            .checked_add(
                u32::try_from(circuit.public_inputs.len())
                    .map_err(|_| error("limit", "Public ABI size overflow."))?,
            )
            .ok_or_else(|| error("limit", "Public ABI size overflow."))?;
        resources.max_replay_steps = resources.max_replay_steps.max(circuit.replay_steps);
        if resources.instructions > target.max_instructions
            || resources.zkir_bytes > target.max_artifact_bytes
        {
            return Err(error(
                "limit",
                "Program exceeds target instruction or ZKIR byte limit.",
            ));
        }
        circuits.push(StagedCircuit {
            action: circuit.action,
            segment: layout.segment,
            entry_site: layout.entry_site,
            exit_site: layout.exit_site,
            zkir: circuit.zkir,
            context_hash: circuit.context_hash,
            capture_width: layout.slots.len() as u16,
            capture_in: layout.slots[..layout.len_in as usize].to_vec(),
            capture_out: layout.slots[..layout.len_out as usize].to_vec(),
            event: layout.event.clone(),
            public_inputs: circuit.public_inputs,
            pending_positions,
            instruction_count: circuit.instruction_count,
            effect_frame: circuit.effect_frame,
            balance_frame: circuit.balance_frame,
            liability_frame: circuit.liability_frame,
            replay_steps: circuit.replay_steps,
        });
    }
    let mut artifact = StagedArtifact {
        format: STAGED_ARTIFACT_FORMAT.into(),
        abi: STAGED_ABI_VERSION.into(),
        relation: STAGED_RELATION_VERSION.into(),
        authorization_mode: target.authorization_mode().into(),
        program_hash: proving::hex(&sp.program_hash()),
        plan_hash: proving::hex(&sp.plan_hash()),
        semantics: program.semantics.clone(),
        qualification: STAGE_LABELS[0].1.into(),
        authentication: STAGE_LABELS[1].1.into(),
        consumption: STAGE_LABELS[2].1.into(),
        target: target.clone(),
        circuits,
        resources,
        gates: [
            "native-proof-generation",
            "circuit-cost-and-k-measurement",
            "fraud-proof-cost-and-k-measurement",
            "authenticated-ledger-frame-and-phase-correspondence",
            "signed-intent-and-predecessor-history-verification",
            "live-ledger-deployment",
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .chain(STAGE_GATES)
        .map(|p| Gate {
            predicate: p.into(),
            status: "unperformed".into(),
        })
        .collect(),
    };
    // Identical fixed-point procedure and pretty serialization to /3.
    for _ in 0..12 {
        let bytes = serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&artifact)
            .map_err(|e| error("serialization", e.to_string()))?;
        let count = u32::try_from(bytes.len())
            .map_err(|_| error("limit", "Artifact envelope exceeds u32 byte count."))?;
        if count > target.max_artifact_bytes {
            return Err(error(
                "limit",
                "Artifact envelope exceeds target byte limit.",
            ));
        }
        if count == artifact.resources.artifact_bytes {
            // Nothing the decoder would refuse is ever returned for publication.
            artifact.validate()?;
            return Ok(artifact);
        }
        artifact.resources.artifact_bytes = count;
    }
    Err(error(
        "serialization",
        "Artifact size count did not stabilize.",
    ))
}

fn logical_resources(sp: &StagedProgram) -> Vec<ActionLogical> {
    sp.plan()
        .actions
        .iter()
        .map(|action| ActionLogical {
            action: action.action.clone(),
            plan_total: action.plan_total,
            segments: action
                .segment_cost
                .iter()
                .zip(&action.committed_before)
                .enumerate()
                .map(
                    |(segment, (&segment_logical, &committed_before))| SegmentLogical {
                        segment: segment as u16,
                        segment_logical,
                        committed_before,
                    },
                )
                .collect(),
        })
        .collect()
}

===== COMPLETE BODY current-source/crates/mori-backend/src/stage/mod.rs SHA256 69fdceb125c3ef29a29f80b45b54f09eb601311d3ed181a2579f85049b796693 =====
//! Staged `moriarty/2` compilation to `/4` artifacts: N + 1 successful-segment
//! circuits per action with N awaits, through the ordinary `/3` lowerer.
//! `/4` is a distinct type with no conversion to the `/3` Artifact, so proving,
//! signed and native consumers cannot accept it. Unsigned only: there is no
//! event authentication, stage authority or consumption, and all ten gates
//! remain unperformed.
mod artifact;
pub(crate) mod context;
pub(crate) mod frame;
mod lower;

pub use artifact::{
    ActionLogical, LOGICAL_ENFORCEMENT, MAX_STAGED_CIRCUITS, PendingPositions,
    STAGE_CONTEXT_FORMAT, STAGE_GATES, STAGED_ABI_VERSION, STAGED_ARTIFACT_FORMAT,
    STAGED_RELATION_VERSION, SegmentLogical, StagedArtifact, StagedCircuit, StagedResources,
};
pub use lower::compile_staged;

use crate::{Error, error};
use mori_core::Program;

/// Explicit `/1` entry refusal for legacy backend consumers, with the existing
/// `mori::backend::program` wrapping of Core's profile error.
pub(crate) fn require_core1(program: &Program) -> Result<(), Error> {
    mori_core::stage::require_core1(program).map_err(|e| error("program", e.to_string()))
}

/// Typed refusal for an Await reaching a `/1`-only statement match.
pub(crate) fn core1_refusal(program: &Program) -> Error {
    match require_core1(program) {
        Err(e) => e,
        Ok(()) => error(
            "program",
            "mori::core::profile: Await statements require the moriarty/2 staged compiler.",
        ),
    }
}

[package]
name = "mori-core"
version = "0.1.0"
edition.workspace = true
rust-version.workspace = true
license.workspace = true

[features]
core2-disjoint-draft = []
# Opt-in and non-default: enables only the Midnight-native signing-frame
# compatibility test. Ordinary mori-core consumers and SDK-free test
# selection never pull these dependencies.
midnight-compat-tests = ["dep:midnight-base-crypto", "dep:midnight-serialize"]

[dependencies]
mori-lexer.workspace = true
serde.workspace = true
serde_json.workspace = true
thiserror.workspace = true
sha2.workspace = true
k256.workspace = true

//! Versioned canonical owner intent for the native core.
//! Signature checks establish possession of a key, not ledger authority or proof validity.
use crate::{
    AssetId, Effect, Error, Execution, Liability, Program, SEMANTICS, Scenario, UInt, simulate,
};
use k256::{ecdsa, schnorr};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub const INTENT_PROFILE: &str = "moriarty-owner-intent/2";
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Scheme {
    SchnorrBip340,
    EcdsaSecp256k1Sha256,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct AssetLimit {
    pub asset: AssetId,
    pub beneficiary: String,
    pub gross_cap: UInt,
    pub fee_cap: UInt,
    pub net_floor: UInt,
    pub liability_cap: UInt,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Authorization {
    pub network: String,
    pub anchor: String,
    pub program_address: String,
    pub target_hash: String,
    pub signer: String,
    pub public_key_hex: String,
    pub scheme: Scheme,
    pub nonce: UInt,
    pub predecessor: String,
    pub not_before: UInt,
    pub not_after: UInt,
    pub limits: Vec<AssetLimit>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct OwnerIntent {
    pub profile: String,
    pub source_hash: String,
    pub program_hash: String,
    pub semantics: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub scenario_hash: String,
    pub assets: Vec<AssetId>,
    pub authority: Authorization,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PreparedIntent {
    pub intent: OwnerIntent,
    pub signing_message_hex: String,
    pub signing_message_sha256: String,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct SignedIntent {
    pub intent: OwnerIntent,
    pub signature_hex: String,
}

pub fn hash_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    hex(&Sha256::digest(bytes))
}
pub fn canonical<T: Serialize>(value: &T) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error> {
    fn sort(v: serde_json::Value) -> serde_json::Value {
        match v {
            serde_json::Value::Object(map) => {
                let sorted: std::collections::BTreeMap<_, _> =
                    map.into_iter().map(|(k, v)| (k, sort(v))).collect();
                serde_json::Value::Object(sorted.into_iter().collect())
            }
            serde_json::Value::Array(a) => {
                serde_json::Value::Array(a.into_iter().map(sort).collect())
            }
            v => v,
        }
    }
    let value = serde_json::to_value(value).map_err(|e| Error::new("intent", e.to_string()))?;
    serde_json::to_vec(&sort(value)).map_err(|e| Error::new("intent", e.to_string()))
}
pub fn hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    bytes.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
}
fn unhex(text: &str, len: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error> {
    if text.len() != len * 2
        || !text
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))
    {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "signature",
            "noncanonical signature/key encoding",
        ));
    }
    (0..text.len())
        .step_by(2)
        .map(|i| {
            u8::from_str_radix(&text[i..i + 2], 16)
                .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "invalid hex"))
        })
        .collect()
}
fn check_key(authority: &Authorization) -> Result<(), Error> {
    match authority.scheme {
        Scheme::SchnorrBip340 => {
            schnorr::VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&unhex(&authority.public_key_hex, 32)?)
                .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "invalid BIP340 public key"))?;
        }
        Scheme::EcdsaSecp256k1Sha256 => {
            let key = unhex(&authority.public_key_hex, 33)?;
            if !matches!(key[0], 2 | 3) {
                return Err(Error::new("signature", "expected compressed SEC1 key"));
            }
            ecdsa::VerifyingKey::from_sec1_bytes(&key)
                .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "invalid ECDSA public key"))?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub fn prepare_intent(
    program: &Program,
    source_hash: &str,
    action: &str,
    scenario: &Scenario,
    authority: &Authorization,
) -> Result<PreparedIntent, Error> {
    crate::validate(program)?;
    if !program.actions.iter().any(|a| a.name == action) {
        return Err(Error::new("intent", "unknown action"));
    }
    if source_hash.len() != 64
        || !source_hash
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))
        || [
            &authority.network,
            &authority.anchor,
            &authority.program_address,
            &authority.target_hash,
            &authority.signer,
            &authority.predecessor,
        ]
        .iter()
        .any(|s| s.is_empty() || s.len() > 1024 || s.chars().any(char::is_control))
        || authority.not_after < authority.not_before
        || authority.limits.len() > 256
    {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "intent",
            "invalid source/domain/validity binding",
        ));
    }
    let mut assets: Vec<_> = program.assets.iter().map(|a| a.identity.clone()).collect();
    assets.sort();
    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
    for limit in &authority.limits {
        if !assets.contains(&limit.asset)
            || limit.asset.network != authority.network
            || limit.beneficiary.is_empty()
            || !seen.insert(limit.asset.clone())
        {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "intent",
                "duplicate or mismatched asset authority",
            ));
        }
    }
    check_key(authority)?;
    let intent = OwnerIntent {
        profile: INTENT_PROFILE.into(),
        source_hash: source_hash.into(),
        program_hash: hash_bytes(&canonical(program)?),
        semantics: SEMANTICS.into(),
        action: action.into(),
        scenario_hash: hash_bytes(&canonical(scenario)?),
        assets,
        authority: authority.clone(),
    };
    let message = signing_message(&intent)?;
    Ok(PreparedIntent {
        intent,
        signing_message_hex: hex(&message),
        signing_message_sha256: hash_bytes(&message),
    })
}
pub fn signing_message(intent: &OwnerIntent) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error> {
    let payload = canonical(intent)?;
    if payload.len() > 65536 {
        return Err(Error::new("intent", "canonical intent exceeds 64 KiB"));
    }
    let mut bytes = b"moriarty-owner-intent/2\0".to_vec();
    bytes.extend_from_slice(&(payload.len() as u32).to_be_bytes());
    bytes.extend(payload);
    Ok(bytes)
}
pub fn verify_intent(expected: &OwnerIntent, signed: &SignedIntent, at: UInt) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if expected != &signed.intent
        || expected.profile != INTENT_PROFILE
        || expected.semantics != SEMANTICS
    {
        return Err(Error::new("intent", "signed intent binding mismatch"));
    }
    if at < expected.authority.not_before || at > expected.authority.not_after {
        return Err(Error::new("intent", "intent is outside validity interval"));
    }
    check_key(&expected.authority)?;
    let sig = unhex(&signed.signature_hex, 64)?;
    let message = signing_message(expected)?;
    match expected.authority.scheme {
        Scheme::SchnorrBip340 => {
            let key =
                schnorr::VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&unhex(&expected.authority.public_key_hex, 32)?)
                    .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "invalid BIP340 key"))?;
            let signature = schnorr::Signature::try_from(sig.as_slice())
                .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "invalid BIP340 signature"))?;
            // Midnight's native Verifier hashes the canonical message with SHA-256.
            use k256::schnorr::signature::Verifier;
            key.verify(&message, &signature)
                .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "signature rejected"))?;
        }
        Scheme::EcdsaSecp256k1Sha256 => {
            use k256::ecdsa::signature::Verifier;
            let key = ecdsa::VerifyingKey::from_sec1_bytes(&unhex(
                &expected.authority.public_key_hex,
                33,
            )?)
            .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "invalid ECDSA key"))?;
            let signature = ecdsa::Signature::from_slice(&sig)
                .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "invalid ECDSA signature"))?;
            if signature.normalize_s().is_some() {
                return Err(Error::new(
                    "signature",
                    "noncanonical high-S ECDSA signature",
                ));
            }
            key.verify(&message, &signature)
                .map_err(|_| Error::new("signature", "signature rejected"))?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Check all owner debits, fees, net goals and closing liabilities by exact asset identity.
pub fn check_effects(
    signer: &str,
    limits: &[AssetLimit],
    effects: &[Effect],
    closing: &[Liability],
) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let mut unique = BTreeSet::new();
    for l in limits {
        if !unique.insert(&l.asset) {
            return Err(Error::new("authority", "duplicate asset limit"));
        }
    }
    for effect in effects {
        let touched = match effect {
            Effect::Transfer { asset, from, .. } | Effect::Fee { asset, from, .. }
                if from == signer =>
            {
                Some(asset)
            }
            Effect::Liability { asset, debtor, .. } if debtor == signer => Some(asset),
            _ => None,
        };
        if touched.is_some_and(|asset| !limits.iter().any(|l| &l.asset == asset)) {
            return Err(Error::new("authority", "effect has no asset authority"));
        }
    }
    for liability in closing {
        if liability.debtor == signer && !limits.iter().any(|l| l.asset == liability.asset) {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "authority",
                "closing liability has no authority",
            ));
        }
    }
    for limit in limits {
        let mut gross = 0_u128;
        let mut fees = 0_u128;
        let mut credit = 0_u128;
        let mut debit = 0_u128;
        let add = |a: &mut u128, b: u128| -> Result<(), Error> {
            *a = a
                .checked_add(b)
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("authority", "accounting overflow"))?;
            Ok(())
        };
        for effect in effects {
            match effect {
                Effect::Transfer {
                    asset,
                    from,
                    to,
                    amount,
                } if asset == &limit.asset => {
                    if from == signer {
                        add(&mut gross, amount.0)?;
                    }
                    if to == &limit.beneficiary {
                        add(&mut credit, amount.0)?;
                    }
                    if from == &limit.beneficiary {
                        add(&mut debit, amount.0)?;
                    }
                }
                Effect::Fee {
                    asset,
                    from,
                    to: _,
                    amount,
                } if asset == &limit.asset => {
                    if from == signer {
                        add(&mut gross, amount.0)?;
                        add(&mut fees, amount.0)?;
                    }
                    if from == &limit.beneficiary && from != signer {
                        add(&mut debit, amount.0)?;
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        let obligations = closing
            .iter()
            .filter(|l| l.asset == limit.asset && l.debtor == signer)
            .try_fold(0_u128, |a, l| a.checked_add(l.amount.0))
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("authority", "liability overflow"))?;
        let net = credit.checked_sub(debit).and_then(|v| v.checked_sub(fees));
        if gross > limit.gross_cap.0
            || fees > limit.fee_cap.0
            || obligations > limit.liability_cap.0
            || net.is_none_or(|n| n < limit.net_floor.0)
        {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "authority",
                "gross, fee, net or liability constraint exceeded",
            ));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub fn verify_and_simulate(
    program: &Program,
    source_hash: &str,
    action: &str,
    scenario: &Scenario,
    authority: &Authorization,
    signed: &SignedIntent,
    at: UInt,
) -> Result<Execution, Error> {
    let expected = prepare_intent(program, source_hash, action, scenario, authority)?;
    verify_intent(&expected.intent, signed, at)?;
    let execution = simulate(program, action, scenario)?;
    check_effects(
        &authority.signer,
        &authority.limits,
        &execution.effects,
        &execution.post.liabilities,
    )?;
    Ok(execution)
}

/// Authenticates before metered local execution, then checks complete effects.
/// The caller's unsigned cap is outside the unchanged owner signing message.
/// Preparation, signature and effect checks are outside this logical cost profile.
/// Late authority refusal retains consumed work and exposes no execution.
// The refusal carries its charged cost report by design.
#[allow(clippy::result_large_err)]
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn verify_and_simulate_metered(
    program: &Program,
    source_hash: &str,
    action: &str,
    scenario: &Scenario,
    authority: &Authorization,
    signed: &SignedIntent,
    at: UInt,
    cap: UInt,
) -> Result<crate::MeteredExecution, crate::MeteredExecutionError> {
    let before_execution = |error| crate::MeteredExecutionError {
        error,
        cost: crate::CostReport {
            profile: crate::LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE.into(),
            cap,
            used: UInt(0),
        },
    };
    let expected = prepare_intent(program, source_hash, action, scenario, authority)
        .map_err(before_execution)?;
    verify_intent(&expected.intent, signed, at).map_err(before_execution)?;
    let metered = crate::simulate_metered(program, action, scenario, cap)?;
    check_effects(
        &authority.signer,
        &authority.limits,
        &metered.execution.effects,
        &metered.execution.post.liabilities,
    )
    .map_err(|error| crate::MeteredExecutionError {
        error,
        cost: metered.cost.clone(),
    })?;
    Ok(metered)
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub const LANGUAGE: &str = "moriarty/1";
pub const SEMANTICS: &str = "moriarty-core/1";
pub const MAX_EXPRESSIONS: usize = 8192;
pub const MAX_STATEMENTS: usize = 1024;
pub const MAX_DECLARATIONS: usize = 256;
pub const MAX_TRACE_CELLS: usize = 65_536;

/// Unsigned integral atomic units; JSON uses canonical decimal strings.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub struct UInt(pub u128);
impl Serialize for UInt {
    fn serialize<S: serde::Serializer>(&self, serializer: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {
        serializer.serialize_str(&self.0.to_string())
    }
}
impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for UInt {
    fn deserialize<D: serde::Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {
        let s = String::deserialize(deserializer)?;
        if s.is_empty()
            || !s.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())
            || (s.len() > 1 && s.starts_with('0'))
        {
            return Err(serde::de::Error::custom(
                "expected canonical unsigned decimal string",
            ));
        }
        s.parse().map(UInt).map_err(serde::de::Error::custom)
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Type {
    UInt,
    Bool,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(tag = "type", content = "value", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub enum Value {
    UInt(UInt),
    Bool(bool),
}
impl Value {
    pub fn ty(&self) -> Type {
        match self {
            Self::UInt(_) => Type::UInt,
            Self::Bool(_) => Type::Bool,
        }
    }
}
pub type ExprId = u16;
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BinaryOp {
    Add,
    Sub,
    Mul,
    Eq,
    Ne,
    Lt,
    Le,
    Gt,
    Ge,
    And,
    Or,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub enum Expr {
    LiabilityPresent {
        name: String,
    },
    LiabilityAmount {
        name: String,
    },
    Literal {
        value: Value,
    },
    Name {
        name: String,
    },
    Binary {
        op: BinaryOp,
        lhs: ExprId,
        rhs: ExprId,
    },
    Not {
        value: ExprId,
    },
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct StateDecl {
    pub name: String,
    pub ty: Type,
    pub initial: Value,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct InputDecl {
    pub name: String,
    pub ty: Type,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct AssetId {
    pub network: String,
    pub issuer: String,
    pub reference: String,
    pub scale: u8,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct AssetDecl {
    pub name: String,
    pub identity: AssetId,
}
/// Serialize-only on the shared type: decoding always goes through the frozen
/// `v1` wire, so JSON can never yield `Await`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub enum Statement {
    Await {
        label: String,
        binders: Vec<InputDecl>,
        guard: ExprId,
    },
    Let {
        name: String,
        value: ExprId,
    },
    Requires {
        predicate: ExprId,
    },
    Ensures {
        predicate: ExprId,
    },
    Next {
        state: String,
        value: ExprId,
    },
    Emit {
        name: String,
        value: ExprId,
    },
    Transfer {
        asset: String,
        from: String,
        to: String,
        amount: ExprId,
    },
    Fee {
        asset: String,
        from: String,
        to: String,
        amount: ExprId,
    },
    Liability {
        name: String,
        asset: String,
        debtor: String,
        creditor: String,
        amount: ExprId,
    },
    Discharge {
        name: String,
        amount: ExprId,
    },
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Action {
    pub name: String,
    pub inputs: Vec<InputDecl>,
    pub statements: Vec<Statement>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Program {
    pub language: String,
    pub semantics: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub states: Vec<StateDecl>,
    pub assets: Vec<AssetDecl>,
    pub expressions: Vec<Expr>,
    pub actions: Vec<Action>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Balance {
    pub account: String,
    pub asset: AssetId,
    pub amount: UInt,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Liability {
    pub name: String,
    pub asset: AssetId,
    pub debtor: String,
    pub creditor: String,
    pub amount: UInt,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Scenario {
    pub state: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    pub inputs: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    pub balances: Vec<Balance>,
    pub liabilities: Vec<Liability>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub state: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    pub balances: Vec<Balance>,
    pub liabilities: Vec<Liability>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub enum Effect {
    State {
        name: String,
        before: Value,
        after: Value,
    },
    Transfer {
        asset: AssetId,
        from: String,
        to: String,
        amount: UInt,
    },
    Fee {
        asset: AssetId,
        from: String,
        to: String,
        amount: UInt,
    },
    Liability {
        name: String,
        asset: AssetId,
        debtor: String,
        creditor: String,
        before: UInt,
        after: UInt,
    },
    Emit {
        name: String,
        value: Value,
    },
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Step {
    pub index: u16,
    pub statement: Statement,
    pub value: Option<Value>,
    pub snapshot: Snapshot,
    pub effects: Vec<Effect>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Execution {
    pub semantics: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub pre: Snapshot,
    pub post: Snapshot,
    pub steps: Vec<Step>,
    pub effects: Vec<Effect>,
}
/// Frozen `moriarty-core/1` decode wire: the baseline (fe01bc8b) derives,
/// moved verbatim. Type names are kept so serde's `expecting` text, unknown
/// variant lists and error messages stay byte-identical. There is no Await.
pub mod v1 {
    use super::{AssetDecl, Effect, Expr, ExprId, InputDecl, Snapshot, StateDecl, Value};
    use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
    #[serde(tag = "kind", deny_unknown_fields)]
    pub enum Statement {
        Let {
            name: String,
            value: ExprId,
        },
        Requires {
            predicate: ExprId,
        },
        Ensures {
            predicate: ExprId,
        },
        Next {
            state: String,
            value: ExprId,
        },
        Emit {
            name: String,
            value: ExprId,
        },
        Transfer {
            asset: String,
            from: String,
            to: String,
            amount: ExprId,
        },
        Fee {
            asset: String,
            from: String,
            to: String,
            amount: ExprId,
        },
        Liability {
            name: String,
            asset: String,
            debtor: String,
            creditor: String,
            amount: ExprId,
        },
        Discharge {
            name: String,
            amount: ExprId,
        },
    }
    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
    #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
    pub struct Action {
        pub name: String,
        pub inputs: Vec<InputDecl>,
        pub statements: Vec<Statement>,
    }
    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
    #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
    pub struct Program {
        pub language: String,
        pub semantics: String,
        pub name: String,
        pub states: Vec<StateDecl>,
        pub assets: Vec<AssetDecl>,
        pub expressions: Vec<Expr>,
        pub actions: Vec<Action>,
    }
    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
    #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
    pub struct Step {
        pub index: u16,
        pub statement: Statement,
        pub value: Option<Value>,
        pub snapshot: Snapshot,
        pub effects: Vec<Effect>,
    }
    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
    #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
    pub struct Execution {
        pub semantics: String,
        pub action: String,
        pub pre: Snapshot,
        pub post: Snapshot,
        pub steps: Vec<Step>,
        pub effects: Vec<Effect>,
    }
}
impl From<v1::Statement> for Statement {
    fn from(s: v1::Statement) -> Self {
        use v1::Statement as W;
        match s {
            W::Let { name, value } => Self::Let { name, value },
            W::Requires { predicate } => Self::Requires { predicate },
            W::Ensures { predicate } => Self::Ensures { predicate },
            W::Next { state, value } => Self::Next { state, value },
            W::Emit { name, value } => Self::Emit { name, value },
            W::Transfer {
                asset,
                from,
                to,
                amount,
            } => Self::Transfer {
                asset,
                from,
                to,
                amount,
            },
            W::Fee {
                asset,
                from,
                to,
                amount,
            } => Self::Fee {
                asset,
                from,
                to,
                amount,
            },
            W::Liability {
                name,
                asset,
                debtor,
                creditor,
                amount,
            } => Self::Liability {
                name,
                asset,
                debtor,
                creditor,
                amount,
            },
            W::Discharge { name, amount } => Self::Discharge { name, amount },
        }
    }
}
impl From<v1::Action> for Action {
    fn from(a: v1::Action) -> Self {
        Self {
            name: a.name,
            inputs: a.inputs,
            statements: a.statements.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect(),
        }
    }
}
impl From<v1::Program> for Program {
    fn from(p: v1::Program) -> Self {
        Self {
            language: p.language,
            semantics: p.semantics,
            name: p.name,
            states: p.states,
            assets: p.assets,
            expressions: p.expressions,
            actions: p.actions.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect(),
        }
    }
}
impl From<v1::Step> for Step {
    fn from(s: v1::Step) -> Self {
        Self {
            index: s.index,
            statement: s.statement.into(),
            value: s.value,
            snapshot: s.snapshot,
            effects: s.effects,
        }
    }
}
impl From<v1::Execution> for Execution {
    fn from(e: v1::Execution) -> Self {
        Self {
            semantics: e.semantics,
            action: e.action,
            pre: e.pre,
            post: e.post,
            steps: e.steps.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect(),
            effects: e.effects,
        }
    }
}
// Every direct, nested or generic decode of a shared type (CLI, replay,
// backend bundles) is routed through `v1`, so no decode site can be missed.
macro_rules! decode_via_v1 {
    ($($ty:ident),*) => {$(
        impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for $ty {
            fn deserialize<D: serde::Deserializer<'de>>(decoder: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {
                v1::$ty::deserialize(decoder).map(Self::from)
            }
        }
    )*};
}
decode_via_v1!(Statement, Action, Program, Step, Execution);
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
#[error("{code}: {message}")]
pub struct Error {
    pub code: String,
    pub message: String,
    pub offset: Option<u32>,
    pub step: Option<u16>,
}
impl Error {
    pub(crate) fn new(code: &str, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            code: format!("mori::core::{code}"),
            message: message.into(),
            offset: None,
            step: None,
        }
    }
}

//! Bounded JSON parsing that rejects duplicate keys before schema decoding.
use crate::Error;
use serde::de::{DeserializeSeed, MapAccess, SeqAccess, Visitor};
use serde_json::{Map, Value};
use std::{cell::Cell, fmt};
const MAX_BYTES: usize = 1_048_576;
struct Seed<'a> {
    depth: usize,
    nodes: &'a Cell<usize>,
}
impl<'de> DeserializeSeed<'de> for Seed<'_> {
    type Value = Value;
    fn deserialize<D: serde::Deserializer<'de>>(self, d: D) -> Result<Value, D::Error> {
        use serde::de::Error as _;
        self.nodes.set(self.nodes.get() + 1);
        if self.depth > 32 || self.nodes.get() > 65_536 {
            return Err(D::Error::custom("JSON work/depth limit"));
        }
        d.deserialize_any(self)
    }
}
impl<'de> Visitor<'de> for Seed<'_> {
    type Value = Value;
    fn expecting(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter) -> fmt::Result {
        f.write_str("bounded JSON")
    }
    fn visit_bool<E: serde::de::Error>(self, v: bool) -> Result<Value, E> {
        Ok(Value::Bool(v))
    }
    fn visit_unit<E: serde::de::Error>(self) -> Result<Value, E> {
        Ok(Value::Null)
    }
    fn visit_i64<E: serde::de::Error>(self, v: i64) -> Result<Value, E> {
        Ok(v.into())
    }
    fn visit_u64<E: serde::de::Error>(self, v: u64) -> Result<Value, E> {
        Ok(v.into())
    }
    fn visit_f64<E: serde::de::Error>(self, v: f64) -> Result<Value, E> {
        serde_json::Number::from_f64(v)
            .map(Value::Number)
            .ok_or_else(|| E::custom("nonfinite JSON number"))
    }
    fn visit_str<E: serde::de::Error>(self, v: &str) -> Result<Value, E> {
        Ok(v.into())
    }
    fn visit_string<E: serde::de::Error>(self, v: String) -> Result<Value, E> {
        Ok(v.into())
    }
    fn visit_seq<A: SeqAccess<'de>>(self, mut a: A) -> Result<Value, A::Error> {
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        while let Some(v) = a.next_element_seed(Seed {
            depth: self.depth + 1,
            nodes: self.nodes,
        })? {
            out.push(v)
        }
        Ok(Value::Array(out))
    }
    fn visit_map<A: MapAccess<'de>>(self, mut a: A) -> Result<Value, A::Error> {
        use serde::de::Error as _;
        let mut out = Map::new();
        while let Some(k) = a.next_key::<String>()? {
            if out.contains_key(&k) {
                return Err(A::Error::custom("duplicate decoded key"));
            }
            let v = a.next_value_seed(Seed {
                depth: self.depth + 1,
                nodes: self.nodes,
            })?;
            out.insert(k, v);
        }
        Ok(Value::Object(out))
    }
}
/// Strict recursive JSON value decoding for versioned DTO dynamic fields.
/// Duplicate decoded keys and the existing depth/node bounds apply even when
/// a consumer invokes serde directly instead of the bounded text entry point.
pub fn deserialize_strict<'de, D: serde::Deserializer<'de>>(decoder: D) -> Result<Value, D::Error> {
    let nodes = Cell::new(0);
    Seed {
        depth: 0,
        nodes: &nodes,
    }
    .deserialize(decoder)
}
pub fn parse(text: &str) -> Result<Value, Error> {
    parse_inner(text, MAX_BYTES, "JSON exceeds 1 MiB")
}
/// The same strict parser under a caller-selected byte ceiling. Duplicate-key,
/// depth (32) and node (65,536) limits are unchanged; `/2` stage files use it.
pub fn parse_limited(text: &str, max: usize, too_large: &'static str) -> Result<Value, Error> {
    parse_inner(text, max, too_large)
}
fn parse_inner(text: &str, max: usize, too_large: &'static str) -> Result<Value, Error> {
    use serde::de::DeserializeSeed;
    if text.len() > max {
        return Err(Error::new("json", too_large));
    }
    let nodes = Cell::new(0);
    let mut decoder = serde_json::Deserializer::from_str(text);
    let value = Seed {
        depth: 0,
        nodes: &nodes,
    }
    .deserialize(&mut decoder)
    .map_err(|e| Error::new("json", e.to_string()))?;
    decoder
        .end()
        .map_err(|e| Error::new("json", e.to_string()))?;
    Ok(value)
}

/// ASCII-safe pretty JSON that fits the same public reader profile, including
/// the final newline added by CLI printing. Parsed values and signing bytes
/// are preserved; terminal-controlled Unicode remains visibly escaped.
pub fn export(value: &impl serde::Serialize) -> Result<String, Error> {
    let json =
        serde_json::to_string_pretty(value).map_err(|e| Error::new("json", e.to_string()))?;
    parse(&json)?;
    let mut display = String::with_capacity(json.len());
    for character in json.chars() {
        if character.is_ascii() && (!character.is_control() || character == '\n') {
            display.push(character);
        } else {
            for unit in character.encode_utf16(&mut [0; 2]) {
                use std::fmt::Write;
                write!(&mut display, "\\u{unit:04x}").expect("writing to String cannot fail");
            }
        }
        if display.len() >= MAX_BYTES {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "json",
                "Escaped JSON and final newline exceed 1 MiB",
            ));
        }
    }
    parse(&display)?;
    Ok(display)
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn duplicate_decoded_keys_and_trailing_data_reject() {
        assert!(parse(r#"{"x":1,"\u0078":2}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse("{} []").is_err());
        assert!(parse(r#"{"x":"1"}"#).is_ok());
    }
}

//! Local primitive interpretation cost, separate from execution and replay schemas.
//! Dust, service and external costs are unmeasured by this profile.
use crate::policy::{Cost, Meter};
use crate::{DetailedReplayError, Error, Execution, Program, Scenario, UInt, compare_execution};
use serde::Serialize;

pub const LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE: &str = "moriarty-reference-logical-cost/1";
pub const LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE_2: &str = "moriarty-reference-logical-cost/2";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct CostReport {
    pub profile: String,
    pub cap: UInt,
    pub used: UInt,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct MeteredExecution {
    pub execution: Execution,
    pub cost: CostReport,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
#[error("{error}")]
pub struct MeteredExecutionError {
    pub error: Error,
    pub cost: CostReport,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
#[error("{error}")]
pub struct MeteredReplayError {
    pub error: DetailedReplayError,
    pub cost: CostReport,
}
/// Executes with a separately trusted, unsigned local logical-work cap.
/// A refusal reports charged attempts and exposes no partial execution.
// The refusal carries its charged cost report by design.
#[allow(clippy::result_large_err)]
pub fn simulate_metered(
    program: &Program,
    action: &str,
    scenario: &Scenario,
    cap: UInt,
) -> Result<MeteredExecution, MeteredExecutionError> {
    let mut meter = Meter::new(Cost {
        logical: cap,
        ..Cost::default()
    });
    let result = crate::runtime::simulate_with_meter(program, action, scenario, Some(&mut meter));
    let cost = CostReport {
        profile: LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE.into(),
        cap,
        used: meter.used().logical,
    };
    match result {
        Ok(execution) => Ok(MeteredExecution { execution, cost }),
        Err(error) => Err(MeteredExecutionError { error, cost }),
    }
}
/// Reconstructs once under the trusted cap, then uses existing first-divergence
/// comparison. Comparison and serialization work are outside this cost profile.
// The refusal carries its charged cost report by design.
#[allow(clippy::result_large_err)]
pub fn replay_detailed_metered(
    program: &Program,
    action: &str,
    scenario: &Scenario,
    candidate: &Execution,
    cap: UInt,
) -> Result<CostReport, MeteredReplayError> {
    let metered =
        simulate_metered(program, action, scenario, cap).map_err(|refused| MeteredReplayError {
            error: DetailedReplayError::Execution(refused.error),
            cost: refused.cost,
        })?;
    compare_execution(&metered.execution, candidate).map_err(|difference| MeteredReplayError {
        error: DetailedReplayError::Divergence(difference),
        cost: metered.cost.clone(),
    })?;
    Ok(metered.cost)
}
