use crate::*;
use mori_lexer::{TokenIdx, TokenKind};

/// Parses the explicitly versioned core profile; beta grammar is untouched.
pub fn parse(source: &str) -> Result<Program, Error> {
    parse_profile(source, false)
}

/// Syntax-only parser; only stage::load admits the resulting source.
pub(crate) fn parse_v2(source: &str) -> Result<Program, Error> {
    parse_profile(source, true)
}

fn parse_profile(source: &str, staged: bool) -> Result<Program, Error> {
    let lexed = mori_lexer::lex(source).map_err(|e| Error::new("lex", e.to_string()))?;
    let tokens = (0..lexed.len())
        .map(|i| {
            let idx = TokenIdx::new(i);
            let span = lexed.span(idx, source);
            let raw = &source[span.start as usize..span.end as usize];
            let text = if lexed.kind(idx) == TokenKind::String {
                mori_lexer::decode_string(raw)
            } else {
                raw.to_owned()
            };
            Tok {
                text,
                kind: lexed.kind(idx),
                start: span.start,
            }
        })
        .collect();
    let mut parser = Parser {
        tokens,
        pos: 0,
        program: Program {
            language: if staged {
                crate::stage::LANGUAGE_2
            } else {
                LANGUAGE
            }
            .into(),
            semantics: if staged {
                crate::stage::SEMANTICS_2
            } else {
                SEMANTICS
            }
            .into(),
            name: String::new(),
            states: vec![],
            assets: vec![],
            expressions: vec![],
            actions: vec![],
        },
    };
    parser.expect("language")?;
    let profile = parser.string()?;
    let expected_language = if staged {
        crate::stage::LANGUAGE_2
    } else {
        LANGUAGE
    };
    if profile != expected_language {
        return Err(parser.error(
            "profile",
            format!("Expected language \"{expected_language}\"; found {profile:?}."),
        ));
    }
    parser.expect(";")?;
    parser.expect("agreement")?;
    parser.program.name = parser.word()?;
    parser.expect("{")?;
    while !parser.eat("}") {
        if parser.eat("state") {
            let name = parser.word()?;
            parser.expect(":")?;
            let ty = parser.ty()?;
            parser.expect("=")?;
            let initial = parser.literal()?;
            parser.expect(";")?;
            parser.program.states.push(StateDecl { name, ty, initial });
        } else if parser.eat("asset") {
            let name = parser.word()?;
            parser.expect("=")?;
            parser.expect("{")?;
            parser.expect("network")?;
            parser.expect(":")?;
            let network = parser.string()?;
            parser.expect(",")?;
            parser.expect("issuer")?;
            parser.expect(":")?;
            let issuer = parser.string()?;
            parser.expect(",")?;
            parser.expect("reference")?;
            parser.expect(":")?;
            let reference = parser.string()?;
            parser.expect(",")?;
            parser.expect("scale")?;
            parser.expect(":")?;
            let Value::UInt(UInt(scale)) = parser.literal()? else {
                return Err(parser.error("asset", "Asset scale must be an unsigned integer."));
            };
            let scale = u8::try_from(scale)
                .map_err(|_| parser.error("asset", "Asset scale must fit u8."))?;
            parser.eat(",");
            parser.expect("}")?;
            parser.expect(";")?;
            parser.program.assets.push(AssetDecl {
                name,
                identity: AssetId {
                    network,
                    issuer,
                    reference,
                    scale,
                },
            });
        } else if parser.eat("action") {
            let name = parser.word()?;
            parser.expect("(")?;
            let mut inputs = vec![];
            if !parser.eat(")") {
                loop {
                    let name = parser.word()?;
                    parser.expect(":")?;
                    let ty = parser.ty()?;
                    inputs.push(InputDecl { name, ty });
                    if parser.eat(")") {
                        break;
                    }
                    parser.expect(",")?;
                }
            }
            parser.expect("{")?;
            let mut statements = vec![];
            while !parser.eat("}") {
                if parser.eat("input") {
                    if !statements.is_empty() {
                        return Err(
                            parser.error("input", "Declare action inputs before statements.")
                        );
                    }
                    let name = parser.word()?;
                    parser.expect(":")?;
                    let ty = parser.ty()?;
                    parser.expect(";")?;
                    inputs.push(InputDecl { name, ty });
                    continue;
                }
                statements.push(parser.statement()?);
                if statements.len() > MAX_STATEMENTS {
                    return Err(parser.error("bound", "Action exceeds 1,024 statements."));
                }
            }
            parser.program.actions.push(Action {
                name,
                inputs,
                statements,
            });
        } else {
            return Err(parser.error("unsupported", "Expected state, asset or action declaration; loops and native calls are unsupported."));
        }
        if parser.program.states.len() + parser.program.assets.len() + parser.program.actions.len()
            > MAX_DECLARATIONS
        {
            return Err(parser.error("bound", "Program exceeds 256 declarations."));
        }
    }
    if parser.peek().kind != TokenKind::Eof {
        return Err(parser.error("syntax", "Unexpected content after agreement."));
    }
    if !staged {
        validate(&parser.program)?;
    }
    Ok(parser.program)
}
struct Tok {
    text: String,
    kind: TokenKind,
    start: u32,
}
struct Parser {
    tokens: Vec<Tok>,
    pos: usize,
    program: Program,
}
impl Parser {
    fn peek(&self) -> &Tok {
        &self.tokens[self.pos]
    }
    fn error(&self, code: &str, message: impl Into<String>) -> Error {
        let mut e = Error::new(code, message);
        e.offset = Some(self.peek().start);
        e
    }
    fn eat(&mut self, text: &str) -> bool {
        if self.peek().text == text && self.peek().kind != TokenKind::String {
            self.pos += 1;
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }
    fn expect(&mut self, text: &str) -> Result<(), Error> {
        if self.eat(text) {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(self.error(
                "syntax",
                format!("Expected {text:?}; found {:?}.", self.peek().text),
            ))
        }
    }
    fn word(&mut self) -> Result<String, Error> {
        if self.peek().kind.is_word() {
            let text = self.peek().text.clone();
            self.pos += 1;
            Ok(text)
        } else {
            Err(self.error("syntax", "Expected a name."))
        }
    }
    fn string(&mut self) -> Result<String, Error> {
        if self.peek().kind == TokenKind::String {
            let text = self.peek().text.clone();
            self.pos += 1;
            Ok(text)
        } else {
            Err(self.error("syntax", "Expected a quoted string."))
        }
    }
    fn ty(&mut self) -> Result<Type, Error> {
        match self.word()?.as_str() {
            "UInt" => Ok(Type::UInt),
            "Bool" => Ok(Type::Bool),
            _ => Err(self.error("type", "Supported types are UInt and Bool.")),
        }
    }
    fn literal(&mut self) -> Result<Value, Error> {
        if self.eat("true") {
            return Ok(Value::Bool(true));
        }
        if self.eat("false") {
            return Ok(Value::Bool(false));
        }
        if self.peek().kind != TokenKind::Number {
            return Err(self.error("syntax", "Expected an unsigned integer or boolean literal."));
        }
        let raw = self.peek().text.replace('_', "");
        let n = raw.parse::<u128>().map_err(|_| {
            self.error(
                "number",
                "UInt literal must fit u128; amounts use integral atomic units.",
            )
        })?;
        self.pos += 1;
        Ok(Value::UInt(UInt(n)))
    }
    fn push(&mut self, expr: Expr) -> Result<ExprId, Error> {
        if self.program.expressions.len() >= MAX_EXPRESSIONS {
            return Err(self.error("bound", "Program exceeds 8,192 expression nodes."));
        }
        let id = self.program.expressions.len() as u16;
        self.program.expressions.push(expr);
        Ok(id)
    }
    fn expr(&mut self, minimum: u8, depth: usize) -> Result<ExprId, Error> {
        if depth > 64 {
            return Err(self.error("bound", "Expression nesting exceeds 64."));
        }
        let mut lhs = if self.eat("(") {
            let e = self.expr(0, depth + 1)?;
            self.expect(")")?;
            e
        } else if self.eat("not") {
            let value = self.expr(6, depth + 1)?;
            self.push(Expr::Not { value })?
        } else if self.peek().kind == TokenKind::Number
            || self.peek().text == "true"
            || self.peek().text == "false"
        {
            let value = self.literal()?;
            self.push(Expr::Literal { value })?
        } else {
            let name = self.word()?;
            if matches!(name.as_str(), "liability_present" | "liability_amount") && self.eat("(") {
                let duty = self.string()?;
                self.expect(")")?;
                self.push(if name == "liability_present" {
                    Expr::LiabilityPresent { name: duty }
                } else {
                    Expr::LiabilityAmount { name: duty }
                })?
            } else {
                self.push(Expr::Name { name })?
            }
        };
        loop {
            // A decoded string spelling an operator is still a string token.
            if self.peek().kind == TokenKind::String {
                break;
            }
            let next = self
                .tokens
                .get(self.pos + 1)
                .filter(|t| t.kind != TokenKind::String)
                .map(|t| t.text.as_str());
            let (op, precedence, length) = match (self.peek().text.as_str(), next) {
                ("or", _) => (BinaryOp::Or, 1, 1),
                ("and", _) => (BinaryOp::And, 2, 1),
                ("=", Some("=")) => (BinaryOp::Eq, 3, 2),
                ("not", Some("=")) => (BinaryOp::Ne, 3, 2),
                ("<", Some("=")) => (BinaryOp::Le, 3, 2),
                (">", Some("=")) => (BinaryOp::Ge, 3, 2),
                ("<", _) => (BinaryOp::Lt, 3, 1),
                (">", _) => (BinaryOp::Gt, 3, 1),
                ("+", _) => (BinaryOp::Add, 4, 1),
                ("-", _) => (BinaryOp::Sub, 4, 1),
                ("*", _) => (BinaryOp::Mul, 5, 1),
                _ => break,
            };
            if precedence < minimum {
                break;
            }
            self.pos += length;
            let rhs = self.expr(precedence + 1, depth + 1)?;
            lhs = self.push(Expr::Binary { op, lhs, rhs })?;
        }
        Ok(lhs)
    }
    fn statement(&mut self) -> Result<Statement, Error> {
        let keyword = self.word()?;
        let s=match keyword.as_str() {
            "await" if self.program.language == crate::stage::LANGUAGE_2 => {
                let label = self.word()?;
                self.expect("(")?;
                let mut binders = Vec::new();
                if !self.eat(")") {
                    loop {
                        let name = self.word()?;
                        self.expect(":")?;
                        let ty = self.ty()?;
                        binders.push(InputDecl { name, ty });
                        if self.eat(")") { break; }
                        self.expect(",")?;
                    }
                }
                self.expect("when")?;
                Statement::Await { label, binders, guard: self.expr(0, 0)? }
            },
            "let"=>{let name=self.word()?;self.expect("=")?;Statement::Let{name,value:self.expr(0,0)?}},
            "next"=>{let state=self.word()?;self.expect("=")?;Statement::Next{state,value:self.expr(0,0)?}},
            "requires"=>Statement::Requires{predicate:self.expr(0,0)?},
            "ensures"=>Statement::Ensures{predicate:self.expr(0,0)?},
            "emit"=>{let name=self.string()?;self.expect("(")?;let value=self.expr(0,0)?;self.expect(")")?;Statement::Emit{name,value}},
            "transfer"|"fee"=>{self.expect("(")?;let asset=self.word()?;self.expect(",")?;let from=self.string()?;self.expect(",")?;let to=self.string()?;self.expect(",")?;let amount=self.expr(0,0)?;self.expect(")")?;if keyword=="fee"{Statement::Fee{asset,from,to,amount}}else{Statement::Transfer{asset,from,to,amount}}},
            "liability"=>{self.expect("(")?;let name=self.string()?;self.expect(",")?;let asset=self.word()?;self.expect(",")?;let debtor=self.string()?;self.expect(",")?;let creditor=self.string()?;self.expect(",")?;let amount=self.expr(0,0)?;self.expect(")")?;Statement::Liability{name,asset,debtor,creditor,amount}},
            "discharge"=>{self.expect("(")?;let name=self.string()?;self.expect(",")?;let amount=self.expr(0,0)?;self.expect(")")?;Statement::Discharge{name,amount}},
            _=>return Err(self.error("unsupported",format!("Unsupported statement {keyword:?}; use let, requires, ensures, next, emit, transfer, fee, liability or discharge."))),
        };
        self.expect(";")?;
        Ok(s)
    }
}

/// Read the explicit source profile for CLI routing; this does not admit a program.
pub fn language(source: &str) -> Result<&'static str, Error> {
    let lexed = mori_lexer::lex(source).map_err(|e| Error::new("lex", e.to_string()))?;
    let mut tokens = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..lexed.len() {
        let idx = TokenIdx::new(i);
        let span = lexed.span(idx, source);
        let raw = &source[span.start as usize..span.end as usize];
        let kind = lexed.kind(idx);
        tokens.push(Tok {
            text: if kind == TokenKind::String {
                mori_lexer::decode_string(raw)
            } else {
                raw.to_owned()
            },
            kind,
            start: span.start,
        });
    }
    let mut parser = Parser {
        tokens,
        pos: 0,
        program: Program {
            language: LANGUAGE.into(),
            semantics: SEMANTICS.into(),
            name: String::new(),
            states: vec![],
            assets: vec![],
            expressions: vec![],
            actions: vec![],
        },
    };
    let expected = format!(
        "language \"{LANGUAGE}\" or \"{}\"",
        crate::stage::LANGUAGE_2
    );
    parser.expect("language").map_err(|mut error| {
        error.message = format!("Expected {expected}. {}", error.message);
        error
    })?;
    let profile = parser.string().map_err(|mut error| {
        error.message = format!("Expected {expected}. {}", error.message);
        error
    })?;
    match profile.as_str() {
        LANGUAGE => Ok(LANGUAGE),
        crate::stage::LANGUAGE_2 => Ok(crate::stage::LANGUAGE_2),
        _ => Err(parser.error(
            "profile",
            format!("Expected {expected}; found {profile:?}."),
        )),
    }
}

//! Deterministic cost accounting and scoped local authority.
//! Authentication of grants and settlement observations belongs to the caller.
use crate::{Error, UInt};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Cost {
    pub logical: UInt,
    pub dust: UInt,
    pub service: UInt,
    pub external: UInt,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CostPlane {
    Logical,
    Dust,
    Service,
    External,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Meter {
    caps: Cost,
    used: Cost,
}
impl Meter {
    pub fn new(caps: Cost) -> Self {
        Self {
            caps,
            used: Cost::default(),
        }
    }
    pub fn used(&self) -> Cost {
        self.used
    }
    pub fn charge(&mut self, plane: CostPlane, amount: UInt) -> Result<(), Error> {
        let (used, cap) = match plane {
            CostPlane::Logical => (&mut self.used.logical, self.caps.logical),
            CostPlane::Dust => (&mut self.used.dust, self.caps.dust),
            CostPlane::Service => (&mut self.used.service, self.caps.service),
            CostPlane::External => (&mut self.used.external, self.caps.external),
        };
        let next = used
            .0
            .checked_add(amount.0)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("meter", "cost counter overflow"))?;
        if next > cap.0 {
            return Err(Error::new("meter", "cost plane cap exceeded"));
        }
        *used = UInt(next);
        Ok(())
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Reservation {
    Reserved(UInt),
    Unknown(UInt),
    Settled(UInt),
    Released,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct FeeCapability {
    id: String,
    network: String,
    anchor: String,
    program: String,
    action: String,
    beneficiary: String,
    cap: UInt,
    spent: UInt,
    reservations: BTreeMap<String, Reservation>,
}
impl FeeCapability {
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn new(
        id: &str,
        network: &str,
        anchor: &str,
        program: &str,
        action: &str,
        beneficiary: &str,
        cap: UInt,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: id.into(),
            network: network.into(),
            anchor: anchor.into(),
            program: program.into(),
            action: action.into(),
            beneficiary: beneficiary.into(),
            cap,
            spent: UInt(0),
            reservations: BTreeMap::new(),
        }
    }
    pub fn spent(&self) -> UInt {
        self.spent
    }
    pub fn available(&self) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
        let pending = self
            .reservations
            .values()
            .try_fold(0_u128, |total, r| match r {
                Reservation::Reserved(a) | Reservation::Unknown(a) => total.checked_add(a.0),
                _ => Some(total),
            })
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("sponsor", "reserved capacity overflow"))?;
        self.cap
            .0
            .checked_sub(self.spent.0)
            .and_then(|n| n.checked_sub(pending))
            .map(UInt)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("sponsor", "invalid capacity accounting"))
    }
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn reserve(
        &mut self,
        id: &str,
        network: &str,
        anchor: &str,
        program: &str,
        action: &str,
        beneficiary: &str,
        amount: UInt,
    ) -> Result<(), Error> {
        if [
            &self.id,
            &self.network,
            &self.anchor,
            &self.program,
            &self.action,
            &self.beneficiary,
        ]
        .iter()
        .any(|s| s.is_empty())
            || id.is_empty()
            || (network, anchor, program, action, beneficiary)
                != (
                    self.network.as_str(),
                    self.anchor.as_str(),
                    self.program.as_str(),
                    self.action.as_str(),
                    self.beneficiary.as_str(),
                )
        {
            return Err(Error::new("sponsor", "fee capability scope mismatch"));
        }
        if self.reservations.contains_key(id) {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "sponsor",
                "reservation identity already consumed",
            ));
        }
        if amount.0 > self.available()?.0 {
            return Err(Error::new("sponsor", "insufficient sponsor capacity"));
        }
        self.reservations
            .insert(id.into(), Reservation::Reserved(amount));
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn mark_unknown(&mut self, id: &str) -> Result<(), Error> {
        match self.reservations.get_mut(id) {
            Some(r @ Reservation::Reserved(_)) => {
                let Reservation::Reserved(a) = *r else {
                    unreachable!()
                };
                *r = Reservation::Unknown(a);
                Ok(())
            }
            _ => Err(Error::new(
                "sponsor",
                "reservation is not pending submission",
            )),
        }
    }
    /// Release a reservation that has never been submitted. Timeout is insufficient.
    pub fn release_unused(&mut self, id: &str) -> Result<(), Error> {
        match self.reservations.get_mut(id) {
            Some(r @ Reservation::Reserved(_)) => {
                *r = Reservation::Released;
                Ok(())
            }
            _ => Err(Error::new(
                "sponsor",
                "cannot release an unknown or finalized spend",
            )),
        }
    }
    /// The caller must authenticate a final settlement observation before reconciling.
    pub fn settle(&mut self, id: &str, actual: UInt) -> Result<(), Error> {
        let bound = match self.reservations.get(id) {
            Some(Reservation::Reserved(a) | Reservation::Unknown(a)) => *a,
            _ => return Err(Error::new("sponsor", "reservation is absent or finalized")),
        };
        if actual.0 > bound.0 {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "sponsor",
                "settled DUST exceeds reserved authority",
            ));
        }
        let spent = self
            .spent
            .0
            .checked_add(actual.0)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("sponsor", "settled spend overflow"))?;
        if spent > self.cap.0 {
            return Err(Error::new("sponsor", "sponsor cap exceeded"));
        }
        self.spent = UInt(spent);
        self.reservations
            .insert(id.into(), Reservation::Settled(actual));
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub enum Role {
    Clear,
    Prove,
    Relay,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct RoleGrant {
    pub members: BTreeSet<String>,
    pub quorum: u16,
    pub calls: BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct FederationPolicy {
    pub network: String,
    pub anchor: String,
    pub epoch: UInt,
    pub grants: BTreeMap<Role, RoleGrant>,
    pub revoked: BTreeSet<String>,
}
impl FederationPolicy {
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn authorize(
        &self,
        role: Role,
        members: &[String],
        epoch: UInt,
        network: &str,
        anchor: &str,
        program: &str,
        action: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), Error> {
        if epoch != self.epoch
            || network != self.network
            || anchor != self.anchor
            || network.is_empty()
            || anchor.is_empty()
        {
            return Err(Error::new("policy", "policy epoch or domain mismatch"));
        }
        let grant = self
            .grants
            .get(&role)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("policy", "role has no grant"))?;
        let unique: BTreeSet<_> = members.iter().collect();
        if grant.quorum == 0
            || usize::from(grant.quorum) > grant.members.len()
            || unique.len() != members.len()
            || unique.len() < usize::from(grant.quorum)
            || unique
                .iter()
                .any(|m| !grant.members.contains(*m) || self.revoked.contains(*m))
            || !grant.calls.contains(&(program.into(), action.into()))
        {
            return Err(Error::new("policy", "role, quorum or call scope denied"));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

use crate::policy::{CostPlane, Meter};
use crate::*;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

fn failure(code: &str, message: impl Into<String>) -> Error {
    Error::new(code, message)
}
pub(crate) fn identifier(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut bytes = name.bytes();
    matches!(bytes.next(), Some(b'A'..=b'Z' | b'a'..=b'z'))
        && name.len() <= 64
        && bytes.all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_')
        && !matches!(
            name,
            "language"
                | "agreement"
                | "state"
                | "asset"
                | "action"
                | "input"
                | "let"
                | "next"
                | "requires"
                | "ensures"
                | "emit"
                | "transfer"
                | "fee"
                | "liability"
                | "discharge"
                | "true"
                | "false"
                | "and"
                | "or"
                | "not"
                | "UInt"
                | "Bool"
        )
}
fn names<'a>(values: impl Iterator<Item = &'a str>, kind: &str) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
    for name in values {
        if !identifier(name) || !seen.insert(name) {
            return Err(failure(
                "duplicate",
                format!("Invalid or duplicate {kind} name {name:?}."),
            ));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(crate) fn expr_type(
    program: &Program,
    id: ExprId,
    env: &BTreeMap<String, Type>,
    depth: usize,
) -> Result<Type, Error> {
    type_cached(
        program,
        id,
        env,
        depth,
        &mut vec![None; program.expressions.len()],
    )
}
fn type_cached(
    program: &Program,
    id: ExprId,
    env: &BTreeMap<String, Type>,
    depth: usize,
    cache: &mut [Option<Type>],
) -> Result<Type, Error> {
    if depth > 64 {
        return Err(failure("bound", "Expression depth exceeds 64."));
    }
    let index = usize::from(id);
    let node = program
        .expressions
        .get(index)
        .ok_or_else(|| failure("expression", "Expression index out of bounds."))?;
    if let Some(ty) = cache[index] {
        return Ok(ty);
    }
    let result = match node {
        Expr::LiabilityPresent { .. } => Ok(Type::Bool),
        Expr::LiabilityAmount { .. } => Ok(Type::UInt),
        Expr::Literal { value } => Ok(value.ty()),
        Expr::Name { name } => env
            .get(name)
            .copied()
            .ok_or_else(|| failure("name", format!("Unknown or forward name {name:?}."))),
        Expr::Not { value } => {
            if type_cached(program, *value, env, depth + 1, cache)? != Type::Bool {
                return Err(failure("type", "not requires Bool."));
            }
            Ok(Type::Bool)
        }
        Expr::Binary { op, lhs, rhs } => {
            let a = type_cached(program, *lhs, env, depth + 1, cache)?;
            let b = type_cached(program, *rhs, env, depth + 1, cache)?;
            let expected = match op {
                BinaryOp::And | BinaryOp::Or => Type::Bool,
                _ => Type::UInt,
            };
            if a != b || (!matches!(op, BinaryOp::Eq | BinaryOp::Ne) && a != expected) {
                return Err(failure("type", "Binary operands have incompatible types."));
            }
            Ok(
                if matches!(op, BinaryOp::Add | BinaryOp::Sub | BinaryOp::Mul) {
                    Type::UInt
                } else {
                    Type::Bool
                },
            )
        }
    }?;
    cache[index] = Some(result);
    Ok(result)
}
/// Validates even manually constructed/deserialized IR before interpretation.
pub fn validate(program: &Program) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if program.language != LANGUAGE || program.semantics != SEMANTICS {
        return Err(failure(
            "profile",
            "Unsupported language or semantics identity.",
        ));
    }
    if !identifier(&program.name) {
        return Err(failure(
            "name",
            "Agreement name must be a non-reserved ASCII identifier of at most 64 bytes.",
        ));
    }
    if program.actions.is_empty() {
        return Err(failure("program", "Program needs at least one action."));
    }
    if program.states.len() + program.assets.len() + program.actions.len() > MAX_DECLARATIONS
        || program.expressions.len() > MAX_EXPRESSIONS
    {
        return Err(failure(
            "bound",
            "Program exceeds finite declaration/expression bounds.",
        ));
    }
    names(
        program
            .states
            .iter()
            .map(|s| s.name.as_str())
            .chain(program.assets.iter().map(|a| a.name.as_str())),
        "declaration",
    )?;
    names(program.actions.iter().map(|a| a.name.as_str()), "action")?;
    let mut identities = BTreeSet::new();
    for asset in &program.assets {
        let id = &asset.identity;
        if id.network.is_empty()
            || id.issuer.is_empty()
            || id.reference.is_empty()
            || id.network.len() > 1024
            || id.issuer.len() > 1024
            || id.reference.len() > 1024
            || id.scale > 38
            || !identities.insert(id)
        {
            return Err(failure(
                "asset",
                "Asset identities must be complete, unique and use scale 0..38.",
            ));
        }
        if program.assets.iter().any(|other| {
            other.identity.network == id.network
                && other.identity.issuer == id.issuer
                && other.identity.reference == id.reference
                && other.identity.scale != id.scale
        }) {
            return Err(failure(
                "asset",
                "Same asset identity cannot have conflicting scales.",
            ));
        }
    }
    let mut depths = Vec::with_capacity(program.expressions.len());
    for (i, e) in program.expressions.iter().enumerate() {
        let refs = match e {
            Expr::Binary { lhs, rhs, .. } => vec![*lhs, *rhs],
            Expr::Not { value } => vec![*value],
            _ => vec![],
        };
        let mut depth = 0;
        for r in refs {
            if usize::from(r) >= i {
                return Err(failure(
                    "expression",
                    "Expression references must point to earlier nodes; cycles are forbidden.",
                ));
            }
            depth = depth.max(depths[usize::from(r)] + 1);
        }
        if depth > 64 {
            return Err(failure("bound", "Expression depth exceeds 64."));
        }
        depths.push(depth);
    }
    let state_types: BTreeMap<_, _> = program
        .states
        .iter()
        .map(|s| (s.name.clone(), s.ty))
        .collect();
    for state in &program.states {
        if state.initial.ty() != state.ty {
            return Err(failure(
                "type",
                format!("Initial state {:?} has wrong type.", state.name),
            ));
        }
    }
    for action in &program.actions {
        if action.inputs.len() > MAX_DECLARATIONS || action.statements.len() > MAX_STATEMENTS {
            return Err(failure(
                "bound",
                "Action exceeds input or statement bounds.",
            ));
        }
        names(action.inputs.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()), "input")?;
        let mut env = state_types.clone();
        for input in &action.inputs {
            if env.insert(input.name.clone(), input.ty).is_some()
                || program.assets.iter().any(|a| a.name == input.name)
            {
                return Err(failure("duplicate", "Input cannot shadow a declaration."));
            }
        }
        for statement in &action.statements {
            match statement {
                // Same identity as `stage::require_core1`; only an in-memory
                // program can carry Await here, since `/1` JSON cannot.
                Statement::Await { .. } => {
                    return Err(failure("profile", "Await requires moriarty-core/2."));
                }
                Statement::Let { name, value } => {
                    let ty = expr_type(program, *value, &env, 0)?;
                    if !identifier(name)
                        || env.insert(name.clone(), ty).is_some()
                        || program.assets.iter().any(|a| a.name == *name)
                    {
                        return Err(failure("duplicate", "let cannot shadow an existing name."));
                    }
                }
                Statement::Requires { predicate } | Statement::Ensures { predicate } => {
                    if expr_type(program, *predicate, &env, 0)? != Type::Bool {
                        return Err(failure("type", "Predicate must have type Bool."));
                    }
                }
                Statement::Next { state, value } => {
                    let expected = state_types
                        .get(state)
                        .ok_or_else(|| failure("name", format!("Unknown state {state:?}.")))?;
                    if expr_type(program, *value, &env, 0)? != *expected {
                        return Err(failure("type", "State update has wrong type."));
                    }
                }
                Statement::Emit { name, value } => {
                    if name.is_empty() || name.len() > 1024 {
                        return Err(failure("name", "Event name must contain 1..1024 bytes."));
                    }
                    expr_type(program, *value, &env, 0)?;
                }
                Statement::Transfer {
                    asset,
                    from,
                    to,
                    amount,
                }
                | Statement::Fee {
                    asset,
                    from,
                    to,
                    amount,
                } => {
                    validate_asset(program, asset)?;
                    if from.is_empty() || to.is_empty() || from.len() > 1024 || to.len() > 1024 {
                        return Err(failure("account", "Account identities cannot be empty."));
                    }
                    require_uint(program, *amount, &env)?;
                }
                Statement::Liability {
                    name,
                    asset,
                    debtor,
                    creditor,
                    amount,
                } => {
                    validate_asset(program, asset)?;
                    if name.is_empty()
                        || debtor.is_empty()
                        || creditor.is_empty()
                        || name.len() > 1024
                        || debtor.len() > 1024
                        || creditor.len() > 1024
                    {
                        return Err(failure(
                            "liability",
                            "Liability identities cannot be empty.",
                        ));
                    }
                    require_uint(program, *amount, &env)?;
                }
                Statement::Discharge { name, amount } => {
                    if name.is_empty() || name.len() > 1024 {
                        return Err(failure(
                            "liability",
                            "Liability name must contain 1..1024 bytes.",
                        ));
                    }
                    require_uint(program, *amount, &env)?;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    validate_liability_reads(program)?;
    Ok(())
}
// New reads require a source-fixed identity. Legacy Core external duties and
// discharge-only programs keep their existing validation rules.
pub(crate) fn validate_liability_reads(p: &Program) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let mut reads = BTreeMap::new();
    for expr in &p.expressions {
        if let Expr::LiabilityPresent { name } | Expr::LiabilityAmount { name } = expr {
            if name.is_empty() || name.len() > 1024 {
                return Err(failure(
                    "liability",
                    "Liability read name must contain 1..1024 bytes.",
                ));
            }
            reads.insert(name.as_str(), None);
        }
    }
    for stmt in p.actions.iter().flat_map(|a| &a.statements) {
        if let Statement::Liability {
            name,
            asset,
            debtor,
            creditor,
            ..
        } = stmt
            && let Some(found) = reads.get_mut(name.as_str())
        {
            let identity = (
                validate_asset(p, asset)?,
                debtor.as_str(),
                creditor.as_str(),
            );
            if found.is_some_and(|previous| previous != identity) {
                return Err(failure(
                    "liability",
                    format!("Conflicting source liability identity for {name:?}."),
                ));
            }
            *found = Some(identity);
        }
    }
    for (name, identity) in reads {
        if identity.is_none() {
            return Err(failure(
                "liability",
                format!("Liability read {name:?} has no source-derived identity."),
            ));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn require_uint(
    p: &Program,
    id: ExprId,
    env: &BTreeMap<String, Type>,
) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if expr_type(p, id, env, 0)? != Type::UInt {
        Err(failure(
            "type",
            "Financial amount requires UInt atomic units.",
        ))
    } else {
        Ok(())
    }
}
pub(crate) fn validate_asset<'a>(p: &'a Program, name: &str) -> Result<&'a AssetId, Error> {
    p.assets
        .iter()
        .find(|a| a.name == name)
        .map(|a| &a.identity)
        .ok_or_else(|| failure("asset", format!("Unknown asset {name:?}.")))
}

fn evaluate(
    p: &Program,
    id: ExprId,
    env: &BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    snapshot: &Snapshot,
    meter: &mut Option<&mut Meter>,
) -> Result<Value, Error> {
    eval_cached(
        p,
        id,
        env,
        snapshot,
        &mut vec![None; p.expressions.len()],
        meter,
    )
}
fn charge_logical(meter: &mut Option<&mut Meter>) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if let Some(meter) = meter.as_deref_mut() {
        meter.charge(CostPlane::Logical, UInt(1))?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn eval_cached(
    p: &Program,
    id: ExprId,
    env: &BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    snapshot: &Snapshot,
    cache: &mut [Option<Value>],
    meter: &mut Option<&mut Meter>,
) -> Result<Value, Error> {
    let i = usize::from(id);
    if let Some(value) = &cache[i] {
        return Ok(value.clone());
    }
    // Charge the attempted node before its children or operation. Cache hits
    // above remain free, and evaluate creates a new cache for each statement.
    charge_logical(meter)?;
    let result = match &p.expressions[i] {
        Expr::LiabilityPresent { name } | Expr::LiabilityAmount { name } => {
            let row = snapshot
                .liabilities
                .binary_search_by(|l| l.name.as_str().cmp(name.as_str()))
                .ok()
                .map(|index| &snapshot.liabilities[index]);
            if matches!(&p.expressions[i], Expr::LiabilityPresent { .. }) {
                Value::Bool(row.is_some())
            } else {
                Value::UInt(row.map_or(UInt(0), |l| l.amount))
            }
        }
        Expr::Literal { value } => value.clone(),
        Expr::Name { name } => env
            .get(name)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| failure("name", format!("Unknown name {name:?}.")))?,
        Expr::Not { value } => match eval_cached(p, *value, env, snapshot, cache, meter)? {
            Value::Bool(b) => Value::Bool(!b),
            _ => return Err(failure("type", "not expects Bool.")),
        },
        Expr::Binary { op, lhs, rhs } => {
            let a = eval_cached(p, *lhs, env, snapshot, cache, meter)?;
            let b = eval_cached(p, *rhs, env, snapshot, cache, meter)?;
            match (op, a, b) {
                (BinaryOp::Add, Value::UInt(UInt(a)), Value::UInt(UInt(b))) => {
                    Value::UInt(UInt(a.checked_add(b).ok_or_else(|| {
                        failure("overflow", "UInt addition exceeds u128.")
                    })?))
                }
                (BinaryOp::Sub, Value::UInt(UInt(a)), Value::UInt(UInt(b))) => {
                    Value::UInt(UInt(a.checked_sub(b).ok_or_else(|| {
                        failure("underflow", "UInt subtraction is negative.")
                    })?))
                }
                (BinaryOp::Mul, Value::UInt(UInt(a)), Value::UInt(UInt(b))) => {
                    Value::UInt(UInt(a.checked_mul(b).ok_or_else(|| {
                        failure("overflow", "UInt multiplication exceeds u128.")
                    })?))
                }
                (BinaryOp::Eq, a, b) => Value::Bool(a == b),
                (BinaryOp::Ne, a, b) => Value::Bool(a != b),
                (BinaryOp::Lt, Value::UInt(a), Value::UInt(b)) => Value::Bool(a < b),
                (BinaryOp::Le, Value::UInt(a), Value::UInt(b)) => Value::Bool(a <= b),
                (BinaryOp::Gt, Value::UInt(a), Value::UInt(b)) => Value::Bool(a > b),
                (BinaryOp::Ge, Value::UInt(a), Value::UInt(b)) => Value::Bool(a >= b),
                (BinaryOp::And, Value::Bool(a), Value::Bool(b)) => Value::Bool(a && b),
                (BinaryOp::Or, Value::Bool(a), Value::Bool(b)) => Value::Bool(a || b),
                _ => return Err(failure("type", "Invalid operand types.")),
            }
        }
    };
    cache[i] = Some(result.clone());
    Ok(result)
}
fn amount(
    p: &Program,
    id: ExprId,
    env: &BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    snapshot: &Snapshot,
    meter: &mut Option<&mut Meter>,
) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    match evaluate(p, id, env, snapshot, meter)? {
        Value::UInt(v) => Ok(v),
        _ => Err(failure("type", "Expected UInt amount.")),
    }
}
// Core also supports incoming duties without a source declaration. Only names
// declared by Liability statements have a source-fixed identity; do not impose
// the backend's closed, sixteen-entry compiler profile on general Core input.
fn liability_identities(p: &Program) -> Result<BTreeMap<&str, (&AssetId, &str, &str)>, Error> {
    let mut identities = BTreeMap::new();
    for stmt in p.actions.iter().flat_map(|action| &action.statements) {
        if let Statement::Liability {
            name,
            asset,
            debtor,
            creditor,
            ..
        } = stmt
        {
            let identity = (
                validate_asset(p, asset)?,
                debtor.as_str(),
                creditor.as_str(),
            );
            if let Some(existing) = identities.insert(name.as_str(), identity)
                && existing != identity
            {
                return Err(failure(
                    "liability",
                    format!("Conflicting source liability identity for {name:?}."),
                ));
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(identities)
}
pub(crate) fn scenario_snapshot(p: &Program, a: &Action, s: &Scenario) -> Result<Snapshot, Error> {
    if s.balances.len() > 4096 || s.liabilities.len() > 1024 {
        return Err(failure(
            "bound",
            "Scenario exceeds 4,096 balances or 1,024 liabilities.",
        ));
    }
    let added_liabilities = a
        .statements
        .iter()
        .filter(|stmt| matches!(stmt, Statement::Liability { .. }))
        .count();
    let trace_records =
        p.states.len() + s.balances.len() + s.liabilities.len() + added_liabilities + 1;
    if trace_records.saturating_mul(a.statements.len() + 2) > MAX_TRACE_CELLS {
        return Err(failure(
            "bound",
            "Scenario and action exceed 65,536 trace snapshot records.",
        ));
    }
    if !s.state.is_empty() && s.state.len() != p.states.len() {
        return Err(failure(
            "state",
            "Provide either initial state {} or every declared state value.",
        ));
    }
    let mut state = BTreeMap::new();
    for d in &p.states {
        let value = if s.state.is_empty() {
            d.initial.clone()
        } else {
            s.state
                .get(&d.name)
                .ok_or_else(|| failure("state", format!("Missing state {:?}.", d.name)))?
                .clone()
        };
        if value.ty() != d.ty {
            return Err(failure(
                "state",
                format!("Wrong type for state {:?}.", d.name),
            ));
        }
        state.insert(d.name.clone(), value);
    }
    if s.inputs.len() != a.inputs.len() {
        return Err(failure(
            "input",
            "Scenario must provide exactly the selected action's inputs.",
        ));
    }
    for input in &a.inputs {
        if s.inputs.get(&input.name).map(Value::ty) != Some(input.ty) {
            return Err(failure(
                "input",
                format!("Missing or wrong type for input {:?}.", input.name),
            ));
        }
    }
    let ids: BTreeSet<_> = p.assets.iter().map(|a| a.identity.clone()).collect();
    let mut keys = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut balances = s.balances.clone();
    for b in &balances {
        if !ids.contains(&b.asset) {
            return Err(failure(
                "asset",
                format!(
                    "Undeclared exact asset identity for account {:?}.",
                    b.account
                ),
            ));
        }
        if b.account.is_empty()
            || b.account.len() > 1024
            || !keys.insert((b.asset.clone(), b.account.clone()))
        {
            return Err(failure(
                "balance",
                "Duplicate or invalid balance account/asset.",
            ));
        }
    }
    for stmt in &a.statements {
        if let Statement::Transfer {
            asset, from, to, ..
        }
        | Statement::Fee {
            asset, from, to, ..
        } = stmt
        {
            let id = validate_asset(p, asset)?;
            for account in [from, to] {
                if !keys.contains(&(id.clone(), account.clone())) {
                    return Err(failure(
                        "balance",
                        format!("Missing explicit balance for {account:?} and asset {asset:?}."),
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    balances.sort_by(|a, b| (&a.asset, &a.account).cmp(&(&b.asset, &b.account)));
    let identities = liability_identities(p)?;
    let mut liabilities = s.liabilities.clone();
    let mut names = BTreeSet::new();
    for l in &liabilities {
        if !ids.contains(&l.asset) {
            return Err(failure(
                "asset",
                "Liability uses undeclared exact asset identity.",
            ));
        }
        if l.name.is_empty()
            || l.name.len() > 1024
            || l.debtor.is_empty()
            || l.creditor.is_empty()
            || l.debtor.len() > 1024
            || l.creditor.len() > 1024
            || !names.insert(l.name.clone())
        {
            return Err(failure(
                "liability",
                "Invalid or duplicate liability identity.",
            ));
        }
        if let Some(identity) = identities.get(l.name.as_str())
            && *identity != (&l.asset, l.debtor.as_str(), l.creditor.as_str())
        {
            return Err(failure(
                "liability",
                format!("Existing liability identity differs for {:?}.", l.name),
            ));
        }
    }
    liabilities.sort_by(|a, b| a.name.cmp(&b.name));
    Ok(Snapshot {
        state,
        balances,
        liabilities,
    })
}
fn move_funds(
    snapshot: &mut Snapshot,
    id: &AssetId,
    from: &str,
    to: &str,
    amount: UInt,
) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let f = snapshot
        .balances
        .iter()
        .position(|b| b.asset == *id && b.account == from)
        .ok_or_else(|| failure("balance", "Missing source balance."))?;
    let t = snapshot
        .balances
        .iter()
        .position(|b| b.asset == *id && b.account == to)
        .ok_or_else(|| failure("balance", "Missing recipient balance."))?;
    let debit = snapshot.balances[f]
        .amount
        .0
        .checked_sub(amount.0)
        .ok_or_else(|| failure("balance", format!("Insufficient balance for {from:?}.")))?;
    if f != t {
        let credit = snapshot.balances[t]
            .amount
            .0
            .checked_add(amount.0)
            .ok_or_else(|| failure("overflow", "Recipient balance exceeds u128."))?;
        snapshot.balances[f].amount = UInt(debit);
        snapshot.balances[t].amount = UInt(credit);
    }
    Ok(())
}
/// Shared scalar statement execution for legacy actions and admitted segments.
fn execute_statement(
    program: &Program,
    stmt: &Statement,
    env: &mut BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    current: &mut Snapshot,
    meter: &mut Option<&mut Meter>,
    effects: &mut Vec<Effect>,
) -> Result<Option<Value>, Error> {
    charge_logical(meter)?;
    match stmt {
        Statement::Await { guard, .. } => {
            let v = evaluate(program, *guard, env, current, meter)?;
            Ok(Some(v))
        }
        Statement::Let { name, value } => {
            let v = evaluate(program, *value, env, current, meter)?;
            env.insert(name.clone(), v.clone());
            Ok(Some(v))
        }
        Statement::Requires { predicate } | Statement::Ensures { predicate } => {
            let v = evaluate(program, *predicate, env, current, meter)?;
            if v != Value::Bool(true) {
                return Err(failure(
                    if matches!(stmt, Statement::Requires { .. }) {
                        "requires"
                    } else {
                        "ensures"
                    },
                    "Action predicate evaluated to false.",
                ));
            }
            Ok(Some(v))
        }
        Statement::Next { state, value } => {
            let after = evaluate(program, *value, env, current, meter)?;
            let before = current.state[state].clone();
            current.state.insert(state.clone(), after.clone());
            env.insert(state.clone(), after.clone());
            effects.push(Effect::State {
                name: state.clone(),
                before,
                after: after.clone(),
            });
            Ok(Some(after))
        }
        Statement::Emit { name, value } => {
            let v = evaluate(program, *value, env, current, meter)?;
            effects.push(Effect::Emit {
                name: name.clone(),
                value: v.clone(),
            });
            Ok(Some(v))
        }
        Statement::Transfer {
            asset,
            from,
            to,
            amount: expr,
        }
        | Statement::Fee {
            asset,
            from,
            to,
            amount: expr,
        } => {
            let amount = amount(program, *expr, env, current, meter)?;
            let id = validate_asset(program, asset)?.clone();
            move_funds(current, &id, from, to, amount)?;
            effects.push(if matches!(stmt, Statement::Fee { .. }) {
                Effect::Fee {
                    asset: id,
                    from: from.clone(),
                    to: to.clone(),
                    amount,
                }
            } else {
                Effect::Transfer {
                    asset: id,
                    from: from.clone(),
                    to: to.clone(),
                    amount,
                }
            });
            Ok(Some(Value::UInt(amount)))
        }
        Statement::Liability {
            name,
            asset,
            debtor,
            creditor,
            amount: expr,
        } => {
            let amount = amount(program, *expr, env, current, meter)?;
            let id = validate_asset(program, asset)?.clone();
            let found = current.liabilities.iter().position(|l| l.name == *name);
            let before = if let Some(index) = found {
                let l = &current.liabilities[index];
                if l.asset != id || l.debtor != *debtor || l.creditor != *creditor {
                    return Err(failure("liability", "Existing liability identity differs."));
                }
                l.amount
            } else {
                UInt(0)
            };
            let after = UInt(
                before
                    .0
                    .checked_add(amount.0)
                    .ok_or_else(|| failure("overflow", "Liability exceeds u128."))?,
            );
            if let Some(index) = found {
                current.liabilities[index].amount = after;
            } else {
                current.liabilities.push(Liability {
                    name: name.clone(),
                    asset: id.clone(),
                    debtor: debtor.clone(),
                    creditor: creditor.clone(),
                    amount: after,
                });
                current.liabilities.sort_by(|a, b| a.name.cmp(&b.name));
            }
            effects.push(Effect::Liability {
                name: name.clone(),
                asset: id,
                debtor: debtor.clone(),
                creditor: creditor.clone(),
                before,
                after,
            });
            Ok(Some(Value::UInt(amount)))
        }
        Statement::Discharge { name, amount: expr } => {
            let amount = amount(program, *expr, env, current, meter)?;
            let l = current
                .liabilities
                .iter_mut()
                .find(|l| l.name == *name)
                .ok_or_else(|| {
                    failure("liability", format!("Unknown residual liability {name:?}."))
                })?;
            let before = l.amount;
            let after =
                UInt(before.0.checked_sub(amount.0).ok_or_else(|| {
                    failure("liability", "Discharge exceeds residual liability.")
                })?);
            l.amount = after;
            effects.push(Effect::Liability {
                name: name.clone(),
                asset: l.asset.clone(),
                debtor: l.debtor.clone(),
                creditor: l.creditor.clone(),
                before,
                after,
            });
            Ok(Some(Value::UInt(amount)))
        }
    }
}

/// Executes one finite action on a clone. A refusal exposes no committed effects.
pub fn simulate(program: &Program, action: &str, scenario: &Scenario) -> Result<Execution, Error> {
    simulate_with_meter(program, action, scenario, None)
}
pub(crate) fn simulate_with_meter(
    program: &Program,
    action: &str,
    scenario: &Scenario,
    mut meter: Option<&mut Meter>,
) -> Result<Execution, Error> {
    validate(program)?;
    let selected = program
        .actions
        .iter()
        .find(|a| a.name == action)
        .ok_or_else(|| failure("action", format!("Unknown action {action:?}.")))?;
    let pre = scenario_snapshot(program, selected, scenario)?;
    let mut current = pre.clone();
    let mut env = current.state.clone();
    env.extend(scenario.inputs.clone());
    let mut steps = vec![];
    let mut all_effects = vec![];
    for (i, stmt) in selected.statements.iter().enumerate() {
        let mut effects = vec![];
        let result = execute_statement(
            program,
            stmt,
            &mut env,
            &mut current,
            &mut meter,
            &mut effects,
        );
        let value = result.map_err(|mut e| {
            e.step = Some(i as u16);
            e
        })?;
        all_effects.extend(effects.clone());
        steps.push(Step {
            index: i as u16,
            statement: stmt.clone(),
            value,
            snapshot: current.clone(),
            effects,
        });
    }
    Ok(Execution {
        semantics: SEMANTICS.into(),
        action: action.into(),
        pre,
        post: current,
        steps,
        effects: all_effects,
    })
}
/// Environment seed supplied only by stage admission.
pub enum SegmentEntry<'a> {
    Start {
        inputs: &'a [crate::stage::NamedValue],
    },
    Resume {
        site: u16,
        captures: &'a [crate::stage::NamedValue],
        event: &'a [crate::stage::NamedValue],
    },
}

pub enum SegmentRun {
    NotAdvanced,
    Ran {
        execution: Box<Execution>,
        exit_site: u16,
        captures_out: Vec<crate::stage::NamedValue>,
    },
}

/// Runs an already-admitted finite source segment with the same scalar executor
/// as legacy simulation. The caller owns admission, publication and work policy.
pub(crate) fn run_segment(
    sp: &crate::stage::StagedProgram,
    action: usize,
    entry: SegmentEntry<'_>,
    boundary: Snapshot,
    meter: &mut Meter,
) -> Result<SegmentRun, Error> {
    let program = sp.program();
    let selected = &program.actions[action];
    let plan = &sp.plan().actions[action];
    let pre = boundary;
    let mut current = pre.clone();
    let mut env = pre.state.clone();
    let mut meter = Some(meter);
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    let mut all_effects = Vec::new();
    let begin = match entry {
        SegmentEntry::Start { inputs } => {
            env.extend(inputs.iter().map(|v| (v.name.clone(), v.value.clone())));
            0
        }
        SegmentEntry::Resume {
            site,
            captures,
            event,
        } => {
            env.extend(
                captures
                    .iter()
                    .chain(event)
                    .map(|v| (v.name.clone(), v.value.clone())),
            );
            let ordinal = usize::from(plan.sites[usize::from(site) - 1].ordinal);
            let statement = &selected.statements[ordinal];
            let mut effects = Vec::new();
            // execute_statement charges dispatch once, then visits the guard.
            let value = execute_statement(
                program,
                statement,
                &mut env,
                &mut current,
                &mut meter,
                &mut effects,
            )
            .map_err(|mut error| {
                error.step = Some(ordinal as u16);
                error
            })?;
            if value != Some(Value::Bool(true)) {
                return Ok(SegmentRun::NotAdvanced);
            }
            steps.push(Step {
                index: ordinal as u16,
                statement: statement.clone(),
                value,
                snapshot: pre.clone(),
                effects,
            });
            ordinal + 1
        }
    };
    let mut exit_site = 0;
    let mut captures_out = Vec::new();
    for (ordinal, statement) in selected.statements.iter().enumerate().skip(begin) {
        if matches!(statement, Statement::Await { .. }) {
            // Entry charge is attempted now; guard and its Step belong to resume.
            charge_logical(&mut meter).map_err(|mut error| {
                error.step = Some(ordinal as u16);
                error
            })?;
            let site = plan
                .sites
                .iter()
                .find(|s| usize::from(s.ordinal) == ordinal)
                .ok_or_else(|| failure("stage_site", "Await site is absent from stage plan."))?;
            exit_site = site.site;
            for slot in &site.captures {
                let value = env
                    .get(&slot.name)
                    .cloned()
                    .ok_or_else(|| failure("name", format!("Unknown name {:?}.", slot.name)))?;
                captures_out.push(crate::stage::NamedValue {
                    name: slot.name.clone(),
                    value,
                });
            }
            break;
        }
        let mut effects = Vec::new();
        let value = execute_statement(
            program,
            statement,
            &mut env,
            &mut current,
            &mut meter,
            &mut effects,
        )
        .map_err(|mut error| {
            error.step = Some(ordinal as u16);
            error
        })?;
        all_effects.extend(effects.clone());
        steps.push(Step {
            index: ordinal as u16,
            statement: statement.clone(),
            value,
            snapshot: current.clone(),
            effects,
        });
    }
    Ok(SegmentRun::Ran {
        execution: Box::new(Execution {
            semantics: program.semantics.clone(),
            action: selected.name.clone(),
            pre,
            post: current,
            steps,
            effects: all_effects,
        }),
        exit_site,
        captures_out,
    })
}

/// Recomputes all steps and rejects at the first deterministic divergence.
pub fn replay(
    program: &Program,
    action: &str,
    scenario: &Scenario,
    expected: &Execution,
) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let actual = simulate(program, action, scenario)?;
    if actual.semantics != expected.semantics
        || actual.action != expected.action
        || actual.pre != expected.pre
    {
        return Err(failure(
            "replay",
            "Execution identity or predecessor differs.",
        ));
    }
    for i in 0..actual.steps.len().max(expected.steps.len()) {
        if actual.steps.get(i) != expected.steps.get(i) {
            let mut e = failure("replay", format!("First divergence at step {i}."));
            e.step = Some(i as u16);
            return Err(e);
        }
    }
    if actual.effects != expected.effects || actual.post != expected.post {
        return Err(failure(
            "replay",
            "Complete effect sequence or poststate differs.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

//! Ordered local stage admission around the single shared evaluator.
//!
//! Admission is a pure function of its arguments. No consumption, nonce or
//! lock state exists here (P3): a copied continuation resumes again, and a
//! fabricated idle scenario starts again. Every rejection publishes no
//! segment, effects or continuation; the evaluator only ever sees a clone of
//! the boundary, so the caller's input stays valid for a corrected retry (C10).
use super::{
    CONTINUATION_FORMAT, Continuation, NamedValue, PendingFrame, STAGE_EVENT_FORMAT, StageEvent,
    StageLogical, StageOutcome, StageRejection, StageSegment, StagedProgram, cost, decode, digest,
    profile, residual,
};
use crate::runtime::{self, SegmentEntry, SegmentRun};
use crate::{Error, LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE_2, Scenario, UInt};

fn rejected(error: Error, attempted: UInt, committed: UInt) -> StageRejection {
    StageRejection {
        error,
        attempted,
        committed,
    }
}

/// Values must match the slot layout exactly in length, order, name and type.
fn layout_matches<'a, T: 'a>(
    values: &[NamedValue],
    slots: impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = &'a T>,
    shape: impl Fn(&'a T) -> (&'a str, crate::Type),
) -> bool {
    values.len() == slots.len()
        && values.iter().zip(slots).all(|(value, slot)| {
            let (name, ty) = shape(slot);
            value.name == name && value.value.ty() == ty
        })
}

/// A legitimate segment never exceeds its static cost, so a meter refusal
/// under that limit is the same H26 premise violation.
fn premise(error: Error) -> Error {
    if error.code == "mori::core::meter" {
        let mut violated = Error::new("stage_logical", "static cost premise violated");
        violated.step = error.step;
        violated
    } else {
        error
    }
}

/// H25: a Waiting continuation must decode back to itself through the actual
/// resume decoder and pass R7, or it is not emitted.
fn emit_checked(sp: &StagedProgram, action: usize, c: &Continuation) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let unresumable = || {
        Error::new(
            "bound",
            "Continuation exceeds the resume decoder limits or cannot be resumed.",
        )
    };
    let text = serde_json::to_string(c).map_err(|_| unresumable())?;
    if text.len() > decode::MAX_CONTINUATION_BYTES {
        return Err(unresumable());
    }
    let decoded = decode::continuation(&text).map_err(|_| unresumable())?;
    if decoded != *c {
        return Err(unresumable());
    }
    decode::canonical_boundary(sp, action, &decoded.captures, &decoded.boundary)
        .map_err(|_| unresumable())
}

/// An admitted segment: `entry` is its entry site (0 = start) and therefore
/// its segment index.
struct Admitted<'a> {
    sp: &'a StagedProgram,
    action: usize,
    entry: u16,
    /// Committed work reported on rejection: the unchanged input's value.
    committed: UInt,
    stored_cap: Option<UInt>,
    frame_pre: PendingFrame,
}

impl Admitted<'_> {
    fn finish(
        self,
        run: Result<SegmentRun, Error>,
        meter: &crate::policy::Meter,
        unchanged: Option<Continuation>,
    ) -> Result<StageOutcome, StageRejection> {
        let Admitted {
            sp,
            action: index,
            entry,
            committed: kept,
            stored_cap,
            frame_pre,
        } = self;
        let attempted = meter.used().logical;
        let fail = |error: Error| rejected(error, attempted, kept);
        let plan = &sp.plan().actions[index];
        let action = &sp.program().actions[index].name;
        let segment = usize::from(entry);
        match run.map_err(|error| fail(premise(error)))? {
            SegmentRun::NotAdvanced => {
                // Only a resume evaluates a guard; a false one changes nothing.
                let Some(c) = unchanged else {
                    return Err(fail(Error::new(
                        "stage_site",
                        "A start segment has no guard to observe.",
                    )));
                };
                let dispatch = cost::dispatch_cost(sp.program(), plan, entry).map_err(fail)?;
                cost::require_static(meter, dispatch).map_err(fail)?;
                emit_checked(sp, index, &c).map_err(fail)?;
                let owed = residual(&c.boundary);
                Ok(StageOutcome::Waiting {
                    advanced: false,
                    segment: None,
                    continuation: c,
                    attempted,
                    residual: owed,
                })
            }
            SegmentRun::Ran {
                execution,
                exit_site,
                captures_out,
            } => {
                let planned = *plan.segment_cost.get(segment).ok_or_else(|| {
                    fail(Error::new(
                        "stage_logical",
                        "Segment is absent from the stage plan.",
                    ))
                })?;
                cost::require_static(meter, planned).map_err(fail)?;
                let committed = cost::committed_after(plan, segment).map_err(fail)?;
                let expected_exit = if segment == plan.sites.len() {
                    0
                } else {
                    entry + 1
                };
                if exit_site != expected_exit {
                    return Err(fail(Error::new(
                        "stage_site",
                        "Segment exit differs from the stage plan.",
                    )));
                }
                let frame_post = PendingFrame {
                    site: exit_site,
                    captures: captures_out,
                };
                if exit_site == 0 {
                    if !frame_post.captures.is_empty() || committed != plan.plan_total {
                        return Err(fail(Error::new(
                            "stage_logical",
                            "Completed work differs from the action plan total.",
                        )));
                    }
                    let owed = residual(&execution.post);
                    return Ok(StageOutcome::Complete {
                        segment: StageSegment {
                            action: action.clone(),
                            frame_pre,
                            frame_post,
                            execution: *execution,
                        },
                        committed,
                        attempted,
                        residual: owed,
                    });
                }
                let site = &plan.sites[usize::from(exit_site) - 1];
                if !layout_matches(&frame_post.captures, site.captures.iter(), |s| {
                    (s.name.as_str(), s.ty)
                }) {
                    return Err(fail(Error::new(
                        "stage_capture",
                        "Segment captures differ from the next site layout.",
                    )));
                }
                if plan.committed_before.get(usize::from(exit_site)) != Some(&committed) {
                    return Err(fail(Error::new(
                        "stage_logical",
                        "Committed work differs from the stage plan.",
                    )));
                }
                let continuation = Continuation {
                    format: CONTINUATION_FORMAT.into(),
                    program_hash: digest::hex(&sp.program_hash()),
                    plan_hash: digest::hex(&sp.plan_hash()),
                    action: action.clone(),
                    site: exit_site,
                    captures: frame_post.captures.clone(),
                    logical: StageLogical {
                        profile: LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE_2.into(),
                        cap: stored_cap,
                        committed,
                    },
                    boundary: execution.post.clone(),
                };
                emit_checked(sp, index, &continuation).map_err(fail)?;
                let owed = residual(&execution.post);
                Ok(StageOutcome::Waiting {
                    advanced: true,
                    segment: Some(StageSegment {
                        action: action.clone(),
                        frame_pre,
                        frame_post,
                        execution: *execution,
                    }),
                    continuation,
                    attempted,
                    residual: owed,
                })
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Runs segment 0 of `action`. The scenario passes the existing
/// `scenario_snapshot`, then the plan-total cap preflight (C3, attempted 0).
/// The pre frame is locally assumed idle (P3); `cap` becomes the stored cap.
pub fn start(
    sp: &StagedProgram,
    action: &str,
    scenario: Scenario,
    cap: Option<UInt>,
) -> Result<StageOutcome, StageRejection> {
    let refuse = |error: Error| rejected(error, UInt(0), UInt(0));
    let index = sp
        .action_index(action)
        .ok_or_else(|| refuse(Error::new("action", format!("Unknown action {action:?}."))))?;
    let selected = &sp.program().actions[index];
    let plan = sp
        .plan()
        .actions
        .get(index)
        .filter(|plan| plan.action == action)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            refuse(Error::new(
                "stage_site",
                "Action is absent from the stage plan.",
            ))
        })?;
    let pre = runtime::scenario_snapshot(sp.program(), selected, &scenario).map_err(refuse)?;
    let limit = cost::start_preflight(plan, cap).map_err(refuse)?;
    let mut inputs = Vec::with_capacity(selected.inputs.len());
    for input in &selected.inputs {
        let value = scenario.inputs.get(&input.name).cloned().ok_or_else(|| {
            refuse(Error::new(
                "input",
                format!("Missing or wrong type for input {:?}.", input.name),
            ))
        })?;
        inputs.push(NamedValue {
            name: input.name.clone(),
            value,
        });
    }
    // A boundary that R7 would refuse must not start an awaiting action:
    // its continuation could never be resumed (H25).
    if !plan.sites.is_empty() {
        decode::canonical_boundary(sp, index, &inputs, &pre).map_err(refuse)?;
    }
    let mut meter = cost::segment_meter(limit);
    let run = runtime::run_segment(
        sp,
        index,
        SegmentEntry::Start { inputs: &inputs },
        pre,
        &mut meter,
    );
    Admitted {
        sp,
        action: index,
        entry: 0,
        committed: UInt(0),
        stored_cap: cap,
        frame_pre: PendingFrame::idle(),
    }
    .finish(run, &meter, None)
}

/// Resumes `c` with event `e`, applying R0 then R1-R7 in order. The first
/// failure rejects with attempted 0 and the input's committed value. A false
/// guard returns the input continuation unchanged, with its stored cap; the
/// CLI cap only checks this invocation.
pub fn resume(
    sp: &StagedProgram,
    c: Continuation,
    e: StageEvent,
    cli_action: Option<&str>,
    cli_cap: Option<UInt>,
) -> Result<StageOutcome, StageRejection> {
    // Echoes the unchanged input; it is unverified when R1-R6 refuse.
    let kept = c.logical.committed;
    let refuse = |code: &str, message: &str| rejected(Error::new(code, message), UInt(0), kept);
    let refused = |error: Error| rejected(error, UInt(0), kept);
    // R0: an explicitly selected action must be the continuation's action.
    if cli_action.is_some_and(|selected| selected != c.action) {
        return Err(refuse(
            "stage_site",
            "Selected action differs from the continuation action.",
        ));
    }
    // R1
    if c.format != CONTINUATION_FORMAT || c.logical.profile != LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE_2 {
        return Err(refuse(
            "stage_format",
            "Unsupported continuation format or logical profile.",
        ));
    }
    // R2
    profile::require_core2(sp.program()).map_err(refused)?;
    if !digest::matches(&c.program_hash, &sp.program_hash())
        || !digest::matches(&c.plan_hash, &sp.plan_hash())
    {
        return Err(refuse(
            "stage_program",
            "Continuation does not belong to this program and stage plan.",
        ));
    }
    // R3
    let Some(index) = sp.action_index(&c.action) else {
        return Err(refuse("stage_site", "Continuation action does not exist."));
    };
    let Some(plan) = sp
        .plan()
        .actions
        .get(index)
        .filter(|plan| plan.action == c.action)
    else {
        return Err(refuse(
            "stage_site",
            "Action is absent from the stage plan.",
        ));
    };
    if c.site == 0 || usize::from(c.site) > plan.sites.len() {
        return Err(refuse(
            "stage_site",
            "Continuation site is not a pending await of this action.",
        ));
    }
    let site = &plan.sites[usize::from(c.site) - 1];
    // R4
    if !layout_matches(&c.captures, site.captures.iter(), |s| {
        (s.name.as_str(), s.ty)
    }) {
        return Err(refuse(
            "stage_capture",
            "Captures differ from the pending site layout.",
        ));
    }
    // R5
    if e.format != STAGE_EVENT_FORMAT
        || e.action != c.action
        || e.label != site.label
        || !layout_matches(&e.values, site.binders.iter(), |b| (b.name.as_str(), b.ty))
    {
        return Err(refuse(
            "stage_event",
            "Event does not match the pending await.",
        ));
    }
    // R6
    let limit = cost::resume_preflight(plan, c.site, &c.logical, cli_cap).map_err(refused)?;
    // R7
    decode::canonical_boundary(sp, index, &c.captures, &c.boundary).map_err(refused)?;
    let mut meter = cost::segment_meter(limit);
    let run = runtime::run_segment(
        sp,
        index,
        SegmentEntry::Resume {
            site: c.site,
            captures: &c.captures,
            event: &e.values,
        },
        c.boundary.clone(),
        &mut meter,
    );
    Admitted {
        sp,
        action: index,
        entry: c.site,
        committed: kept,
        stored_cap: c.logical.cap,
        frame_pre: PendingFrame {
            site: c.site,
            captures: c.captures.clone(),
        },
    }
    .finish(run, &meter, Some(c))
}
