//! Static logical work of successful segments (C3).
//!
//! The evaluator charges one unit per statement dispatch and one per distinct
//! expression node it visits. Its cache is per statement, and `And`/`Or`
//! evaluate both operands, so a successful statement costs exactly
//! `1 + |nodes reachable from its root expression|`, independent of values.
//! Segment j runs from entry site j (0 = start) and stops at the next await,
//! whose entry charge of 1 is paid but whose guard belongs to segment j+1.
use super::{ActionPlan, StageLogical, StagePlan};
use crate::policy::{Cost, Meter};
use crate::{Error, Expr, ExprId, Program, Statement, UInt};

fn overflow() -> Error {
    Error::new("bound", "Stage logical plan exceeds u128.")
}

fn add(a: UInt, b: UInt) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    a.0.checked_add(b.0).map(UInt).ok_or_else(overflow)
}

/// Distinct expression nodes reachable from `root`: the per-statement cache
/// charges each shared DAG node once.
fn expression_nodes(program: &Program, root: ExprId) -> Result<u128, Error> {
    let mut seen = vec![false; program.expressions.len()];
    let mut pending = vec![root];
    let mut count = 0_u128;
    while let Some(id) = pending.pop() {
        let index = usize::from(id);
        let node = program
            .expressions
            .get(index)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("expression", "Expression index out of bounds."))?;
        if std::mem::replace(&mut seen[index], true) {
            continue;
        }
        count += 1;
        match node {
            Expr::Binary { lhs, rhs, .. } => pending.extend([*lhs, *rhs]),
            Expr::Not { value } => pending.push(*value),
            Expr::LiabilityPresent { .. }
            | Expr::LiabilityAmount { .. }
            | Expr::Literal { .. }
            | Expr::Name { .. } => {}
        }
    }
    Ok(count)
}

fn root(statement: &Statement) -> ExprId {
    match statement {
        Statement::Await { guard, .. } => *guard,
        Statement::Let { value, .. }
        | Statement::Next { value, .. }
        | Statement::Emit { value, .. } => *value,
        Statement::Requires { predicate } | Statement::Ensures { predicate } => *predicate,
        Statement::Transfer { amount, .. }
        | Statement::Fee { amount, .. }
        | Statement::Liability { amount, .. }
        | Statement::Discharge { amount, .. } => *amount,
    }
}

/// Successful cost of one statement. For an Await this is its resume dispatch
/// plus guard; reaching an Await at segment exit costs 1 instead.
pub(crate) fn statement_cost(program: &Program, statement: &Statement) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    let nodes = expression_nodes(program, root(statement))?;
    nodes.checked_add(1).map(UInt).ok_or_else(overflow)
}

fn segment_cost(
    program: &Program,
    action: usize,
    plan: &ActionPlan,
    segment: usize,
) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    let statements = &program
        .actions
        .get(action)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("program", "Stage plan invariant violated: action."))?
        .statements;
    let ordinal = |site: usize| -> Result<usize, Error> {
        plan.sites
            .get(site - 1)
            .map(|s| usize::from(s.ordinal))
            .filter(|ordinal| *ordinal < statements.len())
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("program", "Stage plan invariant violated: site."))
    };
    let n = plan.sites.len();
    let begin = if segment == 0 { 0 } else { ordinal(segment)? };
    let end = if segment == n {
        statements.len()
    } else {
        ordinal(segment + 1)?
    };
    if begin >= end && !(segment == 0 && begin == end) {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "program",
            "Stage plan invariant violated: ordinals.",
        ));
    }
    let mut total = UInt(0);
    for (offset, statement) in statements[begin..end].iter().enumerate() {
        // Only an advance's first statement is an Await (its dispatch).
        if matches!(statement, Statement::Await { .. }) && (segment == 0 || offset != 0) {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "program",
                "Stage plan invariant violated: unplanned await.",
            ));
        }
        total = add(total, statement_cost(program, statement)?)?;
    }
    if segment < n {
        total = add(total, UInt(1))?;
    }
    Ok(total)
}

/// Fills `segment_cost`, `committed_before` and `plan_total` for every action
/// of a Sol-built plan whose cost vectors are still empty.
pub(crate) fn fill(program: &Program, plan: &mut StagePlan) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if plan.actions.len() != program.actions.len() {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "program",
            "Stage plan invariant violated: action count.",
        ));
    }
    for (index, action) in plan.actions.iter_mut().enumerate() {
        if !action.segment_cost.is_empty()
            || !action.committed_before.is_empty()
            || action.plan_total != UInt(0)
        {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "program",
                "Stage plan invariant violated: costs are already filled.",
            ));
        }
        let mut costs = Vec::with_capacity(action.sites.len() + 1);
        let mut committed = Vec::with_capacity(action.sites.len() + 1);
        let mut total = UInt(0);
        for segment in 0..=action.sites.len() {
            let cost = segment_cost(program, index, action, segment)?;
            committed.push(total);
            total = add(total, cost)?;
            costs.push(cost);
        }
        action.segment_cost = costs;
        action.committed_before = committed;
        action.plan_total = total;
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Attempted work of a resume whose guard at `site` is false: one dispatch
/// plus the guard's distinct nodes. Committed work is unchanged by it.
pub(crate) fn dispatch_cost(
    program: &Program,
    plan: &ActionPlan,
    site: u16,
) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    let action = program
        .actions
        .iter()
        .find(|a| a.name == plan.action)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("stage_site", "Unknown staged action."))?;
    let statement = usize::from(site)
        .checked_sub(1)
        .and_then(|i| plan.sites.get(i))
        .and_then(|s| action.statements.get(usize::from(s.ordinal)))
        .filter(|s| matches!(s, Statement::Await { .. }))
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("stage_site", "Pending site is not an await of this action."))?;
    statement_cost(program, statement)
}

/// Start refusal before evaluation (C3). Without a cap there is no admission
/// check. Returns the meter limit of segment 0.
pub(crate) fn start_preflight(plan: &ActionPlan, cap: Option<UInt>) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    let first = *plan
        .segment_cost
        .first()
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("stage_logical", "Stage plan has no segment costs."))?;
    if let Some(cap) = cap
        && plan.plan_total > cap
    {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "stage_logical",
            "Action plan total exceeds the logical cap.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(first)
}

/// R6. Call only after R1-R5 admitted `site`. The CLI cap checks this
/// invocation only and can never raise the stored cap, which the output
/// continuation carries unchanged. Returns the meter limit of segment `site`.
pub(crate) fn resume_preflight(
    plan: &ActionPlan,
    site: u16,
    logical: &StageLogical,
    cli_cap: Option<UInt>,
) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    let refuse = |message: &str| Error::new("stage_logical", message);
    let index = usize::from(site);
    let (Some(before), Some(cost)) = (
        plan.committed_before.get(index),
        plan.segment_cost.get(index),
    ) else {
        return Err(refuse("Pending site has no planned segment."));
    };
    if index == 0 || logical.committed != *before {
        return Err(refuse("Committed work differs from the stage plan."));
    }
    let needed = logical
        .committed
        .0
        .checked_add(cost.0)
        .ok_or_else(|| refuse("Committed work overflows u128."))?;
    let effective = match (cli_cap, logical.cap) {
        (Some(cli), Some(stored)) if cli > stored => {
            return Err(refuse("Logical cap cannot exceed the stored cap."));
        }
        (Some(cli), _) => Some(cli),
        (None, stored) => stored,
    };
    if effective.is_some_and(|cap| needed > cap.0) {
        return Err(refuse("Next segment exceeds the logical cap."));
    }
    Ok(*cost)
}

pub(crate) fn segment_meter(limit: UInt) -> Meter {
    Meter::new(Cost {
        logical: limit,
        ..Cost::default()
    })
}

/// H26: the evaluator must have charged exactly the planned work.
pub(crate) fn require_static(meter: &Meter, expected: UInt) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if meter.used().logical != expected {
        return Err(Error::new("stage_logical", "static cost premise violated"));
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Committed work after segment `segment` succeeds.
pub(crate) fn committed_after(plan: &ActionPlan, segment: usize) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    match (
        plan.committed_before.get(segment),
        plan.segment_cost.get(segment),
    ) {
        (Some(before), Some(cost)) => add(*before, *cost),
        _ => Err(Error::new(
            "stage_logical",
            "Segment is absent from the stage plan.",
        )),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::policy::CostPlane;
    use crate::{Action, BinaryOp, InputDecl, Type};

    fn plan(costs: &[u128]) -> ActionPlan {
        let mut before = Vec::new();
        let mut total = 0_u128;
        for cost in costs {
            before.push(UInt(total));
            total = total.saturating_add(*cost);
        }
        ActionPlan {
            action: "a".into(),
            statement_count: 0,
            sites: Vec::new(),
            segment_cost: costs.iter().copied().map(UInt).collect(),
            committed_before: before,
            plan_total: UInt(total),
        }
    }
    fn logical(committed: u128, cap: Option<u128>) -> StageLogical {
        StageLogical {
            profile: crate::LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE_2.into(),
            cap: cap.map(UInt),
            committed: UInt(committed),
        }
    }
    fn code(result: Result<UInt, Error>) -> String {
        result.unwrap_err().code
    }

    #[test]
    fn shared_dag_nodes_are_charged_once_per_statement() {
        let program = Program {
            language: super::super::LANGUAGE_2.into(),
            semantics: super::super::SEMANTICS_2.into(),
            name: "D".into(),
            states: vec![],
            assets: vec![],
            expressions: vec![
                Expr::Name { name: "x".into() },
                Expr::Binary {
                    op: BinaryOp::Add,
                    lhs: 0,
                    rhs: 0,
                },
                Expr::Binary {
                    op: BinaryOp::Mul,
                    lhs: 1,
                    rhs: 1,
                },
            ],
            actions: vec![Action {
                name: "a".into(),
                inputs: vec![InputDecl {
                    name: "x".into(),
                    ty: Type::UInt,
                }],
                statements: vec![Statement::Let {
                    name: "y".into(),
                    value: 2,
                }],
            }],
        };
        let statement = &program.actions[0].statements[0];
        assert_eq!(statement_cost(&program, statement).unwrap(), UInt(4));
    }

    #[test]
    fn start_preflight_refuses_only_an_unaffordable_plan_total() {
        let p = plan(&[5, 6]);
        assert_eq!(start_preflight(&p, None).unwrap(), UInt(5));
        assert_eq!(start_preflight(&p, Some(UInt(11))).unwrap(), UInt(5));
        assert_eq!(
            code(start_preflight(&p, Some(UInt(10)))),
            "mori::core::stage_logical"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn resume_preflight_enforces_plan_committed_and_never_raises_the_stored_cap() {
        let p = plan(&[5, 6]);
        let run = |l: StageLogical, cli: Option<u128>| resume_preflight(&p, 1, &l, cli.map(UInt));
        assert_eq!(run(logical(5, None), None).unwrap(), UInt(6));
        assert_eq!(run(logical(5, Some(11)), None).unwrap(), UInt(6));
        assert_eq!(run(logical(5, Some(11)), Some(11)).unwrap(), UInt(6));
        assert_eq!(run(logical(5, None), Some(11)).unwrap(), UInt(6));
        for (l, cli) in [
            (logical(4, None), None),         // H8: committed below plan
            (logical(6, None), None),         // H8: committed above plan
            (logical(5, Some(11)), Some(12)), // H8: CLI cap above stored cap
            (logical(5, Some(11)), Some(10)), // H8: committed + segment > CLI cap
            (logical(5, Some(10)), None),     // committed + segment > stored cap
            (logical(5, None), Some(10)),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(code(run(l, cli)), "mori::core::stage_logical");
        }
        for site in [0, 2, u16::MAX] {
            assert_eq!(
                code(resume_preflight(&p, site, &logical(0, None), None)),
                "mori::core::stage_logical"
            );
        }
        let huge = ActionPlan {
            committed_before: vec![UInt(0), UInt(u128::MAX)],
            ..plan(&[u128::MAX, 1])
        };
        assert_eq!(
            code(resume_preflight(&huge, 1, &logical(u128::MAX, None), None)),
            "mori::core::stage_logical"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn h26_require_static_rejects_any_meter_difference() {
        let mut meter = segment_meter(UInt(6));
        for _ in 0..5 {
            meter.charge(CostPlane::Logical, UInt(1)).unwrap();
        }
        let error = require_static(&meter, UInt(6)).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.code, "mori::core::stage_logical");
        assert_eq!(error.message, "static cost premise violated");
        meter.charge(CostPlane::Logical, UInt(1)).unwrap();
        assert!(require_static(&meter, UInt(6)).is_ok());
        assert!(require_static(&meter, UInt(5)).is_err());
        // The planned limit is a hard ceiling: excess work cannot be metered.
        assert!(meter.charge(CostPlane::Logical, UInt(1)).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn committed_after_is_checked_prefix_plus_segment() {
        let p = plan(&[11, 15, 10]);
        assert_eq!(committed_after(&p, 0).unwrap(), UInt(11));
        assert_eq!(committed_after(&p, 1).unwrap(), UInt(26));
        assert_eq!(committed_after(&p, 2).unwrap(), UInt(36));
        assert!(committed_after(&p, 3).is_err());
        let huge = ActionPlan {
            committed_before: vec![UInt(0), UInt(u128::MAX)],
            ..plan(&[u128::MAX, 1])
        };
        assert_eq!(
            committed_after(&huge, 1).unwrap_err().code,
            "mori::core::bound"
        );
    }
}

//! Strict bounded decoding of local stage files and the R7 canonical boundary.
//!
//! Size limits apply to bytes actually read, before parsing. Parsing keeps the
//! legacy duplicate-key, depth and node limits; only the byte ceiling differs.
//! A resumed boundary is checked as written and is never re-sorted.
use super::{Continuation, NamedValue, StageEvent, StagedProgram};
use crate::{AssetId, Error, MAX_TRACE_CELLS, Program, Snapshot, Statement};
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use std::path::Path;

pub use super::{MAX_CONTINUATION_BYTES, MAX_EVENT_BYTES};

pub const CONTINUATION_TOO_LARGE: &str = "Continuation JSON exceeds 4 MiB";
pub const EVENT_TOO_LARGE: &str = "Stage event JSON exceeds 64 KiB";

/// Reads at most `max + 1` bytes, so the limit holds even if the file grows
/// after it is opened. Oversized or non-UTF-8 input fails with `json`.
pub fn read_bounded(path: &Path, max: usize, too_large: &'static str) -> Result<String, Error> {
    use std::io::Read;
    let file = std::fs::File::open(path)
        .map_err(|e| Error::new("io", format!("Cannot open stage input: {e}")))?;
    let limit = u64::try_from(max).unwrap_or(u64::MAX).saturating_add(1);
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    file.take(limit)
        .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
        .map_err(|e| Error::new("io", format!("Cannot read stage input: {e}")))?;
    if bytes.len() > max {
        return Err(Error::new("json", too_large));
    }
    String::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| Error::new("json", "Stage input is not UTF-8"))
}

fn strict<T: DeserializeOwned>(
    text: &str,
    max: usize,
    too_large: &'static str,
) -> Result<T, Error> {
    let value = crate::json::parse_limited(text, max, too_large)?;
    serde_json::from_value(value).map_err(|e| Error::new("json", e.to_string()))
}

/// Exactly one `Continuation`; unknown fields, a missing `cap` key and
/// non-canonical UInt strings are rejected by the type's derives.
pub fn continuation(text: &str) -> Result<Continuation, Error> {
    strict(text, MAX_CONTINUATION_BYTES, CONTINUATION_TOO_LARGE)
}

pub fn event(text: &str) -> Result<StageEvent, Error> {
    strict(text, MAX_EVENT_BYTES, EVENT_TOO_LARGE)
}

type Identity<'a> = (&'a AssetId, &'a str, &'a str);

/// Source-fixed liability identities over every action of the program.
fn registry(p: &Program) -> Result<BTreeMap<&str, Identity<'_>>, Error> {
    let mut identities = BTreeMap::new();
    for stmt in p.actions.iter().flat_map(|action| &action.statements) {
        if let Statement::Liability {
            name,
            asset,
            debtor,
            creditor,
            ..
        } = stmt
        {
            let id = asset_identity(p, asset)?;
            let identity = (id, debtor.as_str(), creditor.as_str());
            if identities
                .insert(name.as_str(), identity)
                .is_some_and(|existing| existing != identity)
            {
                return Err(Error::new(
                    "liability",
                    format!("Conflicting source liability identity for {name:?}."),
                ));
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(identities)
}

fn asset_identity<'a>(p: &'a Program, name: &str) -> Result<&'a AssetId, Error> {
    p.assets
        .iter()
        .find(|a| a.name == name)
        .map(|a| &a.identity)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("asset", format!("Unknown asset {name:?}.")))
}

/// R7: the legacy `scenario_snapshot` predicates applied to a resumed boundary
/// without normalisation. Codes and messages follow `scenario_snapshot`;
/// ordering failures are new `balance`/`liability` messages.
///
/// Balances keep the baseline predicate: every account the whole action
/// transfers from or to must be present, and other declared-asset accounts
/// (such as an untouched observer) are preserved, not refused.
pub fn canonical_boundary(
    sp: &StagedProgram,
    action: usize,
    captures: &[NamedValue],
    b: &Snapshot,
) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let p = sp.program();
    let (Some(a), Some(plan)) = (p.actions.get(action), sp.plan().actions.get(action)) else {
        return Err(Error::new("stage_site", "Unknown staged action."));
    };
    // (d) Snapshot has no inputs component (U-1); R4 alone binds captures.
    let _ = captures;
    if b.balances.len() > 4096 || b.liabilities.len() > 1024 {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "bound",
            "Scenario exceeds 4,096 balances or 1,024 liabilities.",
        ));
    }
    // (a) States: exactly the declared states, each of its declared type.
    if b.state.len() != p.states.len() {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "state",
            "Boundary must provide every declared state value and no other.",
        ));
    }
    for d in &p.states {
        let value = b
            .state
            .get(&d.name)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("state", format!("Missing state {:?}.", d.name)))?;
        if value.ty() != d.ty {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "state",
                format!("Wrong type for state {:?}.", d.name),
            ));
        }
    }
    // (b) Balances: declared assets, valid accounts, strictly ascending by
    // (asset, account), which also excludes duplicates.
    let ids: BTreeSet<&AssetId> = p.assets.iter().map(|a| &a.identity).collect();
    let mut previous: Option<(&AssetId, &str)> = None;
    for balance in &b.balances {
        if !ids.contains(&balance.asset) {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "asset",
                format!(
                    "Undeclared exact asset identity for account {:?}.",
                    balance.account
                ),
            ));
        }
        if balance.account.is_empty() || balance.account.len() > 1024 {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "balance",
                "Duplicate or invalid balance account/asset.",
            ));
        }
        let key = (&balance.asset, balance.account.as_str());
        if previous.is_some_and(|prior| prior >= key) {
            return Err(Error::new("balance", "Balances are not canonical"));
        }
        previous = Some(key);
    }
    for stmt in &a.statements {
        if let Statement::Transfer {
            asset, from, to, ..
        }
        | Statement::Fee {
            asset, from, to, ..
        } = stmt
        {
            let id = asset_identity(p, asset)?;
            for account in [from, to] {
                if b.balances
                    .binary_search_by(|x| {
                        (&x.asset, x.account.as_str()).cmp(&(id, account.as_str()))
                    })
                    .is_err()
                {
                    return Err(Error::new(
                        "balance",
                        format!("Missing explicit balance for {account:?} and asset {asset:?}."),
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    // (c) Liabilities: strictly ascending by name; every name is a source
    // liability of this program with exactly its registered identity.
    let registry = registry(p)?;
    let mut previous: Option<&str> = None;
    for l in &b.liabilities {
        if !ids.contains(&l.asset) {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "asset",
                "Liability uses undeclared exact asset identity.",
            ));
        }
        if l.name.is_empty()
            || l.name.len() > 1024
            || l.debtor.is_empty()
            || l.creditor.is_empty()
            || l.debtor.len() > 1024
            || l.creditor.len() > 1024
        {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "liability",
                "Invalid or duplicate liability identity.",
            ));
        }
        if previous.is_some_and(|prior| prior >= l.name.as_str()) {
            return Err(Error::new("liability", "Liabilities are not canonical"));
        }
        previous = Some(l.name.as_str());
        if registry.get(l.name.as_str())
            != Some(&(&l.asset, l.debtor.as_str(), l.creditor.as_str()))
        {
            return Err(Error::new(
                "liability",
                format!("Existing liability identity differs for {:?}.", l.name),
            ));
        }
    }
    // (e) Trace cells over the whole action, as at start.
    let added = a
        .statements
        .iter()
        .filter(|stmt| matches!(stmt, Statement::Liability { .. }))
        .count();
    let records = p.states.len() + b.balances.len() + b.liabilities.len() + added + 1;
    if records.saturating_mul(usize::from(plan.statement_count) + 2) > MAX_TRACE_CELLS {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "bound",
            "Scenario and action exceed 65,536 trace snapshot records.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

//! Canonical program and plan digests. Both hash `intent::canonical`, the
//! same encoding the backend uses for its `/3` program hash.
use super::StagePlan;
use crate::{Error, Program};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

fn digest<T: serde::Serialize>(value: &T) -> Result<[u8; 32], Error> {
    Ok(Sha256::digest(crate::intent::canonical(value)?).into())
}

pub(crate) fn hex(hash: &[u8; 32]) -> String {
    crate::intent::hex(hash)
}

/// Exact comparison against the canonical 64-character lowercase encoding.
pub(crate) fn matches(text: &str, hash: &[u8; 32]) -> bool {
    text == hex(hash)
}

pub(crate) fn program_hash(program: &Program) -> Result<[u8; 32], Error> {
    digest(program)
}

/// Hashes the costed plan, which already names its program hash and format.
pub(crate) fn plan_hash(plan: &StagePlan) -> Result<[u8; 32], Error> {
    let filled = plan.program_hash.len() == 64
        && plan
            .program_hash
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b));
    if !filled {
        return Err(Error::new(
            "program",
            "Stage plan invariant violated: program hash is not filled.",
        ));
    }
    digest(plan)
}

//! Local product of two staged actions with disjoint state and asset domains.
use super::{PendingFrame, StageEvent, StageOutcome, StageRejection, StagedProgram};
use crate::{AssetId, Effect, Scenario, Snapshot, Statement, UInt};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub const MAX_INVOCATIONS: usize = 256;

pub struct Member {
    pub program: StagedProgram,
    pub action: String,
    pub scenario: Scenario,
    pub cap: UInt,
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum Error {
    #[error("local disjoint product: {0}")]
    Shape(String),
    #[error("member {member}: {rejection}")]
    Branch {
        member: usize,
        rejection: StageRejection,
        /// Start may have attempted an earlier member before this refusal.
        attempted_before: Vec<UInt>,
        earlier_start_committed: Vec<UInt>,
        pair_attempted_before: Option<UInt>,
        pair_committed_before: Option<[UInt; 2]>,
    },
    #[error("local disjoint product {phase}: {message}")]
    AfterInvocation {
        phase: &'static str,
        message: String,
        member: Option<usize>,
        attempted: Vec<Option<UInt>>,
        local_committed: Vec<UInt>,
        pair_attempted_before: Option<UInt>,
        pair_committed_before: Option<[UInt; 2]>,
    },
}

impl Error {
    /// Complete diagnostic data; no product boundary, effects or continuation
    /// is published by an error. Original stage refusals retain their envelope.
    pub fn public_report(&self) -> Value {
        let detail = match self {
            Self::Shape(message) => json!({"kind":"before-invocation","message":message}),
            Self::Branch {
                member,
                rejection,
                attempted_before,
                earlier_start_committed,
                pair_attempted_before,
                pair_committed_before,
            } => json!({
                "kind":"stage-rejection", "member":member,
                "rejection":super::outcome_report(Err(rejection.clone())),
                "earlier_start_attempted":attempted_before,
                "earlier_start_local_committed":earlier_start_committed,
                "pair_attempted_before":pair_attempted_before,
                "pair_committed_before":pair_committed_before,
            }),
            Self::AfterInvocation {
                phase,
                message,
                member,
                attempted,
                local_committed,
                pair_attempted_before,
                pair_committed_before,
            } => json!({
                "kind":"composition-after-invocation", "phase":phase,
                "message":message, "member":member,
                "invocation_attempted":attempted,
                "candidate_local_committed":local_committed,
                "pair_attempted_before":pair_attempted_before,
                "pair_committed_before":pair_committed_before,
            }),
        };
        json!({
            "format":"moriarty-local-disjoint-error-draft/1", "status":"rejected",
            "qualification":"local-reference-stage", "authentication":"none",
            "consumption":"untracked", "detail":detail,
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Observation {
    member: usize,
    outcome: StageOutcome,
}

/// Opaque local state: member boundaries come only from actual stage outputs.
/// It has no decoder, authentication or consumption tracking.
pub struct Pair {
    programs: [StagedProgram; 2],
    caps: [UInt; 2],
    cap_total: UInt,
    attempted_total: UInt,
    opening: Snapshot,
    latest: [StageOutcome; 2],
    history: Vec<Observation>,
}

fn shape(message: &str) -> Error {
    Error::Shape(message.into())
}

fn sum(left: UInt, right: UInt) -> Result<UInt, Error> {
    left.0
        .checked_add(right.0)
        .map(UInt)
        .ok_or_else(|| shape("logical sum exceeds UInt"))
}

fn declared_domains(
    program: &StagedProgram,
) -> (BTreeSet<&str>, BTreeSet<&AssetId>, BTreeSet<&str>) {
    let program = program.program();
    let states = program
        .states
        .iter()
        .map(|state| state.name.as_str())
        .collect();
    let assets = program.assets.iter().map(|asset| &asset.identity).collect();
    let duties = program
        .actions
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|action| &action.statements)
        .filter_map(|stmt| match stmt {
            Statement::Liability { name, .. } => Some(name.as_str()),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect();
    (states, assets, duties)
}

fn disjoint(left: &StagedProgram, right: &StagedProgram) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let (left_state, left_assets, left_duties) = declared_domains(left);
    let (right_state, right_assets, right_duties) = declared_domains(right);
    if !left_state.is_disjoint(&right_state)
        || !left_assets.is_disjoint(&right_assets)
        || !left_duties.is_disjoint(&right_duties)
    {
        return Err(shape(
            "requires disjoint declared state names, exact assets and duty names",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn merge(left: &Snapshot, right: &Snapshot) -> Result<Snapshot, Error> {
    let mut state = left.state.clone();
    for (name, value) in &right.state {
        if state.insert(name.clone(), value.clone()).is_some() {
            return Err(shape("state domains overlap"));
        }
    }
    let mut balances = left.balances.clone();
    balances.extend(right.balances.iter().cloned());
    balances.sort_by(|a, b| (&a.asset, &a.account).cmp(&(&b.asset, &b.account)));
    if balances
        .windows(2)
        .any(|rows| rows[0].asset == rows[1].asset && rows[0].account == rows[1].account)
    {
        return Err(shape("balance domains overlap"));
    }
    let mut liabilities = left.liabilities.clone();
    liabilities.extend(right.liabilities.iter().cloned());
    liabilities.sort_by(|a, b| a.name.cmp(&b.name));
    if liabilities
        .windows(2)
        .any(|rows| rows[0].name == rows[1].name)
    {
        return Err(shape("duty domains overlap"));
    }
    Ok(Snapshot {
        state,
        balances,
        liabilities,
    })
}

fn boundary(outcome: &StageOutcome) -> &Snapshot {
    match outcome {
        StageOutcome::Waiting { continuation, .. } => &continuation.boundary,
        StageOutcome::Complete { segment, .. } => &segment.execution.post,
    }
}

fn start_pre(outcome: &StageOutcome) -> Result<&Snapshot, Error> {
    match outcome {
        StageOutcome::Waiting {
            segment: Some(segment),
            ..
        }
        | StageOutcome::Complete { segment, .. } => Ok(&segment.execution.pre),
        _ => Err(shape("start did not supply a committed segment")),
    }
}

fn attempted(outcome: &StageOutcome) -> UInt {
    match outcome {
        StageOutcome::Waiting { attempted, .. } | StageOutcome::Complete { attempted, .. } => {
            *attempted
        }
    }
}

fn committed(outcome: &StageOutcome) -> UInt {
    match outcome {
        StageOutcome::Waiting { continuation, .. } => continuation.logical.committed,
        StageOutcome::Complete { committed, .. } => *committed,
    }
}

fn frame(outcome: &StageOutcome) -> PendingFrame {
    match outcome {
        StageOutcome::Waiting { continuation, .. } => PendingFrame {
            site: continuation.site,
            captures: continuation.captures.clone(),
        },
        StageOutcome::Complete { segment, .. } => segment.frame_post.clone(),
    }
}

fn effects(outcome: &StageOutcome) -> &[Effect] {
    match outcome {
        StageOutcome::Waiting {
            segment: Some(segment),
            ..
        }
        | StageOutcome::Complete { segment, .. } => &segment.execution.effects,
        StageOutcome::Waiting { segment: None, .. } => &[],
    }
}

impl Pair {
    /// Start uses a declared left-then-right reference linearization. Neither
    /// member receives the other's boundary, effects, captures or inputs.
    pub fn start([left, right]: [Member; 2]) -> Result<Self, Error> {
        disjoint(&left.program, &right.program)?;
        let caps = [left.cap, right.cap];
        let cap_total = sum(caps[0], caps[1])?;
        let programs = [left.program, right.program];
        let first = super::start(&programs[0], &left.action, left.scenario, Some(caps[0]))
            .map_err(|rejection| Error::Branch {
                member: 0,
                rejection,
                attempted_before: vec![],
                earlier_start_committed: vec![],
                pair_attempted_before: None,
                pair_committed_before: None,
            })?;
        let second = super::start(&programs[1], &right.action, right.scenario, Some(caps[1]))
            .map_err(|rejection| Error::Branch {
                member: 1,
                rejection,
                attempted_before: vec![attempted(&first)],
                earlier_start_committed: vec![committed(&first)],
                pair_attempted_before: None,
                pair_committed_before: None,
            })?;
        let prepared = (|| {
            let attempted_total = sum(attempted(&first), attempted(&second))?;
            let opening = merge(start_pre(&first)?, start_pre(&second)?)?;
            merge(boundary(&first), boundary(&second))?;
            Ok((attempted_total, opening))
        })();
        let (attempted_total, opening) =
            prepared.map_err(|error: Error| Error::AfterInvocation {
                phase: "start",
                message: error.to_string(),
                member: None,
                attempted: vec![Some(attempted(&first)), Some(attempted(&second))],
                local_committed: vec![committed(&first), committed(&second)],
                pair_attempted_before: None,
                pair_committed_before: None,
            })?;
        let history = vec![
            Observation {
                member: 0,
                outcome: first.clone(),
            },
            Observation {
                member: 1,
                outcome: second.clone(),
            },
        ];
        Ok(Self {
            programs,
            caps,
            cap_total,
            attempted_total,
            opening,
            latest: [first, second],
            history,
        })
    }

    /// Resume one member from its own actual continuation. Publication replaces
    /// only that member after its stage call and complete frame merge succeed.
    pub fn resume(&mut self, member: usize, event: StageEvent) -> Result<(), Error> {
        if self.history.len() >= MAX_INVOCATIONS {
            return Err(shape("invocation limit reached"));
        }
        let Some(StageOutcome::Waiting { continuation, .. }) = self.latest.get(member) else {
            return Err(shape("member must identify a pending branch"));
        };
        let outcome = super::resume(
            &self.programs[member],
            continuation.clone(),
            event,
            Some(&continuation.action),
            None,
        )
        .map_err(|rejection| Error::Branch {
            member,
            rejection,
            attempted_before: vec![],
            earlier_start_committed: vec![],
            pair_attempted_before: Some(self.attempted_total),
            pair_committed_before: Some(self.latest.each_ref().map(committed)),
        })?;
        let mut latest = self.latest.clone();
        latest[member] = outcome.clone();
        let prepared = (|| {
            merge(boundary(&latest[0]), boundary(&latest[1]))?;
            sum(self.attempted_total, attempted(&outcome))
        })();
        let attempted_total = prepared.map_err(|error: Error| {
            let mut invocation_attempted = vec![None, None];
            invocation_attempted[member] = Some(attempted(&outcome));
            Error::AfterInvocation {
                phase: "resume",
                message: error.to_string(),
                member: Some(member),
                attempted: invocation_attempted,
                local_committed: latest.each_ref().map(committed).to_vec(),
                pair_attempted_before: Some(self.attempted_total),
                pair_committed_before: Some(self.latest.each_ref().map(committed)),
            }
        })?;
        self.latest = latest;
        self.attempted_total = attempted_total;
        self.history.push(Observation { member, outcome });
        Ok(())
    }

    /// The report contains the complete product frame and all original member
    /// envelopes. Effects retain invocation order, then source statement order.
    pub fn report(&self) -> Result<Value, Error> {
        let post = merge(boundary(&self.latest[0]), boundary(&self.latest[1]))?;
        let committed = [committed(&self.latest[0]), committed(&self.latest[1])];
        let committed_total = sum(committed[0], committed[1])?;

        let ordered_effects: Vec<_> = self
            .history
            .iter()
            .flat_map(|item| effects(&item.outcome))
            .collect();
        let history: Vec<_> = self.history.iter().map(|item| {
            json!({"member":item.member,"outcome":super::outcome_report(Ok(item.outcome.clone()))})
        }).collect();
        let members: Vec<_> = self
            .latest
            .iter()
            .map(|outcome| super::outcome_report(Ok(outcome.clone())))
            .collect();
        let frames = self.latest.each_ref().map(frame);
        let waiting = self
            .latest
            .iter()
            .any(|outcome| matches!(outcome, StageOutcome::Waiting { .. }));
        Ok(json!({
            "format":"moriarty-local-disjoint-draft/1",
            "status":if waiting { "waiting" } else { "complete" },
            "qualification":"local-reference-stage",
            "composition_qualification":"unadopted-local-disjoint-product",
            "authentication":"none", "consumption":"untracked",
            "ordering":{"start":[0,1],"effects":"invocation-then-source-order"},
            "pre":self.opening, "post":post, "frames":frames,
            "members":members, "history":history, "effects":ordered_effects,
            "logical":{"caps":self.caps,"cap_total":self.cap_total,
                "committed":committed,"committed_total":committed_total,
                "attempted_total":self.attempted_total},
            "residual":super::residual(&post),
        }))
    }
}

===== COMPLETE BODY current-source/crates/mori-core/src/stage/mod.rs SHA256 e3c4a463319056d0a32ac192c57b3f587584976e08974f4dec04c9e881f80f23 =====
//! Staged `moriarty/2` programs and their local continuation interface.
//!
//! Local and unsigned only. Event binders and captures are caller-chosen values
//! (no authentication), a continuation can be resumed any number of times (no
//! consumption tracking), and no stage authority exists. Outputs carry
//! `STAGE_LABELS` so these limits stay visible.
mod admit;
mod cost;
pub mod decode;
mod digest;
#[cfg(feature = "core2-disjoint-draft")]
pub mod disjoint;
mod plan;
mod profile;
mod report;
mod validate;

pub use crate::LOGICAL_COST_PROFILE_2;
pub use admit::{resume, start};
pub use plan::{ActionPlan, AwaitSite, CaptureSlot, Origin, StageBinder, StagePlan};
pub use profile::{require_core1, require_core2};
pub use report::outcome_report;

use crate::{Error, Execution, Liability, Program, Snapshot, UInt, Value};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const LANGUAGE_2: &str = "moriarty/2";
pub const SEMANTICS_2: &str = "moriarty-core/2";
pub const STAGE_PLAN_FORMAT: &str = "moriarty-stage-plan/1";
pub const CONTINUATION_FORMAT: &str = "moriarty-continuation/1";
pub const STAGE_EVENT_FORMAT: &str = "moriarty-stage-event/1";
pub const MAX_AWAITS_PER_ACTION: usize = 16;
pub const MAX_EVENT_BINDERS: usize = 16;
pub const MAX_CAPTURE_SLOTS: usize = 256;
pub const MAX_CONTINUATION_BYTES: usize = 4 * 1_048_576;
pub const MAX_EVENT_BYTES: usize = 65_536;
/// Inserted verbatim into every `/2` output object.
pub const STAGE_LABELS: [(&str, &str); 3] = [
    ("qualification", "local-reference-stage"),
    ("authentication", "none"),
    ("consumption", "untracked"),
];

/// A `/2` program that passed profile, validation, plan invariants and static
/// costing, with its canonical digests. Only `load` constructs one; there is
/// no public constructor, `Default` or `Deserialize`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct StagedProgram {
    program: Program,
    plan: StagePlan,
    program_hash: [u8; 32],
    plan_hash: [u8; 32],
}
impl StagedProgram {
    pub fn program(&self) -> &Program {
        &self.program
    }
    pub fn plan(&self) -> &StagePlan {
        &self.plan
    }
    pub fn program_hash(&self) -> [u8; 32] {
        self.program_hash
    }
    pub fn plan_hash(&self) -> [u8; 32] {
        self.plan_hash
    }
    /// Declaration index of an action; plan and program actions share it.
    pub(crate) fn action_index(&self, name: &str) -> Option<usize> {
        self.program.actions.iter().position(|a| a.name == name)
    }
}

/// Parses, admits and plans a `moriarty/2` source. Legacy `parse`/`validate`
/// remain `/1`-only, so a `/2` source fails every legacy consumer.
pub fn load(src: &str) -> Result<StagedProgram, Error> {
    let program = crate::parser::parse_v2(src)?;
    profile::require_core2(&program)?;
    validate::validate_v2(&program)?;
    let mut plan = plan::build(&program)?;
    validate::plan_invariants(&program, &plan)?;
    cost::fill(&program, &mut plan)?;
    validate::cost_invariants(&program, &plan)?;
    let program_hash = digest::program_hash(&program)?;
    plan.program_hash = digest::hex(&program_hash);
    let plan_hash = digest::plan_hash(&plan)?;
    Ok(StagedProgram {
        program,
        plan,
        program_hash,
        plan_hash,
    })
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct NamedValue {
    pub name: String,
    pub value: Value,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct StageLogical {
    pub profile: String,
    /// Mandatory key; `None` is written as `null`.
    #[serde(deserialize_with = "Option::deserialize")]
    pub cap: Option<UInt>,
    pub committed: UInt,
}
/// Resume input. `site` and `captures` are the executing action's pending
/// frame; `boundary` is the legacy Snapshot. Nothing here is authenticated.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Continuation {
    pub format: String,
    pub program_hash: String,
    pub plan_hash: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub site: u16,
    pub captures: Vec<NamedValue>,
    pub logical: StageLogical,
    pub boundary: Snapshot,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct StageEvent {
    pub format: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub label: String,
    pub values: Vec<NamedValue>,
}
/// Local frame of one action. `site == 0` exactly when `captures` is empty
/// and the action is idle; no padding is represented locally.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct PendingFrame {
    pub site: u16,
    pub captures: Vec<NamedValue>,
}
impl PendingFrame {
    pub(crate) fn idle() -> Self {
        Self {
            site: 0,
            captures: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}
/// Output only. Segment history is never decoded or trusted on resume, so no
/// `Deserialize` exists over the shared Await-bearing `Statement`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct StageSegment {
    pub action: String,
    pub frame_pre: PendingFrame,
    pub frame_post: PendingFrame,
    pub execution: Execution,
}
/// `residual` lists every post-boundary liability with a positive amount, in
/// the existing complete `Liability` representation.
// Public outcome; boxing `Waiting` would change every caller's pattern.
#[allow(clippy::large_enum_variant)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub enum StageOutcome {
    Waiting {
        advanced: bool,
        segment: Option<StageSegment>,
        continuation: Continuation,
        attempted: UInt,
        residual: Vec<Liability>,
    },
    Complete {
        segment: StageSegment,
        committed: UInt,
        attempted: UInt,
        residual: Vec<Liability>,
    },
}
/// A rejected invocation publishes no segment, effects or continuation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, thiserror::Error)]
#[error("{error}")]
pub struct StageRejection {
    pub error: Error,
    pub attempted: UInt,
    pub committed: UInt,
}

/// Positive residual duties of a boundary, in its (name-sorted) order.
pub(crate) fn residual(snapshot: &Snapshot) -> Vec<Liability> {
    snapshot
        .liabilities
        .iter()
        .filter(|l| l.amount.0 > 0)
        .cloned()
        .collect()
}

===== COMPLETE BODY current-source/crates/mori-core/src/stage/plan.rs SHA256 9d8e71b2d5089bf3221b3e06454038357559a9eddab8245464d55b19653d1e1c =====
//! Source-derived scalar layouts. Admission and work costs belong to sibling modules.
use crate::{Error, InputDecl, Program, Statement, Type, UInt};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Origin {
    Input,
    Let,
    Event,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct CaptureSlot {
    pub name: String,
    pub ty: Type,
    pub origin: Origin,
    pub ordinal: u16,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct StageBinder {
    pub name: String,
    pub ty: Type,
}
impl From<&InputDecl> for StageBinder {
    fn from(value: &InputDecl) -> Self {
        Self {
            name: value.name.clone(),
            ty: value.ty,
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct AwaitSite {
    pub site: u16,
    pub ordinal: u16,
    pub label: String,
    pub binders: Vec<StageBinder>,
    pub captures: Vec<CaptureSlot>,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ActionPlan {
    pub action: String,
    pub statement_count: u16,
    pub sites: Vec<AwaitSite>,
    pub segment_cost: Vec<UInt>,
    pub committed_before: Vec<UInt>,
    pub plan_total: UInt,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct StagePlan {
    pub format: String,
    pub program_hash: String,
    pub actions: Vec<ActionPlan>,
}

/// Builds layouts after validate_v2. Costs and hashes are filled by stage::load.
pub(crate) fn build(program: &Program) -> Result<StagePlan, Error> {
    let mut actions = Vec::new();
    for action in &program.actions {
        let mut types: BTreeMap<_, _> = program
            .states
            .iter()
            .map(|state| (state.name.clone(), state.ty))
            .collect();
        let mut captures = Vec::new();
        for (ordinal, input) in action.inputs.iter().enumerate() {
            types.insert(input.name.clone(), input.ty);
            captures.push(CaptureSlot {
                name: input.name.clone(),
                ty: input.ty,
                origin: Origin::Input,
                ordinal: ordinal as u16,
            });
        }
        let mut sites = Vec::new();
        for (ordinal, statement) in action.statements.iter().enumerate() {
            match statement {
                Statement::Let { name, value } => {
                    let ty = crate::runtime::expr_type(program, *value, &types, 0)?;
                    types.insert(name.clone(), ty);
                    captures.push(CaptureSlot {
                        name: name.clone(),
                        ty,
                        origin: Origin::Let,
                        ordinal: ordinal as u16,
                    });
                }
                Statement::Await { label, binders, .. } => {
                    sites.push(AwaitSite {
                        site: sites.len() as u16 + 1,
                        ordinal: ordinal as u16,
                        label: label.clone(),
                        binders: binders.iter().map(StageBinder::from).collect(),
                        captures: captures.clone(),
                    });
                    for binder in binders {
                        types.insert(binder.name.clone(), binder.ty);
                        captures.push(CaptureSlot {
                            name: binder.name.clone(),
                            ty: binder.ty,
                            origin: Origin::Event,
                            ordinal: ordinal as u16,
                        });
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        actions.push(ActionPlan {
            action: action.name.clone(),
            statement_count: action.statements.len() as u16,
            sites,
            segment_cost: Vec::new(),
            committed_before: Vec::new(),
            plan_total: UInt(0),
        });
    }
    Ok(StagePlan {
        format: "moriarty-stage-plan/1".into(),
        program_hash: String::new(),
        actions,
    })
}

//! Profile isolation between `moriarty-core/1` and `moriarty-core/2` consumers.
//! Both helpers keep the existing `mori::core::profile` error identity.
use super::{LANGUAGE_2, SEMANTICS_2};
use crate::{Error, LANGUAGE, Program, SEMANTICS, Statement};

const PROFILE_MESSAGE: &str = "Unsupported language or semantics identity.";

/// Required at every `/1` entry point before evaluation or layout. An
/// in-memory program labelled `/1` that still carries an Await is refused.
pub fn require_core1(program: &Program) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if program.language != LANGUAGE || program.semantics != SEMANTICS {
        return Err(Error::new("profile", PROFILE_MESSAGE));
    }
    if program
        .actions
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|action| &action.statements)
        .any(|statement| matches!(statement, Statement::Await { .. }))
    {
        return Err(Error::new("profile", "Await requires moriarty-core/2."));
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Required at every staged entry point; `/1` programs are refused.
pub fn require_core2(program: &Program) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if program.language != LANGUAGE_2 || program.semantics != SEMANTICS_2 {
        return Err(Error::new("profile", PROFILE_MESSAGE));
    }
    Ok(())
}

//! Complete qualified JSON envelopes for the local Core/2 outcome API.
use super::{STAGE_LABELS, StageOutcome, StageRejection};
use serde_json::{Value, json};

/// Render an outcome or rejection without executing a program or performing I/O.
/// Every envelope retains the explicit local-stage qualifications.
pub fn outcome_report(result: Result<StageOutcome, StageRejection>) -> Value {
    let mut report = match result {
        Ok(StageOutcome::Waiting {
            advanced,
            segment,
            continuation,
            attempted,
            residual,
        }) => {
            let committed = continuation.logical.committed;
            json!({"status":"waiting","advanced":advanced,"segment":segment,
                "continuation":continuation,"attempted":attempted,
                "committed":committed,"residual":residual})
        }
        Ok(StageOutcome::Complete {
            segment,
            committed,
            attempted,
            residual,
        }) => json!({"status":"complete","segment":segment,"attempted":attempted,
                "committed":committed,"residual":residual}),
        Err(rejected) => json!({"status":"rejected","error":rejected.error,
            "attempted":rejected.attempted,"committed":rejected.committed}),
    };
    let object = report
        .as_object_mut()
        .expect("stage reports are JSON objects");
    for (name, value) in STAGE_LABELS {
        object.insert(name.into(), Value::String(value.into()));
    }
    report
}

===== COMPLETE BODY current-source/crates/mori-core/src/stage/validate.rs SHA256 d301530692f2f02a51bb68cf8b30ee51db6d3f3c9019b9045ed2a50e3ad8debf =====
//! `/2` static validation and stage plan invariants.
//!
//! `validate_v2` applies every legacy `/1` program check with the legacy codes
//! and messages, plus the Await rules: top-level placement (structural in IR),
//! unique labels, non-shadowing binders, Bool guards typed over the prefix and
//! the Await's own binders, the per-action bounds, and `await`/`when` reserved.
//! Legacy `validate` is unchanged and remains `/1`-only.
use super::{
    AwaitSite, CaptureSlot, LANGUAGE_2, MAX_AWAITS_PER_ACTION, MAX_CAPTURE_SLOTS,
    MAX_EVENT_BINDERS, Origin, SEMANTICS_2, STAGE_PLAN_FORMAT, StageBinder, StagePlan,
};
use crate::runtime::{
    expr_type, identifier, require_uint, validate_asset, validate_liability_reads,
};
use crate::{
    Error, Expr, MAX_DECLARATIONS, MAX_EXPRESSIONS, MAX_STATEMENTS, Program, Statement, Type, UInt,
};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

fn failure(code: &str, message: impl Into<String>) -> Error {
    Error::new(code, message)
}

fn invariant(what: &str) -> Error {
    failure("program", format!("Stage plan invariant violated: {what}."))
}

/// Legacy identifier rule with `await` and `when` also reserved (C6).
fn identifier_v2(name: &str) -> bool {
    identifier(name) && !matches!(name, "await" | "when")
}

fn names_v2<'a>(values: impl Iterator<Item = &'a str>, kind: &str) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
    for name in values {
        if !identifier_v2(name) || !seen.insert(name) {
            return Err(failure(
                "duplicate",
                format!("Invalid or duplicate {kind} name {name:?}."),
            ));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Full finite validation of a `moriarty-core/2` program.
pub(crate) fn validate_v2(program: &Program) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if program.language != LANGUAGE_2 || program.semantics != SEMANTICS_2 {
        return Err(failure(
            "profile",
            "Unsupported language or semantics identity.",
        ));
    }
    if !identifier_v2(&program.name) {
        return Err(failure(
            "name",
            "Agreement name must be a non-reserved ASCII identifier of at most 64 bytes.",
        ));
    }
    if program.actions.is_empty() {
        return Err(failure("program", "Program needs at least one action."));
    }
    if program.states.len() + program.assets.len() + program.actions.len() > MAX_DECLARATIONS
        || program.expressions.len() > MAX_EXPRESSIONS
    {
        return Err(failure(
            "bound",
            "Program exceeds finite declaration/expression bounds.",
        ));
    }
    names_v2(
        program
            .states
            .iter()
            .map(|s| s.name.as_str())
            .chain(program.assets.iter().map(|a| a.name.as_str())),
        "declaration",
    )?;
    names_v2(program.actions.iter().map(|a| a.name.as_str()), "action")?;
    let mut identities = BTreeSet::new();
    for asset in &program.assets {
        let id = &asset.identity;
        if id.network.is_empty()
            || id.issuer.is_empty()
            || id.reference.is_empty()
            || id.network.len() > 1024
            || id.issuer.len() > 1024
            || id.reference.len() > 1024
            || id.scale > 38
            || !identities.insert(id)
        {
            return Err(failure(
                "asset",
                "Asset identities must be complete, unique and use scale 0..38.",
            ));
        }
        if program.assets.iter().any(|other| {
            other.identity.network == id.network
                && other.identity.issuer == id.issuer
                && other.identity.reference == id.reference
                && other.identity.scale != id.scale
        }) {
            return Err(failure(
                "asset",
                "Same asset identity cannot have conflicting scales.",
            ));
        }
    }
    let mut depths = Vec::with_capacity(program.expressions.len());
    for (i, e) in program.expressions.iter().enumerate() {
        let refs = match e {
            Expr::Binary { lhs, rhs, .. } => vec![*lhs, *rhs],
            Expr::Not { value } => vec![*value],
            _ => vec![],
        };
        let mut depth = 0;
        for r in refs {
            if usize::from(r) >= i {
                return Err(failure(
                    "expression",
                    "Expression references must point to earlier nodes; cycles are forbidden.",
                ));
            }
            depth = depth.max(depths[usize::from(r)] + 1);
        }
        if depth > 64 {
            return Err(failure("bound", "Expression depth exceeds 64."));
        }
        depths.push(depth);
    }
    let state_types: BTreeMap<_, _> = program
        .states
        .iter()
        .map(|s| (s.name.clone(), s.ty))
        .collect();
    for state in &program.states {
        if state.initial.ty() != state.ty {
            return Err(failure(
                "type",
                format!("Initial state {:?} has wrong type.", state.name),
            ));
        }
    }
    for action in &program.actions {
        if action.inputs.len() > MAX_DECLARATIONS || action.statements.len() > MAX_STATEMENTS {
            return Err(failure(
                "bound",
                "Action exceeds input or statement bounds.",
            ));
        }
        names_v2(action.inputs.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()), "input")?;
        let mut env = state_types.clone();
        for input in &action.inputs {
            if env.insert(input.name.clone(), input.ty).is_some()
                || program.assets.iter().any(|a| a.name == input.name)
            {
                return Err(failure("duplicate", "Input cannot shadow a declaration."));
            }
        }
        let mut labels = BTreeSet::new();
        let mut awaits = 0_usize;
        // Inputs, lets and earlier binders: the captures of the next site.
        let mut captured = action.inputs.len();
        for statement in &action.statements {
            match statement {
                Statement::Await {
                    label,
                    binders,
                    guard,
                } => {
                    awaits += 1;
                    if awaits > MAX_AWAITS_PER_ACTION {
                        return Err(failure("bound", "Action exceeds 16 awaits."));
                    }
                    if binders.len() > MAX_EVENT_BINDERS {
                        return Err(failure("bound", "Await exceeds 16 event binders."));
                    }
                    if captured > MAX_CAPTURE_SLOTS {
                        return Err(failure("bound", "Await site exceeds 256 capture slots."));
                    }
                    if !identifier_v2(label) || !labels.insert(label.as_str()) {
                        return Err(failure(
                            "duplicate",
                            format!("Invalid or duplicate await label {label:?}."),
                        ));
                    }
                    for binder in binders {
                        if !identifier_v2(&binder.name)
                            || env.insert(binder.name.clone(), binder.ty).is_some()
                            || program.assets.iter().any(|a| a.name == binder.name)
                        {
                            return Err(failure(
                                "duplicate",
                                "Await binder cannot shadow an existing name.",
                            ));
                        }
                    }
                    if expr_type(program, *guard, &env, 0)? != Type::Bool {
                        return Err(failure("type", "Await guard must have type Bool."));
                    }
                    captured += binders.len();
                }
                Statement::Let { name, value } => {
                    let ty = expr_type(program, *value, &env, 0)?;
                    if !identifier_v2(name)
                        || env.insert(name.clone(), ty).is_some()
                        || program.assets.iter().any(|a| a.name == *name)
                    {
                        return Err(failure("duplicate", "let cannot shadow an existing name."));
                    }
                    captured += 1;
                }
                Statement::Requires { predicate } | Statement::Ensures { predicate } => {
                    if expr_type(program, *predicate, &env, 0)? != Type::Bool {
                        return Err(failure("type", "Predicate must have type Bool."));
                    }
                }
                Statement::Next { state, value } => {
                    let expected = state_types
                        .get(state)
                        .ok_or_else(|| failure("name", format!("Unknown state {state:?}.")))?;
                    if expr_type(program, *value, &env, 0)? != *expected {
                        return Err(failure("type", "State update has wrong type."));
                    }
                }
                Statement::Emit { name, value } => {
                    if name.is_empty() || name.len() > 1024 {
                        return Err(failure("name", "Event name must contain 1..1024 bytes."));
                    }
                    expr_type(program, *value, &env, 0)?;
                }
                Statement::Transfer {
                    asset,
                    from,
                    to,
                    amount,
                }
                | Statement::Fee {
                    asset,
                    from,
                    to,
                    amount,
                } => {
                    validate_asset(program, asset)?;
                    if from.is_empty() || to.is_empty() || from.len() > 1024 || to.len() > 1024 {
                        return Err(failure("account", "Account identities cannot be empty."));
                    }
                    require_uint(program, *amount, &env)?;
                }
                Statement::Liability {
                    name,
                    asset,
                    debtor,
                    creditor,
                    amount,
                } => {
                    validate_asset(program, asset)?;
                    if name.is_empty()
                        || debtor.is_empty()
                        || creditor.is_empty()
                        || name.len() > 1024
                        || debtor.len() > 1024
                        || creditor.len() > 1024
                    {
                        return Err(failure(
                            "liability",
                            "Liability identities cannot be empty.",
                        ));
                    }
                    require_uint(program, *amount, &env)?;
                }
                Statement::Discharge { name, amount } => {
                    if name.is_empty() || name.len() > 1024 {
                        return Err(failure(
                            "liability",
                            "Liability name must contain 1..1024 bytes.",
                        ));
                    }
                    require_uint(program, *amount, &env)?;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    validate_liability_reads(program)?;
    Ok(())
}

/// Independently re-derives each action's sites and capture layouts and
/// requires the Sol-built plan to equal them, then checks the A3 predicates
/// explicitly. Runs before costing, so cost vectors and hash must be empty.
pub(crate) fn plan_invariants(program: &Program, plan: &StagePlan) -> Result<(), Error> {
    if plan.format != STAGE_PLAN_FORMAT {
        return Err(invariant("format"));
    }
    if !plan.program_hash.is_empty() {
        return Err(invariant("program hash filled before costing"));
    }
    if plan.actions.len() != program.actions.len() {
        return Err(invariant("action count"));
    }
    let state_names: BTreeSet<_> = program.states.iter().map(|s| s.name.as_str()).collect();
    for (action, ap) in program.actions.iter().zip(&plan.actions) {
        if ap.action != action.name || usize::from(ap.statement_count) != action.statements.len() {
            return Err(invariant("action identity"));
        }
        if !ap.segment_cost.is_empty()
            || !ap.committed_before.is_empty()
            || ap.plan_total != UInt(0)
        {
            return Err(invariant("costs filled before costing"));
        }
        // Independent derivation in declaration and statement order.
        let mut types: BTreeMap<_, _> = program
            .states
            .iter()
            .map(|s| (s.name.clone(), s.ty))
            .collect();
        let mut slots = Vec::with_capacity(action.inputs.len());
        for (index, input) in action.inputs.iter().enumerate() {
            types.insert(input.name.clone(), input.ty);
            slots.push(CaptureSlot {
                name: input.name.clone(),
                ty: input.ty,
                origin: Origin::Input,
                ordinal: u16::try_from(index).map_err(|_| invariant("input ordinal"))?,
            });
        }
        let mut expected = Vec::new();
        for (index, statement) in action.statements.iter().enumerate() {
            let ordinal = u16::try_from(index).map_err(|_| invariant("statement ordinal"))?;
            match statement {
                Statement::Let { name, value } => {
                    let ty = expr_type(program, *value, &types, 0)?;
                    types.insert(name.clone(), ty);
                    slots.push(CaptureSlot {
                        name: name.clone(),
                        ty,
                        origin: Origin::Let,
                        ordinal,
                    });
                }
                Statement::Await { label, binders, .. } => {
                    expected.push(AwaitSite {
                        site: u16::try_from(expected.len() + 1).map_err(|_| invariant("site"))?,
                        ordinal,
                        label: label.clone(),
                        binders: binders.iter().map(StageBinder::from).collect(),
                        captures: slots.clone(),
                    });
                    for binder in binders {
                        types.insert(binder.name.clone(), binder.ty);
                        slots.push(CaptureSlot {
                            name: binder.name.clone(),
                            ty: binder.ty,
                            origin: Origin::Event,
                            ordinal,
                        });
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        if ap.sites != expected {
            return Err(invariant("sites differ from source"));
        }
        // Explicit A3 predicates, stated separately from the derivation.
        if ap.sites.len() > MAX_AWAITS_PER_ACTION {
            return Err(failure("bound", "Action exceeds 16 awaits."));
        }
        let mut previous: Option<&AwaitSite> = None;
        for (index, site) in ap.sites.iter().enumerate() {
            if usize::from(site.site) != index + 1 {
                return Err(invariant("sites are not numbered 1..N"));
            }
            if previous.is_some_and(|p| p.ordinal >= site.ordinal) {
                return Err(invariant("ordinals are not strictly increasing"));
            }
            if site.binders.len() > MAX_EVENT_BINDERS || site.captures.len() > MAX_CAPTURE_SLOTS {
                return Err(failure("bound", "Await site exceeds stage layout bounds."));
            }
            let inputs = action.inputs.len();
            if site.captures.len() < inputs
                || site.captures[..inputs]
                    .iter()
                    .zip(&action.inputs)
                    .enumerate()
                    .any(|(i, (slot, input))| {
                        slot.origin != Origin::Input
                            || slot.name != input.name
                            || slot.ty != input.ty
                            || usize::from(slot.ordinal) != i
                    })
            {
                return Err(invariant("captures do not begin with all inputs"));
            }
            let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
            for slot in &site.captures {
                if state_names.contains(slot.name.as_str())
                    || !seen.insert(slot.name.as_str())
                    || (slot.origin != Origin::Input && slot.ordinal >= site.ordinal)
                {
                    return Err(invariant(
                        "capture names a state, repeats or follows its site",
                    ));
                }
            }
            if let Some(p) = previous
                && !site.captures.starts_with(&p.captures)
            {
                return Err(invariant("captures are not prefix-monotone"));
            }
            previous = Some(site);
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Checks the filled cost vectors: N+1 entries, prefix sums, and a total that
/// also equals the per-statement sum plus one entry charge per await.
pub(crate) fn cost_invariants(program: &Program, plan: &StagePlan) -> Result<(), Error> {
    let overflow = || failure("bound", "Stage logical plan exceeds u128.");
    for (action, ap) in program.actions.iter().zip(&plan.actions) {
        let segments = ap.sites.len() + 1;
        if ap.segment_cost.len() != segments || ap.committed_before.len() != segments {
            return Err(invariant("cost vector lengths"));
        }
        let mut running = 0_u128;
        for (cost, before) in ap.segment_cost.iter().zip(&ap.committed_before) {
            if before.0 != running {
                return Err(invariant("committed_before is not a prefix sum"));
            }
            running = running.checked_add(cost.0).ok_or_else(overflow)?;
        }
        if ap.plan_total.0 != running {
            return Err(invariant("plan total"));
        }
        let mut independent = u128::try_from(ap.sites.len()).map_err(|_| overflow())?;
        for statement in &action.statements {
            let cost = super::cost::statement_cost(program, statement)?;
            independent = independent.checked_add(cost.0).ok_or_else(overflow)?;
        }
        if independent != running {
            return Err(invariant("plan total differs from statement costs"));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

[package]
